Result: Qwen-VL在车辆损伤分类上达到87.3%准确率但空间定位系统性不可靠；提出的对比学习损失改善了微小损伤/背景可分性；Locally-grounded管道将报告幻觉率从92%（纯文本）和78%（纯图像）降至31%；在100份人工验证报告上进行了受控评估。

Conclusion: VLM的语义能力强但不适合直接承担空间定位任务，通过"分割模型负责定位、VLM负责语义"的混合架构可以显著降低报告幻觉率，损失函数选择对微小目标定位有决定性影响，focal loss在小目标场景下反而不利。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as reasoning agents in real-world visual assessment pipelines, yet their spatial grounding remains unreliable for fine-grained, visually ambiguous targets. We study this gap in the context of automated vehicle damage assessment, where fine-grained defects such as scratches and hairline cracks occupy few pixels, produce weak gradient signal, and are easily confused with reflections and surface texture. We show that a state-of-the-art VLM (Qwen-VL) achieves strong semantic classification accuracy (87.3%) on this task but is systematically ungrounded at the spatial level: it hallucinates damage in reflective regions, misses elongated scratches entirely, and produces spatially inconsistent outputs when prompted for localization. We propose TinyDamage, a hybrid architecture that delegates spatial grounding to a dedicated multi-task segmentation model while reserving the VLM for semantic reasoning and report generation. On the segmentation side, we find that the choice of loss function has an outsized and underexplored effect on tiny-object grounding: focal loss, widely used for class imbalance, collapses tiny-damage detection to zero, while a supervised contrastive objective measurably improves damage/background separability. We integrate the segmentation model into a 7-node LangGraph agent pipeline that grounds every VLM generation step in the segmentation output, and show that this grounding reduces the report hallucination rate from 92% (text-only) and 78% (image-only) to 31% in a controlled evaluation on 100 human-verified reports. We introduce DET_l, a permissive per-category detection metric for evaluating tiny-object grounding under class imbalance, and report latency and reliability characteristics of the deployed pipeline.

</details>


### [271] [Action-grounded tissue affordance enables anticipatory auto-framing that lowers surgeon cognitive workload during laparoscopic surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02471)
*Jiayu Gu,Yiwei Wang,Jie Zhang,Guojun Cao,Keshen Lyu,Song Zhou,Yimeng Chen,Haorui Wang,Qingmin Feng,Shenchao Shi,Huan Zhao,Wenbin Chen,Caihua Xiong,Chidan Wan,Jing Samantha Pan,Xiong Cai,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffeoAfford框架，通过微分同胚约束的组织追踪和器械轨迹分析，从已完成的手术中回顾性地生成组织可供性热图标签，无需人工逐帧标注，用于训练实时预测模型并实现腹腔镜手术的辅助自动取景系统AffordView。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算注意力模型有助于外科医生管理腹腔镜手术中的视觉需求，但需要密集的空间标签，而这些标签难以获取，因为手术意图高度专业化且属于隐性知识。

Method: DiffeoAfford结合微分同胚约束的组织追踪与器械轨迹分析，从完成的手术流程中回顾性地生成可供性热点标签，无需逐帧人工标注；随后用这些标签训练实时预测模型，驱动AffordView辅助自动取景系统。

Result: 该框架与专家标注及术中外科医生注视方向一致；在真实场景评估中，通过主观、生理和行为指标证明其能降低外科医生的认知工作负荷。

Conclusion: DiffeoAfford为腹腔镜手术的视觉注意力建模提供了无需手动标注的可扩展方案，且开发的辅助系统在实际应用中能有效减轻外科医生认知负担。

Abstract: Computational attention models could help surgeons manage the visual demands of laparoscopy, but they require dense spatial labels that are difficult to obtain because surgical intent is highly specialized and tacit. Here, we introduce DiffeoAfford, an action-grounded tissue affordance framework that retrospectively derives visual attention supervision from completed surgical procedures. By combining diffeomorphism-constrained tissue tracking with instrument trajectory analysis, DiffeoAfford generates affordance hotspot labels without manual per-frame annotation. A real-time prediction model trained on these labels anticipates relevant surgical regions and enables AffordView, an assistive auto-framing system for laparoscopic visualization. The proposed framework aligns with expert annotations and intraoperative surgeon gaze, and reduces surgeon cognitive workload during real-world evaluations using subjective, physiological, and behavioral measures.

</details>


### [272] [EchoCache: Energy-Guided Cross-Modal Caching for Efficient Audio-Driven Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02474)
*Jiayu Chen,Xiaoyu Wu,Rongshan Gao,Maoliang Li,Zihao Zheng,Xinhao Sun,Hailong Zou,Guojie Luo,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoCache，一个基于音频时频能量的跨模态缓存框架，通过音频能量作为显著性锚点引导潜在特征缓存更新，并引入动态时间步长-潜在特征缓存机制，在不损失生成质量的前提下显著加速音频驱动视频生成（A2V）的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动视频生成虽已取得进展，但扩散模型的迭代去噪过程使其推理成本高昂。现有缓存方法仅利用视觉特征的时间冗余，忽视了A2V中音频驱动视觉生成的跨模态对齐特性（即音频对视觉生成具有高度非均匀的时间重要性），导致存在时序语义错配和计算存储错配两个层面的问题。

Method: EchoCache包含两个核心组件：1）利用音频时频能量作为显著性锚点，引导潜在特征层的缓存更新策略；2）引入动态时间步长-潜在特征缓存机制，结合量化缓存管理，在效率与内存使用上实现联合优化。

Result: 在主流A2V模型上的大量实验表明，EchoCache一致性地改善了延迟-质量权衡，同时保持生成质量与音视频一致性。在Wan2.2-S2V模型上，EchoCache在EMTD基准上实现了2.46倍加速，并取得最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: EchoCache通过能量引导的跨模态缓存框架有效解决了A2V缓存中的时序语义错配与计算存储错配问题，为高效音频驱动视频生成提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Audio-driven video generation (A2V) has achieved promising progress in synthesizing temporally coherent and audio-visually aligned videos, yet its inference remains expensive due to the iterative denoising process of diffusion models. Existing caching methods mainly exploit temporal redundancy in visual features while overlooking the cross-modal alignment of A2V, where audio drives visual generation with highly non-uniform temporal importance. In this paper, we identify two levels of misalignment in existing A2V caching methods: temporal-semantic and computation-storage misalignment. To address them, we propose EchoCache, an energy-guided cross-modal caching framework for efficient A2V generation. EchoCache leverages audio time-frequency energy as a saliency anchor to guide latent-level cache updates and further introduces a dynamic timestep-latent caching mechanism with quantized cache management for joint efficiency and memory optimization. Extensive experiments on mainstream A2V models show that EchoCache consistently improves the latency-quality trade-off while preserving generation quality and audio-visual consistency. In particular, on Wan2.2-S2V over the EMTD benchmark, EchoCache achieves a 2.46x speedup with the best overall performance. Code is available at https://github.com/IF-LAB-PKU/EchoCache.

</details>


### [273] [Fermat Active Laplace Learning for Semi-Supervised Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02483)
*Vutichart Buranasiri,James M. Murphy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于密度感知Fermat距离和泊松加权调和标签传播的高光谱图像主动学习算法FALL和A-FALL。FALL使用完整数据点间的Fermat距离构建亲和矩阵，A-FALL则通过地标点采样和多维缩放实现近似计算以提高大规模场景的可扩展性。实验表明FALL在Salinas A和Pavia数据集上有效，A-FALL可扩展到大规模高光谱场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像分类中，标注样本获取成本高昂，需要高效的主动学习算法来选择最有信息量的样本进行标注，同时需要充分利用数据的流形结构提升分类精度。

Method: 提出FALL和A-FALL两种算法：(1) FALL构建全数据点间的Fermat距离亲和矩阵，结合最小范数采集函数和PWLL实现主动查询；(2) A-FALL通过最远点采样选取地标点，用地标多维缩放构建亲和矩阵，并通过留一交叉验证选择Fermat指数p。

Result: 在Salinas A和Pavia数据集上的实验验证了FALL的有效性以及A-FALL在大规模高光谱场景中的可扩展性。

Conclusion: Fermat距离与调和标签传播的结合能够提供密度感知的数据流形估计，从而提升高光谱图像的标注精度；A-FALL通过近似计算在保持性能的同时解决了大规模场景下的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Two active learning algorithms for hyperspectral image (HSI) classification are proposed that combine density-aware Fermat distances with Poisson-reweighted harmonic label propagation. Our methods actively query points using an uncertainty-based acquisition function, extending Poisson ReWeighted Laplace Learning (PWLL). Our first algorithm, Fermat Active Laplace Learning (FALL), builds an affinity matrix using Fermat distances between all data points. Then, PWLL is run with a diagonal perturbation using the minimum-norm acquisition function. In contrast, Approximate FALL (A-FALL) computes Fermat distances between each data point and landmark pixels selected via farthest-point sampling and constructs the affinity matrix using landmark multidimensional scaling. After several query rounds, A-FALL selects the Fermat exponent $p$ using a leave-one-out cross-validation variant. FALL and A-FALL leverage Fermat distances and subsequent harmonic label propagation to provide a density-aware estimation of the data manifold, improving labeling accuracy. Experiments on Salinas A and Pavia show the effectiveness of FALL and the scalability of A-FALL to large HSI scenes.

</details>


### [274] [DyFrDet: Towards Accurate Small Object Detection via Dynamic Frequency Suppression with Label Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02495)
*Zihan Yang,Yang Guo,Hongxing Zhang,Dan Lu,Siyuan Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DyFrDet，一种通过动态抑制频域背景干扰来解决小目标检测中低视觉线索、频域噪声和标签模糊问题的新型检测器，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小目标检测因视觉线索不足而困难，以往方法多聚焦于构建判别性表示，但忽略了频域噪声和标签模糊对定位精度的严重影响。

Method: 提出DyFrDet，包含两个核心模块：(1) 动态频域感知特征金字塔网络（DyFrFPN），通过动态频带预测器（DBP）在频域中自适应抑制低频冗余和高频噪声；(2) 标签消歧模块（LDM），利用概率分布显式建模并缓解目标标签的固有模糊性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能，验证了方法在挑战性场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DyFrDet通过频域动态去噪和标签消歧，有效提升了小目标检测的定位精度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable progress over the past decades, accurately identifying small objects remains challenging because of their insufficient visual cues. Previous works typically attempt to construct discriminative representation of the small objects. However, the wide range frequency domain noises and label ambiguities have been greatly overlooked, which significantly hinders the accurate localization. To address these issues, we propose a novel small object detection (SOD) detector termed DyFrDet, which is able to precisely localize the small object by dynamically suppressing the background distractions in frequency domain. Specifically, we propose a Dynamic Frequency-aware Feature Pyramid Network (DyFrFPN) to adaptively suppress low-frequency redundancy and excessive high-frequency noises. The DyFrFPN transforms the hierarchical features into frequency domain representation, and introduces a Dynamic Band Predictor (DBP) to preserve the discriminative components for small object identification. Afterwards, we present a novel Label Disambiguation Module (LDM), which leverages probabilistic distributions to explicitly model and alleviate the inherent ambiguity of target labels, yielding efficient improvement in localization precision of the small objects with low-resolution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DyFrDet achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, indicating its effectiveness and robustness in various challenging scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/ManOfStory/DyFrDet.

</details>


### [275] [Token Radius Attention for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02504)
*Jiayu Chen,Zhikun Jiang,Maoliang Li,Jiayi Luo,Jiawei Yang,Zihao Zheng,Hengyi Zhang,Guojie Luo,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Token Radius Attention (TRA)，一种无需训练的框架，通过查询熵映射到解析式token预算并转化为时间衰减半径，在仅保留9-19%注意力交互的情况下实现1.56x-2.05x加速，同时保持竞争力生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer（VDiTs）的密集3D自注意力产生二次方计算成本，现有头部级和块级稀疏方法在查询之间共享计算预算，忽略了token特定的注意力需求。

Method: 提出TRA框架：1) 根据观察——保留密度随查询变化但与注意力熵呈对数线性相关，且主导交互形成以查询为中心的、半径随token变化的邻域；2) 将查询熵映射为解析式token预算，并转换为时间衰减半径，无需显式键排序；3) 融合熵提取、预热重用和块稀疏掩码构建以减少额外开销。

Result: 在Wan2.1、Wan2.2和HunyuanVideo的7种T2V/I2V配置上，TRA仅保留9-19%的注意力交互，实现1.56x-2.05x速度提升，同时生成质量具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 注意力熵可作为token级稀疏预算的有效预测指标，基于半径的token特定稀疏策略能在不牺牲质量的前提下显著降低VDiTs的计算成本，为视频生成大模型的推理加速提供了新的无需训练方案。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers (VDiTs) enable high-fidelity generation but incur quadratic cost from dense 3D self-attention. Existing head- and block-level sparse methods share computation budgets across queries, overlooking token-specific attention demand. We observe that retained density varies across queries yet correlates log-linearly with attention entropy, while dominant interactions form query-centered neighborhoods with token-dependent radii. Based on these findings, we propose Token Radius Attention (TRA), a training-free framework that maps query entropy to an analytic token budget and converts it into a temporally decayed radius without explicit key ranking. Fused entropy extraction, warm-up reuse, and block-sparse mask construction further reduce overhead. Across seven Wan2.1, Wan2.2, and HunyuanVideo T2V/I2V configurations, TRA retains only 9-19% of attention interactions and achieves 1.56x-2.05x speedup with competitive generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/IF-LAB-PKU/Token-Radius-Attention.

</details>


### [276] [ReMiX-MAE: Learning Missing-Channel Cross-Modal Representations from RGB-Only Clinical Facial Videos for Sympathetic-Mediated Pain Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02561)
*Nan Bi,Taoyue Wang,Lijun Yin,Vandana Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReMiX-MAE，一种自监督多模态掩码预训练框架，利用同步的RGB、热成像和深度视频学习面部疼痛表征，并显式训练对缺失模态的鲁棒性，实现仅用RGB即可部署。同时收集了具有纵向治疗轨迹的SMP疼痛数据集，实验证明其在RGB-only部署下优于纯RGB基线，且在外部数据集上表现出更强的鲁棒性和标签效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床环境中的自动疼痛评估面临两大挑战：一是缺乏临床场景下带弱标签（序列级自报告）的面部视频数据；二是疼痛线索在RGB中可能微妙或近中性，而热成像和深度信号虽有信息量却难以在常规临床中部署。因此需要一种既能利用多模态信息又能在RGB-only条件下工作的解决方案。

Method: 提出ReMiX-MAE（Reconstructing Missing Channel Cross-Modal Masked Autoencoder），一种自监督多模态掩码预训练框架。它从同步的RGB、热成像和深度视频中学习可迁移的面部表征，并显式训练对缺失模态的鲁棒性，使模型能够仅依赖RGB进行部署。同时收集了SMP（Sympathetic Mediated Pain）数据集，包含多次就诊的配对前后视频记录以及视频级自报告标签和纵向治疗轨迹。

Result: 在RGB-only部署下，ReMiX-MAE在SMP数据集上持续优于纯RGB的掩码自动编码器基线。使用从RGB解码的伪多模态特征在五分类挑战性设定中带来额外增益。在外部数据集上，ReMiX-MAE相比RGB-only基线表现出更强的鲁棒性和标签效率迁移能力，在数据有限的临床场景中有明显优势。

Conclusion: ReMiX-MAE通过多模态掩码预训练结合缺失模态鲁棒性训练，有效解决了临床疼痛评估中多模态数据丰富但实际部署受限的矛盾，为数据稀缺的临床环境提供了一种标签高效、鲁棒的自动疼痛评估方案。

Abstract: Automated pain assessment in real clinics is limited by scarce clinically grounded facial video data with weak labels (often sequence-level self-report) and by the fact that pain cues can be subtle or near-neutral in RGB, while thermal and depth signals are informative yet impractical to deploy routinely. To address these challenges, we propose ReMiX-MAE (Reconstructing Missing Channel Cross-Modal Masked Autoencoder), a self-supervised multimodal masked pretraining framework that learns transferable facial representations from synchronized RGB, thermal, and depth videos and explicitly trains robustness to missing modalities, enabling RGB-only deployment. To fill the gap of clinically grounded facial pain data with video-level self-report and longitudinal treatment trajectories, we collect the Sympathetic Mediated Pain (SMP) dataset with paired pre- and post-recordings across multiple visits. Under RGB-only deployment, we evaluate ReMiX-MAE using both direct feature extraction and pseudo-multimodal features decoded from RGB. ReMiX-MAE consistently outperforms an RGB-only masked autoencoder baseline on SMP, with pseudo-multimodal features providing additional gains in the challenging five-class setting. Across external datasets, ReMiX-MAE further shows more robust and label-efficient transfer than RGB-only baselines, highlighting its advantage in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [277] [UEmbed: Unified Sparse and Dense Multimodal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02583)
*Tingyu Song,Mingxin Li,Yanzhao Zhang,Dingkun Long,Pengjun Xie,Zhijie Nie,Yilun Zhao,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UEmbed（Unified Embedding），一种仅解码器的多模态嵌入模型，通过在单次因果前向传播中同时生成稀疏词级表示和稠密表示，统一了稀疏检索与稠密检索，并扩展了稀疏检索在多模态场景下的应用。模型在2B/4B/9B三个规模上发布，UEmbed-9B在MMEB-v2上达到71.8（稠密）和71.0（稀疏）的成绩，在公开数据训练的多模态嵌入模型中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型稀疏检索（LSR）仍然局限于编码器式双向架构，且扩展到多模态场景时严重依赖辅助的跨模态模块。这些限制阻碍了稀疏检索在更广泛场景中的应用，需要一种更统一、更简洁的架构来同时支持稠密和稀疏表示，并自然支持多模态输入。

Method: UEmbed采用仅解码器架构，通过向输入追加N个可学习特殊标记，并将词表划分为N个不相交子集。每个特殊标记的因果隐藏状态预测其对应词子集上的稀疏权重，N个子集的输出拼接形成完整的稀疏向量。该模型在单次因果前向传播中同时产生稀疏词级表示和稠密表示。

Result: UEmbed在2B、4B和9B三个规模上发布，UEmbed-9B在MMEB-v2上达到71.8（稠密）和71.0（稀疏），优于在公开数据上训练的多模态嵌入模型（如RzenEmbed）。在BEIR基准上，UEmbed与强稠密和稀疏基线保持竞争力。论文还在有效性、效率和智能体应用三个维度验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: UEmbed提出了一种新范式：在单一模型中统一稠密和稀疏嵌入，同时将稀疏检索扩展到统一文本和多模态输入，为多模态检索领域提供了更高效、更简洁的解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse retrieval underpins modern search systems, from web search to retrieval-augmented generation. Existing work has introduced Learned Sparse Retrieval (LSR) to push beyond exact lexical matching toward richer semantics. Yet LSR has so far remained tied to encoder-style bidirectional architectures, and its extension to multimodal settings still relies heavily on auxiliary cross-modal modules. To address these limitations, we introduce UEmbed (Unified Embedding), a decoder-only multimodal embedding model that produces both sparse lexical and dense representations in one causal forward pass. UEmbed appends N learnable special tokens to the input and partitions the vocabulary into N disjoint subsets. Each token's causal hidden state predicts sparse weights over its assigned subset, and the N subsets are concatenated into the full sparse vector. Trained on public data, we release UEmbed at 2B, 4B, and 9B scales. UEmbed-9B reaches 71.8 (dense) and 71.0 (sparse) on MMEB-v2, outperforming multimodal embedding models trained on publicly available data (e.g., RzenEmbed). On BEIR, UEmbed also remains competitive with strong dense and sparse baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate the practical utility of UEmbed across three dimensions: effectiveness, efficiency, and agentic applications. Overall, UEmbed offers a new paradigm: it unifies dense and sparse embeddings in one model, while further extending sparse retrieval to unify text and multimodal inputs.

</details>


### [278] [CAPEval: A Decoupled Caption Evaluation across Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02589)
*Zhipeng Liu,Haochen Wang,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAPEval基准，将字幕质量解耦为覆盖度（Coverage）和精确度（Precision）两个维度，并通过下游实验发现覆盖度与理解任务性能高度相关，精确度与生成任务性能高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字幕质量评估将质量视为单一标量目标，混淆了"字幕覆盖多少视觉信息"和"图像在多大程度上支持其陈述"这两个截然不同的属性。

Method: 设计CAPEval（Coverage And Precision Evaluation）基准，包含人工撰写的真实字幕和人工验证的原子检查清单项。将字幕质量分解为覆盖度（对真实事实内容的覆盖程度）和精确度（字幕中所有陈述的事实正确率）。选取10个字幕生成器，在四个模型家族中进行受控下游端到端实验，仅以字幕来源为变量。

Result: 发现一致的任务依赖解离现象：覆盖度是理解性能的更强相关指标，而精确度是生成性能的主导预测因子。

Conclusion: 解耦评估范式不仅提供了更细粒度的字幕质量诊断，还为不同下游任务选择和优化字幕生成器提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Captions serve as a primary supervision signal for both multimodal understanding and text-to-image generation. However, previous evaluations treat the caption quality as a single scalar objective, which conflates two distinct properties: (1) how much visual information a caption covers and (2) how reliably the image supports its stated claims. To this end, we design a decoupled caption evaluation benchmark, CAPEval (Coverage And Precision Evaluation), with human-written ground-truth captions and human-verified atomic checklist items. Specifically, CAPEval decomposes caption quality into Coverage and Precision. The former quantifies how thoroughly a caption covers ground-truth factual content, while the latter reflects the factual correctness rate of all claims expressed in the caption. We select 10 captioners and further conduct controlled downstream end-to-end experiments with them from four model families, where the caption source is the only variable. Empirically, we find a consistent task-dependent dissociation: Coverage serves as the stronger correlate for understanding performance, whereas Precision acts as the dominant predictor for generation performance. This decoupled evaluation paradigm not only delivers a more fine-grained diagnosis of caption quality, but also offers actionable guidance for selecting and optimizing captioners tailored to different downstream tasks.

</details>


### [279] [VR3D: View-Robust 3D Representation Learning for Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02598)
*Chao Ji,Shiyu Xuan,Zechao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VR3D框架，通过将2D图像映射到统一的3D坐标空间来实现视角无关的特征交互，解决跨平台视角变化导致的遮挡和几何形变问题。在三个基准数据集上均取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空中-地面行人重识别方法仅在2D图像空间学习视角不变表示，但剧烈的视角变化导致学习到的特征仍然与视角偏差耦合，难以有效解决跨平台视角变化带来的遮挡和几何形变问题。

Method: VR3D框架包含两个核心模块：(1) View-Robust 3D Representation Interaction (VR3I)，利用从单张2D观测中提取的3D先验，将2D外观特征提升到规范3D空间，并通过3D Geometry-Semantic Attention建立2D补丁与3D体素之间的交互；(2) Reliability-Aware Fusion，估计样本特定的可靠性并自适应聚合多源表示。

Result: 在CARGO、AG-ReID.v1和AG-ReID.v2三个基准数据集上均优于现有方法，其中在CARGO上Rank-1指标提升了5.63%。

Conclusion: 将行人重识别从2D空间提升到3D规范空间进行表示学习，并结合可靠性感知融合机制，能够有效缓解跨平台视角变化带来的挑战，为空中-地面行人重识别提供了新思路。

Abstract: Aerial-ground person re-identification is a challenging task due to cross-platform viewpoint variations, which cause severe occlusion and geometric deformation. Existing methods attempt to learn view-invariant representations exclusively within the 2D image space, where drastic viewpoint variations cause the learned features to remain coupled with viewpoint bias. To address this, we propose VR3D, a View-Robust 3D Representation Learning framework that maps images into a unified 3D coordinate space to achieve view-independent feature interaction. Specifically, we introduce View-Robust 3D Representation Interaction, which leverages 3D priors extracted from single 2D observations to lift 2D appearance features into a canonical 3D space. VR3I employs 3D Geometry-Semantic Attention to establish interactions between 2D patches and 3D voxels from corresponding body parts based on their 3D spatial locations, effectively grounding 2D semantics within a 3D framework. In addition, as the reliability of these representations varies across samples due to viewpoint changes and 3D reconstruction errors, we introduce Reliability-Aware Fusion, which estimates sample-specific reliability and adaptively aggregates the multi-source representations. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets (CARGO, AG-ReID.v1, and AG-ReID.v2) demonstrate that VR3D outperforms recent methods. For example, it achieves a 5.63% improvement in Rank-1 on CARGO. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [280] [WorldExam: Benchmarking World Models from Apparent Appearance to Inherent Reactivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02603)
*Yuxue Yang,Shuyao Shang,Jiahe Wang,Zitong Zhou,Liang Tan,Junhan Zeng,Ruizhi Li,Junyan Li,Yu Liu,Xiao Yang,Yong Li,Jun Zhu,Hongsheng Li,Tieniu Tan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldExam，一个分层诊断基准，用于评估视频生成模型作为世界模型时的内在反应性（inherent reactivity），涵盖视觉质量、控制遵循、空间一致性和世界反应性四个层级，共1474个案例、8个任务，评估了20个代表性模型，发现不同驱动范式的模型能力存在明显分化，且视觉质量高和指令遵循好并不保证内在反应性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可控视频生成模型正日益被用作世界模型，但现有基准主要评估视觉质量或显式指令遵循，缺乏对世界模型核心能力——内在反应性（即从场景状态推断世界应如何反应，并生成输入中未明确描述的合理后果）的评估。

Method: 提出了WorldExam分层诊断基准，包含四个层级：视觉质量（Visual Quality）、控制遵循（Control Adherence）、空间一致性（Spatial Consistency）和世界反应性（World Reactivity）。由1474个案例、8个专门任务组成，支持对相机驱动、动作驱动和语言驱动三种模型范式的统一评估。世界反应性层级专门评估场景条件反应和目标导向行为，这些行为超出输入中显式指定的内容。

Result: 对20个代表性模型的评估揭示了明显的能力分化：相机驱动模型擅长相机控制但接口不支持动态交互；动作驱动模型对主体控制更精确但往往使世界缺乏响应；语言驱动模型在交互方面表现更好但遵循复杂控制不够忠实。没有模型能同时具备广泛任务覆盖和一致强劲的性能。

Conclusion: 高视觉质量和显式指令遵循并不能保证内在反应性，这一发现凸显了现有评估范式与视频生成模型作为世界模型角色之间的差距，为未来世界模型评估和开发提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Controllable video generation models are increasingly being developed as world models. Accordingly, evaluating them in this role extends beyond the apparent appearance of generated videos to the inherent reactivity of the worlds they depict: the ability to infer from the scene state how the world should react and to generate plausible consequences not explicitly described in the input. Yet existing benchmarks mainly assess visual quality or explicit instruction fulfillment by checking whether requested actions and interaction outcomes are realized, leaving inherent reactivity underexamined. We introduce WorldExam, a hierarchical diagnostic benchmark spanning four levels: Visual Quality, Control Adherence, Spatial Consistency, and World Reactivity. It comprises 1,474 cases across eight dedicated tasks and supports unified evaluation of camera-, action-, and language-driven model paradigms. The World Reactivity level evaluates scene-conditioned reactions and goal-directed behaviors beyond what is explicitly specified in the input. Evaluation of 20 representative models reveals a clear capability split. Camera-driven models excel at camera control, but their interfaces do not support dynamic interaction; action-driven models control subjects more precisely but often leave the world unresponsive; and language-driven models perform better on interaction but follow complex controls less faithfully. No model combines broad task coverage with consistently strong performance, showing that high visual quality and explicit instruction fulfillment do not guarantee inherent reactivity.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [Track-Guided Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Vehicle Drifting with Minimum-Lap-Time Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00113)
*Sheng Zhao,Bolin Zhao,Xiaodong Wu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向最小圈时漂移场景的规划-控制框架，通过最优控制问题生成MLT漂移规划轨迹，并利用该轨迹作为先验数据训练深度强化学习漂移控制器。提出Track-guided Reinforcement Learning (TgRL)方法，采用渐进式训练策略（从漂移控制策略→漂移弯道策略→完整漂移比赛策略），奖励函数结合即时奖励和基于最小圈时目标设计的终端奖励。仿真结果表明该框架能学习到既保证车辆运动控制性能又有效减少圈时的漂移比赛策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拉力赛中，车手通过主动突破轮胎抓地力极限进行漂移来快速对齐车辆姿态以缩短圈时，但自主执行该操作需要同时解决两个复杂目标：稳定高度非线性的漂移动力学，以及严格最小化圈时，这推动了先进最小圈时漂移控制架构的发展。

Method: 首先构建最优控制问题生成最小圈时漂移规划轨迹作为先验数据，然后提出TgRL（Track-guided Reinforcement Learning）方法，通过渐进式分步训练（从漂移控制策略→漂移弯道策略→综合漂移比赛策略）解决大侧偏角导致的直接学习困难问题。奖励函数由即时奖励和基于MLT目标的终端奖励组成。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的框架能够使智能体学习到漂移比赛策略，不仅能确保车辆运动控制性能，还能有效减少圈时。

Conclusion: 该规划-控制框架结合最优控制和深度强化学习，通过渐进式训练策略成功解决了MLT漂移这一复杂双目标控制问题，验证了漂移作为拉力赛最优竞赛策略的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: In Formula 1, drivers optimize racing lines within tire grip limits to minimize lap times; however, in rally racing, drivers intentionally break traction to drift on loose surfaces. This maneuver rapidly aligns the vehicle for corner exits, ultimately reducing lap time. Autonomously executing such maneuvers formulates a complex dual-objective control problem: stabilizing highly nonlinear drift dynamics while strictly minimizing lap time. Addressing this challenge motivates the development of advanced Minimum-Lap-Time (MLT) drift control architectures. This paper proposes a planning-control framework specifically designed for MLT drifting scenario. First, we formulate an optimal control problem to generate a MLT drift planning trajectory, which is used as prior data to train a deep reinforcement learning drift controller. Given that drifting involves extremely large sideslip angles and is therefore challenging to learn directly, a Track-guided Reinforcement Learning (TgRL) drift control method is proposed to enable progressive training in a step-by-step manner, from drift control policy, to drift corner policy, and finally to a comprehensive drift race policy. The reward function incorporates both an instant reward term and an end reward term derived from the Minimum-Lap-Time objective. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework enables the agent to learn a drift racing policy that not only ensures vehicle motion control performance but also effectively reduces lap time.

</details>


### [282] [Motion Planning for Mobile Manipulators Navigating Doorways via Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00206)
*Kasra Sinaei,Kasun Weerakoon,Christopher Bradley,Seyed Abolfazl Fakoorian,Donald Ebeigbe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非线性模型预测控制（MPC）的移动机械臂门禁穿越运动规划框架，通过将机器人与门建模为耦合动力学系统，在优化框架内生成动态可行且无碰撞的推/拉门开启与穿越轨迹，并通过仿真和硬件实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿越门禁是移动机械臂在人类环境中运行的基本能力，需要在移动基座与机械臂之间实现协调运动，但现有方法在处理推门和拉门两种场景时缺乏统一的、动态可行的轨迹规划方案。

Method: 将机器人与门构建为耦合动力学系统，并嵌入非线性模型预测控制（MPC）优化框架中求解。通过基于惩罚项的约束来保证操作可行性，避免了在规划器中显式建模机械臂运动学。

Result: 仿真实验和硬件实验均表明，所提出的方法能够成功规划出门禁穿越的可行轨迹，同时适用于推门和拉门两种场景。

Conclusion: 该MPC-based运动规划框架为移动机械臂的门禁自主穿越提供了一种统一且有效的解决方案，无需复杂的手臂运动学建模即可生成动态可行的无碰撞轨迹。

Abstract: Navigating doorways is a fundamental capability for mobile manipulators operating in human environments, requiring coordinated motion between the mobile base and manipulator arm. This paper presents a motion planning framework that generates dynamically feasible and collision-free trajectories for autonomously opening and traversing both push and pull doors. The proposed method formulates the robot and door as a coupled dynamical system within a nonlinear Model Predictive Control (MPC) optimization framework. Manipulation feasibility is enforced through a penalty-based constraint, avoiding explicit arm kinematic modeling in the planner. Simulations and a hardware experiment demonstrate that the approach successfully plans feasible trajectories for door traversal.

</details>


### [283] [Developing Combined Manipulation and Locomotion Skills with Interaction Representation and Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00208)
*Fanxing Meng,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于发展心理学原理的人形机器人运动策略学习方法，包括全身抓取策略的学习，以及将其与站立行走策略组合成更复杂的操作与移动策略，实验表明零样本抓取成功率达93%，站立抓物成功率达96-100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人运动策略学习往往依赖外部数据集或预训练模型，且难以将不同策略有效组合。作者希望通过发展心理学原理使机器人自主、泛化地学习运动策略，并将策略组合成更复杂的行为。

Method: (1) 借鉴调和分析理论，采用三次谐函数作为权重，通过空间卷积表征手-物空间关系；基于发展原理设计帧内手指关节解耦课程，使机器人无需外部数据即可自主学习通用抓取策略。(2) 通过分别向两个策略提供各自的观测向量，并利用手-物交互分数决定各策略在何时控制哪些机器人关节，从而实现抓取策略与站起策略的组合。

Result: 抓取未见物体的零样本成功率为93%，持有物体时站立成功率为96-100%。研究还表明，只有当所有策略在同一完整人形身体上学习时，策略组合才有效，即使某些策略（如运动）看似不需要所有身体部位（如手指）。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了基于发展原理的策略学习方法能够实现跨任务的策略组合，并揭示了同体学习对策略组合有效性的关键作用，为人形机器人复杂行为的学习与组合提供了新的思路。

Abstract: This paper addresses how to enable a humanoid robot to learn motion policies based on developmental principles and combine policies to create more sophisticated and useful behaviors. Specifically, we present an approach to (1) learning a whole-body reaching and grasping policy and (2) combining it and a standing-up and walking policy to compose a more complex policy of manipulation and locomotion: grasping, standing up, and walking.
  In (1), our method draws inspiration from harmonic analysis and adopts cubic harmonics as weights to represent the hand-object spatial relationship via spatial convolution. Utilizing an intra-episode finger joint decoupling curriculum based on developmental principles, a robot can autonomously learn a generalizable grasping policy without relying on external datasets or pretrained models.
  In (2), our method combines the grasping policy with a separately learned getting-up policy by providing both policies with their respective observation vectors and using hand-object interaction scores to determine when each policy should control which robot joints. Our results show a 93% zero-shot success rate for grasping unseen objects and a 96-100% success rate for standing up while holding the object. Our work also demonstrates that combining different policies is only effective if each policy learning happens on the same whole humanoid body even if a policy (such as for locomotion) does not seem to need all the body parts (such as fingers).

</details>


### [284] [Compliant Sphere Lattice Contact: Distributed Contact Modeling for Sphere-Based Robot Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00263)
*Nataliya Nechyporenko,Ava Abderezaei,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CSLC（Compliant Sphere Lattice Contact），一种在球体机器人表示上原生运行的分布式接触模型。通过将机器人界面建模为通过锚定弹簧和横向弹簧连接的可变形球体点阵，在受压时产生空间分布的接触斑块，从而在保持球体模型计算效率的同时提升物理精度，并在两个独立求解器上验证了接触斑块形成和抓取稳定性改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人接触规划需要在计算效率和物理精度之间取得平衡。球体表示具有快速碰撞检测和可微几何的优势，但依赖点接触模型，无法捕捉接触斑块面积、压力分布、旋转刚度和摩擦力矩等物理特性，导致物理精度不足。

Method: 将机器人接触界面建模为一种由表面球体构成的柔顺点阵结构，球体之间通过锚定弹簧和横向弹簧相互连接。当点阵与物体接触受压时发生变形，产生空间分布的接触斑块，从而在球体表示上实现分布式的接触建模。

Result: 在两个独立求解器上验证了所提出的CSLC模型，初步结果表明该方法能够形成接触斑块并改善抓取稳定性。

Conclusion: CSLC通过在球体表示上引入柔顺点阵接触模型，有效弥合了基于球体的接触模型在计算效率与物理精度之间的差距，为接触规划和抓取任务提供了更准确的建模方案。

Abstract: Contact planning in robotics requires models that are both computationally efficient and physically accurate. Sphere-based robot representations satisfy the first requirement by enabling fast collision checking and differentiable geometry, but sacrifice physical accuracy by relying on point contact which cannot capture contact patch area, pressure distributions, rotational stiffness, or frictional moments. We introduce Compliant Sphere Lattice Contact (CSLC), a distributed contact model that operates natively on sphere representations by modeling the robot interface as a compliant lattice of surface spheres connected through anchor and lateral springs. When pressed against an object, the lattice deforms to produce a spatially distributed contact patch that improves the physical accuracy of sphere-based contact. We validate CSLC across two independent solvers and show preliminary results demonstrating contact patch formation and improved grasp stability.

</details>


### [285] [Localization in Spatiotemporal Fields via Environmental PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00272)
*Jose Fuentes,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Ana Cavalcanti,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用偏微分方程（PDE）控制时空场作为定位签名的框架，并采用Rao-Blackwellized粒子滤波（RBPF）融合多个环境场通道，在仿真和实地实验中均优于标准粒子滤波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统定位方法在无GPS或信号受限环境中（如近岸、河流环境）面临挑战，而环境中存在的自然物理场（如水流、温度、盐度等）具有空间变异性，可作为被动定位的天然信号源。

Method: 使用两类PDE（浅水方程和对流扩散方程）生成预测环境场；通过数值PDE求解器提供全域预测；将多个场通道作为多模态测量融合；采用RBPF将车辆状态分解为非线性部分（粒子采样）和线性传感器偏置部分（每粒子卡尔曼滤波分析追踪）。

Result: 仿真结果表明RBPF在不同粒子数下相比标准粒子滤波器在最终位置误差和RMSE上均表现更优；实地实验使用自主水面艇测量盐度、温度和溶解氧，验证了PDE控制的环境场具有足够的空间变异性可用于实际定位。

Conclusion: PDE控制的环境场可以作为有效的定位签名，RBPF框架通过模型分解有效减少了所需粒子数量并能处理传感器漂移，为无GPS环境下的自主导航提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a localization framework that uses spatiotemporal fields governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) as localization signatures. Two PDE classes are considered: the shallow water equations, which describe free-surface flows in coastal and riverine environments, and the advection-diffusion equation, which models the transport and mixing of scalar quantities such as temperature, salinity, and dissolved oxygen. A numerical PDE solver provides predicted fields over the domain, and multiple field channels are fused as multimodal measurements to improve localization accuracy. We formulate the problem within a Rao-Blackwellized particle filter (RBPF) that partitions the vehicle state into a nonlinear component sampled by particles and a linear sensor bias component tracked analytically via per-particle Kalman filters. This factorization reduces the required number of particles compared to a standard particle filter while accounting for realistic sensor drift. Simulation studies on both PDE scenarios show that the RBPF consistently outperforms a standard particle filter in terms of final position error and Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) across varying particle counts. Field experiments with an autonomous surface vehicle measuring salinity, temperature, and dissolved oxygen validate that PDE-governed environmental fields provide sufficient spatial variability for practical localization. Related experimental videos are available at https://localization-environmental-pdes.github.io/.

</details>


### [286] [Hybrid Attention Estimation Pipeline for Adaptive HRI Using an Expressive Robotic Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00284)
*Pablo Moraes,Monica Rodriguez,Christopher Peters,Hiago Sodre,Tobias Doernbach,Bruna Guterres,Ricardo Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向人机交互的混合视觉注意力估计方法，基于InMoov表达性机器人头，结合快速几何感知层和基于视觉语言模型的语义感知层，通过有限状态机调节自适应交互行为，并在10名参与者40次试验中验证了系统的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中，机器人需要准确估计人类的视觉注意力以调节交互行为，但传统几何方法（如面部和头部姿态估计）无法捕捉语义层面的注意力内容（如是否在看手机），而纯语义方法又难以满足高频实时性要求。

Method: 提出双层次混合管线：几何感知层以高频提供面部和头部姿态信息用于时间调节，语义感知层基于视觉语言模型仅接收原始第一人称摄像头帧，生成关注机器人、使用手机或关注他处的上下文注意力标签；两者通过有限状态机集成，实现激活、等待、交互恢复和回归静止等自适应行为调节。

Result: 在10名参与者的40次试验中，系统在所有试验中均能可靠启动交互，自适应分心条件下展现出一致的暂停行为，且几何层与语义层的输出之间具有非冗余的语义信息。

Conclusion: 混合视觉注意力估计方法能够有效结合高频几何信息与丰富语义信息，实现可靠且自适应的机器人交互行为调节，验证了该方法的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents an applied case study on hybrid visual attention estimation for human-robot interaction using an expressive robotic head based on the InMoov ecosystem. The proposed pipeline combines a fast geometric perception layer with an independent semantic perception layer based on a vision-language model. The geometric layer provides high-frequency face and head-pose information for temporal regulation, while the semantic layer receives only raw egocentric camera frames and produces contextual attention labels related to attention toward the robot, phone use, or attention elsewhere. These signals are integrated through a finite state machine that regulates adaptive interaction behavior, including activation, waiting, interaction resumption, and return to rest. The system was evaluated with 10 participants across 40 trials covering baseline and adaptive interaction conditions. Results show reliable interaction start across all trials, consistent pause behavior in the adaptive distraction condition, and non-redundant semantic information between the geometric and semantic outputs.

</details>


### [287] [Towards General Language-Conditioned Latent Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00315)
*Ihab Tabbara,Yuxuan Yang,Hussein Sibai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言条件化的安全过滤机制，通过将Hamilton-Jacobi安全演员和评论家网络条件化为语言指定的约束，使机器人政策能够适应不同用户和环境中变化的安全要求，实验证明该方法能减少约束违规并具有一定泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLA模型的普及，机器人策略需要适应不断变化的安全要求，而现有的安全过滤器大多是约束特定的，当安全要求改变时需要重新设计或重新学习，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出语言条件化的安全过滤框架，将Hamilton-Jacobi安全演员和评论家网络以语言指定的约束为条件进行训练，在视觉环境下对抓取放置、擦桌和积木堆叠等任务进行实验评估。

Result: 实验表明语言条件化的安全过滤器能够有效减少约束违规，并在评估的约束族内对未见过的约束实例展现出部分迁移能力。

Conclusion: 语言条件化的安全过滤是一种有前景的方向，能够在不重新设计过滤器的情况下适应新的安全需求，但仍需要进一步提升对未见约束实例的泛化能力。

Abstract: Robot policies are becoming increasingly general, with vision-language-action (VLA) models enabling a single policy to execute diverse tasks specified in natural language. Safe deployment, however, requires adapting not only to new tasks but also to varying safety requirements across users, environments, and applications. Existing safety filters remain largely constraint-specific and thus must be redesigned or relearned when safety requirements change. In this paper, we investigate language-conditioned safety filtering, in which a Hamilton-Jacobi safety actor and critic are conditioned on language-specified constraints. We evaluate this formulation across pick-and-place, table-wiping, and block-stacking tasks in the vision-based setting, examining its ability to enforce language-specified constraints and transfer to unseen constraint instances within the evaluated constraint families. Our experiments provide evidence that language-conditioned safety filters reduce constraint violations and exhibit partial transfer to unseen constraint instances.

</details>


### [288] [Belief-Space Perception Routing under Coupled Sensor Faults and Compute Contention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00322)
*Sparsh Roy,Vihan Aggarwal,Davin Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种感知路由器（perception router），它联合估计传感器故障状态和计算竞争状态，并通过噪声-OR项耦合二者，动态选择四种YOLO检测器配置之一，以确保帧在截止时间前完成。在故障与计算压力同时出现时，耦合策略比独立处理两者的策略将截止时间错过率降低1.1至9.4个百分点，但后续在真实数据集上的24项测试均未发现这种耦合自然存在，因此作者将路由结果限定为机制证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视觉系统面临两个相互交织的问题：恶劣天气下摄像头性能退化，以及共享处理器上计算资源随时间波动。现有系统通常将这两个问题分开建模，但作者推测二者的耦合关系可能被利用来优化感知调度。

Method: 提出一个感知路由器，对传感器故障状态和计算竞争状态进行概率估计，通过噪声-OR项将两者耦合，利用耦合估计在四种YOLO检测器配置（YOLO11x/n，1280或640像素）中做出选择，确保帧在截止时间内完成。

Result: 在传感器故障与计算压力同时存在的情况下，耦合策略比独立策略的截止时间错过率降低1.1至9.4个百分点；六种条件中有五种置信区间不包含零，合并效应符号检验p=0.001。然而在8个真实RADIATE恶劣天气序列和3种工作负载代理上的24项测试（经Benjamini-Hochberg校正及重复运行）均未发现耦合的自然存在。路由开销仅每帧数十微秒。

Conclusion: 耦合策略在人为构造的压力同时出现时有效，但真实数据中未发现这种耦合自然存在，因此结果被限定为机制证明。作者发布了评估管线，让部署者可在自己的数据上进行验证，该问题在实际场景中是否成立仍待探索。

Abstract: A robot that has to see and react on a fixed clock runs into two problems at once. Its cameras degrade in rain, mud, fog, and darkness. And the single onboard processor it runs on is shared with planning and control, so the compute left over for perception moves around from second to second. Most systems model the two separately. We present a perception router that tracks probabilistic estimates of sensor-fault state and compute- contention state, couples them with a noisy-OR term, and uses the coupled estimate to pick one of four detector configurations (YOLO11x/n at 1280 or 640 px) so that the frame finishes before its deadline. Where the two stressors co-occur, the coupled policy cuts the deadline-miss rate by 1.1 to 9.4 percentage points against a policy that treats them independently. The interval excludes zero in five of six conditions, the pooled effect over 10 sequences and 6 conditions has sign-test p = 0.001, and every uncoupled control and the fault-free trajectory sit at exactly 0.0 pp. Routing costs tens of microseconds per frame. We then asked whether the coupling the method exploits arises on its own. Across eight real RADIATE adverse-weather sequences and three workload proxies independent of the fault signal, after Benjamini-Hochberg correction and a replication run, none of 24 tests found it. We report that null and scope the routing result as a proof of mechanism. Whether such coupling occurs in the field is still open, and the released evaluation pipeline lets a deployment settle it on its own traces.

</details>


### [289] [Action Chunk Scheduling for Batched Robot Policy Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00337)
*Rohan Bansal,David He,Nadun Ranawaka Arachchige,Zhenyang Chen,Soobum Kim,Kexin Rong,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了机器人策略远程服务调度问题，构建了Armory服务系统，并提出适应异构机器人动作块消费速率的调度算法，在真实实验中吞吐量提升最高达18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型（如VLA）计算量大，但机器人端设备受功耗和空间限制，无法在本地部署。需要探索从远程GPU为多台机器人提供策略服务的可行性，并解决其调度挑战。

Method: 将多机器人远程策略服务问题形式化为调度问题，构建Armory服务系统，并在模拟和真实机器人集群上验证。针对不同机器人消费动作块速率不同的异构场景，提出考虑该异质性的调度算法。

Result: 在真实实验中，所提出的调度算法相比朴素启发式方法将系统整体吞吐量提升了最高18%。

Conclusion: 传统的批处理方法与机器人策略执行的闭环要求之间存在不匹配，考虑机器人异构性的调度算法能够有效改善系统吞吐量，为机器人基础模型的大规模部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying robot foundation models at scale is the next step towards realizing the potential of general-purpose robots. However, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and other foundation models are computationally demanding, and on-device compute is constrained by power and space. In this paper, we introduce the problem of serving a robot policy to multiple robots from a remote GPU and formulate it as a scheduling problem. We build Armory, a serving system validated on fleets of both simulated and real robots. Our experiments show that naive scheduling heuristics perform well when all robots are the same, but fall short when robots consume action chunks at different rates, uncovering a mismatch between conventional batching methods and the closed-loop requirements of robot policy execution. To address this, we propose a scheduling algorithm that accounts for this heterogeneity and improves overall system throughput by up to $18\%$ in real-world experiments. Additional details are available at https://gatech-rl2.github.io/actionchunkscheduling.

</details>


### [290] [A Robotic System for Automated Manufacturing of Dielectric Elastomer Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00369)
*Van Remenar,Anatol Gogoj,Ang Li,Tomas Verardi,Matei Mandoiu,Victor Jimenez Santiago,Mihai Duduta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于软体电容器（兼具执行器和传感器功能）的自动化机器人制造系统，重点优化了旋涂法制备介电层和冲压法涂覆碳纳米管电极两大关键工艺。该系统成功制造了20个多层介电弹性体执行器（DEA），每个包含12层平均厚度55.37±2.04μm的介电层和11个交替CNT电极，总工艺时间缩短14.2%，且56.1%的工艺时间无需操作员参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体电容器（作为执行器和传感器）的制造质量高度依赖介电层形成和电极涂覆两个关键工艺，但目前缺乏自动化制造系统，传统人工制造存在效率低、质量不稳定等问题。

Method: 开发自动化机器人制造系统，重点优化两个关键工艺：采用旋涂法进行介电层形成，采用冲压法进行碳纳米管（CNT）电极涂覆，实现多层DEA的自动化批量制造。

Result: 成功制造20个多层DEA，每个包含12层介电层（平均厚度55.37±2.04μm）和11个交替CNT电极；总工艺时间减少14.2%，56.1%的工艺时间实现了无人化操作。

Conclusion: 自动化机器人制造系统能够有效提升软体电容器的制造效率和质量一致性，显著减少人工参与时间，为软体执行器/传感器的规模化生产提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This letter presents an automated robotic manufacturing system for soft capacitors which operate as actuators and sensors. Emphasis is placed on the two processes that most directly govern device quality, dielectric layer formation by spin coating and carbon nanotube (CNT) electrode application by stamping. Twenty multilayer DEAs, each comprising 12 dielectric layers with a mean thickness of 55.37 +- 2.04 um and 11 alternating CNT electrodes, were fabricated reducing total process time by 14.2% and removing the operator from 56.1% of it.

</details>


### [291] [The Gate, Not the Cache: Gate Provenance Bounds the Closed-Loop Reliability of Training-Free VLA Token Skipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00391)
*Qi Luo,Shuaijun Liu,Hao Zhao,Kunlin Li,Xiaobo Wang,Ningxing Su,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中token跳过加速方法的闭环可靠性问题，发现性能崩溃的关键不在于跳过机制（复用或删除），而在于门控信号来源是否为无跳过的密集前向传播。作者提出"执行间隙刷新"（actuation-slack refresh）方法，在机器人执行动作块的间隙执行一次密集前向传播，为非关键路径提供干净的门控和新的KV缓存，使两种机制的成功率从0.68/0.31恢复到0.98，且延迟比密集推理低18-22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型使用token跳过加速时，当门控从模型自身加速前向中获取时，被跳过的token对下一门控不可见，错误会在控制步间累积导致任务失败。现有加速方法未关注门控信号来源对闭环可靠性的影响，且现有评估的检测器无法捕捉这种崩溃。

Method: 1) 系统比较两种机制（复用reuse和删除deletion）与两种门控来源（自身加速前向和干净密集前向）的交叉组合；2) 提出actuation-slack refresh方法：在机器人执行动作块期间，利用执行间隙进行一次密集前向传递，为下一步提供干净的门控和新的KV缓存；3) 由于现有检测器无法可靠检测失败，刷新采用无条件触发而非条件触发。

Result: 在LIBERO-Object上，skip ratio为0.9时，使用自身加速门控时reuse降至0.68、deletion降至0.31（密集为1.00），且该崩溃对动作级检测器不可见。引入actuation-slack refresh后，两种机制均恢复至0.98。在2个VLA策略、4个LIBERO套件和4个SIMPLER任务上，刷新修复了所有由自采门控引起的崩溃，服务延迟比密集推理低18-22%（仿真和物理机器人均验证）。

Conclusion: 对于加速版VLA模型的闭环可靠性，决定性因素是门控信号来自哪里（是否来自无跳过的密集前向），而非token如何被跳过。actuation-slack refresh通过在关键路径之外利用机器人执行间隙来完成密集前向，既保留了token跳过的速度优势，又恢复了密集前向的信息完整性，实现了加速与可靠性的兼得。

Abstract: Token skipping is a widely used training-free way to accelerate vision--language--action (VLA) models by bypassing computation for most visual tokens at each control step according to a gate. When the next gate is harvested from the previous accelerated forward, however, the tokens skipped at one step are also the ones least visible to the next gate, and the damage can compound across control steps until the task fails. We study the two mechanisms this class is built on, reuse and deletion, crossing each against where its gate signal comes from on identical episodes. At a skip ratio of 0.9 on LIBERO-Object, both collapse when the gate comes from the model's own accelerated forwards, to 0.68 under reuse and to 0.31 under deletion against a dense 1.00, and the collapse is invisible to the action-level detectors we evaluate. What separates collapse from dense-level operation is not the mechanism but whether the gate is clean, computed by a forward that skipped nothing. We therefore propose actuation-slack refresh, one dense pass run while the robot executes its current action chunk, off the critical path, that hands the next step a clean gate and a fresh KV base. Since the measured detectors do not reliably reveal the failure, the refresh is unconditional rather than triggered. Both mechanisms then recover to 0.98, keeping the speed of skipping and the information of a dense pass. We then integrate the refresh into state-of-the-art caching and pruning methods across two VLA policies, 4 LIBERO suites, and 4 SIMPLER tasks, where it repairs every collapse caused by using a self-harvested gate. Serve latency drops 18--22\% below dense, measured both in simulation and on a physical robot. Where the gate signal comes from, not how tokens are skipped, decides closed-loop reliability for accelerated VLAs.

</details>


### [292] [Embodied Passive Aeroacoustic Perception Enables Relative Sensing and Pursuit Between Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00401)
*Yanbaihui Liu,Ravi Prakash,Li-Yu Lo,Nils Roede,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SonicFly的被动气动声学感知框架，使无人机仅通过聆听另一架无人机飞行时自然产生的声音即可实现相对状态估计和跟随，无需主动声学信号、通信或GPS。实验表明该方法在户外追击任务中实现了1.34米的平均距离保持误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行器在飞行中会产生结构化的气动声场，但这些信号尚未被充分探索作为机载相对感知的信息源，尤其是在强自噪声干扰和多样户外条件下。作者旨在探索利用自然飞行声音进行被动相对感知的可行性。

Method: SonicFly框架包含轻量四麦克风阵列、旋翼机感知的声学表征、神经方位-距离估计器以及置信度门控滤波，用于闭环飞行控制。系统通过声学特征分析、机载定位和户外追击实验进行验证。

Result: 在声学仅有的追击实验中，SonicFly在多样户外轨迹和操作条件下实现了1.34米的平均距离保持误差。声学通道分析揭示了谐波结构、频谱可分性和空间声学线索在可观测性中的作用。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了空中机器人被动气动声学感知的可行性，表明自然产生的行为信号可以作为机器人感知和协调的信息来源，为未来无需额外基础设施的无人机协同感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Aerial robots generate structured aeroacoustic fields during flight, yet these signals have been underexplored as a source of onboard relative perception, particularly under the strong ego-acoustic interference generated during simultaneous flight in various outdoor conditions. We introduce embodied passive aeroacoustic perception, a sensing paradigm in which an aerial robot infers actionable relative-state information from the naturally generated sound of flight while operating within its own evolving aeroacoustic field. We present SonicFly, a passive aeroacoustic perception framework that enables one unmanned aerial vehicle to estimate and follow another using only the leader's intrinsic flight sound, without active acoustic signaling, inter-robot communication, GPS sharing, or external sensing infrastructure. The system uses a lightweight four-microphone array, rotorcraft-informed acoustic representations, a neural bearing-range estimator, and confidence-gated filtering for closed-loop flight. Through acoustic characterization, onboard localization, and outdoor pursuit experiments, we show that multirotor aeroacoustic signals contain sufficient information to support relative perception despite strong ego-acoustic interference, environmental variability, and changing flight geometry. During acoustic-only pursuit, SonicFly achieved a mean distance-maintenance error of 1.34 m across diverse outdoor trajectories and operating conditions. Analysis of the acoustic channel reveals design principles governing embodied passive aeroacoustic perception, including the roles of harmonic structure, spectral separability, and spatial acoustic cues in determining observability. Our results establish the feasibility of embodied passive aeroacoustic perception for aerial robots and suggest that naturally generated behavioral signals can serve as information for robotic perception and coordination.

</details>


### [293] [Learning to Predict Contact Force Distributions from Vision Leveraging Object Geometry Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00464)
*Ryo Hanai,Yukiyasu Domaea,Ixchel G. Ramirez-Alpizar,Abdullah Mustafa,Floris Erich,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出从单张RGB图像预测三维力分布的方法，通过对仿真器输出的点力进行统计平滑（并结合物体几何信息）来训练模型，使机器人能在堆叠日常物体场景中像人类一样进行物理预测和操作规划，且能从仿真泛化到真实环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能基于视觉和经验做出粗略的物理预测并调整操作策略，但传统机器人仿真器输出的点力噪声大且预测不稳定。作者假设预测平滑的力分布比原始点力更能提升预测准确性和下游任务性能。

Method: 使用刚体仿真器采集视觉与力的配对数据；对仿真器输出的点力施加统计平滑（并结合物体几何信息进行几何引导平滑）生成目标三维力分布；构建从单张RGB图像预测三维力分布的模型。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中均进行了广泛评估。平滑方法提升了力预测准确性和下游任务性能，几何引导平滑进一步带来了增益；仅用仿真数据训练的模型能够有效泛化到真实世界场景。

Conclusion: 预测平滑的力分布（尤其是结合几何引导的平滑）能有效提升机器人在堆叠物体场景中的物理预测能力与操作表现，且仿真到真实的迁移是可行的。

Abstract: Based on vision and prior experience, humans can make rough physical predictions and adjust their manipulation strategies. This paper aims to endow robots with a similar ability. To collect paired data of vision and forces, we use a rigid-body simulator commonly adopted in robotics. However, unlike simulators that output noisy point forces, humans are able to make consistent predictions even in unfamiliar situations. Based on this observation, we hypothesize that predicting smooth force distributions rather than raw point forces can improve both force prediction itself and downstream task performance. To validate this hypothesis, we construct a model that predicts three-dimensional force distributions from a single RGB image of piled daily objects. The target distribution is generated by applying statistical smoothing to point forces obtained from the simulator. Moreover, by incorporating object geometry into the smoothing process, we aim to account for variations in contact states and achieve more consistent vision-based predictions. We conduct extensive evaluations in both simulation and real environments. Results show that our approach improves prediction accuracy, enhances downstream task performance through smoothing, and further benefits from geometry-guided smoothing. Remarkably, the trained model generalizes effectively to real-world scenes despite being trained solely in simulation.

</details>


### [294] [From Digital to Physical Reservoir Computing: Co-Optimizing Soft Robotic Reservoirs via Dynamics Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00484)
*Nicola Visentin,Maximilian Stölzle,Mariano Ramírez Montero,Francesco Braghin,Daniela Rus,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种软体机器人物理储层计算（PRC）的动力学级协同优化方法，通过将物理储层预训练到高性能数字参考动力学（随机振荡器网络）上，利用可微物理模型和加速度级方程误差目标函数进行联合优化，在多个分类和预测任务上相比未优化的软体机器人储层平均相对提升33.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层在软体机器人PRC中具有天然优势（柔顺非线性动力学提供时间记忆和高维状态变换），但现有做法通常是"拿来即用"，缺乏预训练或协同优化，导致其性能可能不如数字储层。作者试图弥合这一差距，探索物理储层能否通过预训练对齐高性能数字参考动力学来提升性能。

Method: 提出一个联合优化框架：同时优化物理参数、微分同胚的物理-参考状态映射以及前馈-反馈控制。采用可微物理模型和加速度级方程误差目标函数（避免时间积分），并以模拟软体机器人、随机振荡器网络（RON）参考以及并行多起点梯度下降作为实现方案。

Result: 在四个储层维度下，于sMNIST和ADIAC分类任务以及Mackey-Glass和Lorenz96预测任务上评估。优化后的储层相比未优化的软体机器人储层在所有任务和数据集上实现平均相对33.7%的提升，且性能接近数字参考。

Conclusion: 本研究表明在所考虑的模拟软体机器人储层中，动力学级别的协同优化是可行且有效的，证明了物理储层可以通过预训练到数字参考动力学上来显著提升性能，为软体机器人PRC的进一步研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Soft robotic substrates are promising for Physical Reservoir Computing (PRC) because their compliant nonlinear dynamics can provide temporal memory, high-dimensional state transformations, and efficient inference. However, physical reservoirs are often adopted as-is rather than pretrained or co-optimized, potentially limiting soft robotic PRC performance relative to digital reservoirs. We investigate whether a physical reservoir can instead be pretrained against high-performing digital reference dynamics. Our formulation jointly optimizes physical parameters, a diffeomorphic physical-reference state map, and feedforward-feedback control using a differentiable physical model and an acceleration-level equation-error objective that avoids temporal integration. As a proof of concept, we instantiate the formulation with simulated soft robots, a Random Oscillators Network (RON) reference, and parallel multi-start gradient descent. We evaluate the optimized reservoirs on classification (sMNIST and ADIAC) and forecasting (Mackey-Glass and Lorenz96) tasks across four reservoir dimensions. Compared with unoptimized soft robot reservoirs, the optimized reservoirs achieve a mean relative improvement of 33.7% across all tasks and datasets, while remaining close to the digital reference. These results demonstrate the feasibility of dynamics-level co-optimization for the simulated soft robotic reservoirs considered here.

</details>


### [295] [First Deployable Dynamic-CoM: A Unified Policy and Method-Agnostic Benchmark for Humanoid Single-Leg Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00500)
*Yikai Zhou,Xingyun Wang,Jieming Cui,Bozhou Chen,Yikai Fan,Yixin Zhu,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FDDC策略，通过将可部署的动态质心（dynamic-CoM）观测直接融入硬件策略，并配以从人类姿势控制逐项翻译的奖励库，在单腿平衡基准上大幅超越八种现有SOTA通用策略（从0/90提升到86/90），并成功迁移到真实Unitree G1机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形通用策略虽能处理敏捷全身运动，但无法保持单腿平衡——8种SOTA策略在90个测试动作中没有一个能保持干净的单腿站立，只能通过迈步或跳跃恢复平衡而非预防失衡。关键在于"预防"需要捕捉点（xCoM），但它需要的基线线速度没有板载传感器可提供，因此从未驱动过硬件策略。

Method: 将动态质心观测相对于支撑脚表达，使基线线速度恰好消去，得到仅由编码器和IMU即可重构的观测，并直接放入运行在硬件上的actor。同时构建逐项从人类姿势控制翻译的奖励库，遵循"预防优于修复"原则，使用带特权评论家且无需蒸馏的非对称FastSAC训练。

Result: FDDC在90个测试动作中能保持干净单腿平衡86个（覆盖9个分层姿态类别），成功迁移到真实Unitree G1；消融实验显示动态质心观测是最大贡献因素，单独移除损失40分。还发布了首个方法无关、可复现的sim2sim单腿平衡基准。

Conclusion: 动态质心观测首次在硬件上可行，使单腿平衡从"修复"变为"预防"成为一种可测量的能力。作者认为这是在将平衡从特定任务技巧转化为领域可度量能力上迈出的一步。

Abstract: Unified humanoid policies handle agile whole-body motion, yet stumble on a simple demand: staying balanced on one leg. On our single-leg-balance benchmark, eight released state-of-the-art general policies hold a clean single-leg stance on 0 of 90 test motions; they stay up only by stepping or hopping, recovering from imbalance rather than preventing it. Prevention needs the capture point (xCoM), the center of mass (CoM) extrapolated by its velocity, which has never driven a hardware policy because it requires a base linear velocity no on-board sensor provides; expressed relative to the support foot, that velocity cancels exactly, leaving an observation reconstructible from encoders and IMU alone. We put this first deployable dynamic-CoM observation directly into the actor that runs on hardware, and pair it with a reward library translated term by term from human postural control, under one principle: prevention over repair. Trained by asymmetric FastSAC with a privileged critic and no distillation, the resulting policy, FDDC (First Deployable Dynamic-CoM), holds clean single-leg balance on 86 of 90 held-out motions across nine stratified pose classes and transfers to a real Unitree G1; in ablation, the dynamic-CoM observation is the single largest driver: removing it alone costs 40 points of clean single-leg balance. We release the full stack with the first method-agnostic, reproducible sim2sim benchmark for humanoid single-leg balance, scoring each policy in a simulator distinct from its training one, a step toward turning balance from a per-task trick into a capability the field can measure.

</details>


### [296] [SSTG-Nav: Metric-Grounded Spatial-Semantic Topological Graphs for Reusable Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00527)
*Daojie Peng,Bingtao Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SSTG-Nav，一种可复用的度量-语义记忆系统，将一次性扫描转化为可操作的物体目标，通过跨视角证据融合和空间分离的恢复待机位置，显著提升服务机器人在长期部署中的语义导航可靠性，在HM3D-v2基准上将SR/SPL从0.835/0.560提升至0.926/0.586。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ObjectNav主要被表述为一次性探索任务，未解决实际部署中的核心挑战：识别到物体不代表知道可达的停车位置，且单个地图错误即可导致任务失败。长期在相同环境中运行的机器人不应每次请求都从头搜索熟悉空间。

Method: 提出SSTG-Nav框架：1) 构建目标无关的可复用度量-语义记忆；2) 通过一次性扫描建立可操作物体目标；3) 跨视角融合证据；4) 保留空间上分离的恢复待机位置（Top-3恢复策略）；5) 结合源感知融合机制，并在ROS2/Nav2平台上实现完整流水线。

Result: 在36个场景的1000个HM3D-v2 episode上，几何成功上界达99.4%；固定语义响应时，度量接地将SR/SPL从0.835/0.560提升至0.920/0.603，源感知融合达0.926/0.586；Top-3恢复将Success@1/2/3提升至0.928/0.965/0.975，SPL@3达0.601。消融实验验证了模型、视场角、密度和损坏控制对增益来源的贡献。

Conclusion: 预探索（pre-exploration）是一种强大且实用的导航范式，能够支撑可靠、可重复的长期语义导航任务，为服务机器人在真实环境中长期部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Service robots operating for months in the same homes, offices, and facilities should become more reliable with experience instead of searching familiar space from scratch for every request. Yet ObjectNav is predominantly formulated as one-shot exploration, leaving a central deployment challenge unresolved: recognizing an object does not identify a reachable place to stop, and one confident map error can terminate the task. We introduce SSTG-Nav, a reusable metric-semantic memory that turns a one-time survey into actionable object goals, consolidates evidence across viewpoints, and retains spatially distinct recovery standoffs. On 1,000 HM3D-v2 episodes across 36 scenes, our goal-independent topology achieves a 99.4% geometric success ceiling. Holding semantic responses fixed, metric grounding raises SR/SPL from 0.835/0.560 to 0.920/0.603, and source-aware fusion reaches 0.926/0.586. Fusion-aware Top-3 recovery raises Success@1/2/3 to 0.928/0.965/0.975 and reaches 0.601 SPL@3. Model, field-of-view, density, and corruption controls identify where these gains originate, and a ROS2/Nav2 realization demonstrates the complete reusable query-to-execution pipeline. Together, the results establish pre-exploration as a powerful practical regime for dependable, repeated semantic navigation.

</details>


### [297] [Disentangling Visuo-Tactile Foresight: Oracle-Guided Interface Discovery for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00547)
*Zihang Yao,Chaoyue Ding,Yingying Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Oracle Visuo-Tactile Foresight (OVTF)框架，通过提供来自成功轨迹的成对RGB和触觉未来信号，独立研究未来视觉-触觉表示对动作专家学习的影响。基于该框架提出的不对称相位对齐未来记忆（AFM）在七个任务上达到32.0%的平均成功率，显著优于模态隔离基线（23.7%）和UniVTAC-ACT（14.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务中，物理交互线索难以仅从视觉中观测。虽然近期触觉世界动作模型联合建模未来视觉和触觉信号以指导动作生成，但未来信号应如何结构化才能被动作专家有效利用这一问题尚未被充分探索。直接在端到端模型中研究该问题存在困难，因为其无法分离物理无效的视觉未来、不可靠预测、跨模态不一致的触觉预测等问题对接口的影响。

Method: 引入OVTF框架，使用模拟中已验证的成功轨迹提供成对的RGB和触觉未来信号，从而固定未来提供者、独立研究未来到动作的接口。在该框架下提出AFM：视觉记忆读取未来视觉，每个触觉记忆同时关注自身触觉流和相位对齐的未来视觉，阻断跨触觉访问。与完全隔离模态的IFM基线进行对比。

Result: 在UniVTAC仿真基准的七个任务上，AFM达到32.0%的平均成功率，IFM为23.7%，UniVTAC-ACT为14.9%。结果表明选择性相位对齐的视觉-触觉路由比完全模态隔离提供了更可操作的未来到动作桥梁。

Conclusion: 选择性相位对齐的视觉-触觉路由是动作专家吸收未来信息益处的最佳表示方式，比简单的模态隔离更有效，为触觉世界动作模型中未来表示的构建提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation remains challenging because successful control depends on physical interaction cues that are often weakly observable from vision alone. Recent tactile world action models jointly model future visual observations and tactile signals to guide action generation, but how such futures should be structured for effective use by the action expert remains underexplored. Directly studying this question with learned world action models is difficult because end-to-end behavior entangles physically invalid visual futures, unreliable predictions, inaccurate or cross-modally inconsistent tactile forecasts, and an unreadable future-to-action interface. To make this interface independently studyable, we introduce Oracle Visuo-Tactile Foresight (OVTF), a controlled framework that supplies paired RGB and tactile futures from successful trajectories verified in simulation. By fixing the future provider, OVTF isolates the interface and asks a cleaner question: if the future is successful and physically executable, what representation allows the action expert to absorb its benefit? Within OVTF, we propose Asymmetric Phase-Local Future Memory (AFM), in which visual memory reads future vision, each tactile memory jointly attends to its own tactile stream and phase-aligned future vision, and cross-tactile access is blocked. We compare AFM with Modality-Isolated Future Memory (IFM), which removes visual-to-tactile access and processes each future modality independently. Across seven tasks on the UniVTAC simulation benchmark, AFM achieves 32.0% average success, compared with 23.7% for IFM and 14.9% for UniVTAC-ACT. This controlled comparison shows that selective phase-aligned visual-tactile routing provides a more actionable future-to-action bridge than complete modality isolation.

</details>


### [298] [DexMani: Human-Derived Manipulability Guidance for Dexterous Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00554)
*Xiaoyang Chen,Shengcheng Luo,Haoran Guo,Jiaming Jiang,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexMani是一个将人类演示转化为接触条件可操作性演化的框架，用于引导下游强化学习，从而使灵巧手在多种机器人平台上（Shadow Hand、Allegro Hand、XHand、LEAP Hand）实现卓越的物体旋转技能，在各类已见和未见物体上均取得最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在特定机器人手型上通过试错发现旋转模式，没有显式考虑每个接触转换对手部在后续步骤中维持旋转能力的影响。灵巧物体旋转本质上是序列接触问题，需要每次支撑、释放和重新接触决策既产生期望的物体运动，又为持续旋转预备手部构型。

Method: DexMani将人类演示作为"接触条件可操作性演化"的先验知识进行迁移，该先验捕捉了成功的人类接触转换如何重塑手部可用的旋转方向。框架学习这种可操作性演化规律，并用它来引导下游的强化学习过程，从而在不同运动学和主动接触构型的机器人平台上获取旋转技能。

Result: 在Shadow Hand、Allegro Hand和XHand上，DexMani在所有评估设置（包括已见和未见物体）中均取得最高成功率。在LEAP Hand上达到57.5%的平均成功率，优于其他基线方法，并产生更平滑的旋转运动。

Conclusion: 通过将人类演示中蕴含的接触转换知识作为可操作性演化先验引入强化学习，显著提升了跨机器人平台、跨物体的灵巧旋转技能获取能力，证明了该框架具有良好的一般性和实用性。

Abstract: Dexterous object rotation is a sequential contact problem: each support, release, and re-contact decision must both produce the desired object motion, and prepare the hand configuration for continued rotation. Existing reinforcement learning methods discover such movement patterns through trial and error on specific robotic hand embodiments, without explicitly accounting for how each contact transition affects the hand's ability to sustain object rotation in subsequent steps. We introduce DexMani, a framework that transfers human demonstrations as contact-conditioned manipulability evolution. This prior captures how successful human contact transitions reshape the object-rotation directions available to the hand. DexMani then learns this manipulability evolution and uses it to guide downstream reinforcement learning, enabling rotation skills to be acquired across robot embodiments with distinct kinematics and active-contact configurations. Across the Shadow Hand, Allegro Hand, and XHand, DexMani achieves the highest success rates in every evaluated setting for both seen and unseen objects. DexMani reaches an average success rate of 57.5% on LEAP Hand, outperforming other baselines and producing smoother rotatory motions. Project site: https://dexmani.github.io

</details>


### [299] [Latency-Tolerant Cloud-Edge Collaborative Vision-Language-Action Models via Emergent Representational Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00569)
*Daojie Peng,Fulong Ma,Bingtao Wang,Sheng Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CloudEdgeVLA是一种云-边协同的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，将云端大模型编码的延迟观测转化为缓慢变化的任务特征，边缘轻量模型结合最新云端特征和当前局部视觉进行响应式控制，在LIBERO基准上显著优于现有分层异步方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署十亿级参数VLA策略在移动机器人上存在系统冲突：语义推理受益于云端GPU，但闭环控制必须在本地快速响应，而网络延迟和抖动会导致慢路径表示过时。现有分层异步策略虽提升吞吐量，但慢路径表示仍可能过期，或需要显式调度和延迟提示。

Method: 将时间错位视为表示学习问题：云端VLA将延迟观测编码为缓慢变化的任务特征；边缘轻量头融合最新可用云端特征与当前局部视觉。训练时，当前帧和随机延迟帧与相同当前动作目标配对，分别走新路径和过期路径，促使云端表示保留任务级信息，边缘路径提供状态敏感修正。

Result: 在四个LIBERO套件中，CloudEdgeVLA在40步均匀延迟窗口下保持63.8-78.0%成功率，而VLASH最高仅6.4%，单路径基线最高仅3.0%。

Conclusion: 通过从控制回路中移除阻塞同步，该设计为可扩展的VLA部署提供了实用路径——云端模型可持续增大，而边缘计算保持轻量和响应式。

Abstract: Deploying billion-parameter Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies on mobile robots creates a systems conflict: semantic reasoning benefits from cloud GPUs, whereas closed-loop control must respond locally despite network delay and jitter. Existing hierarchical and asynchronous policies improve throughput, but their slow-path representations can still arrive stale or require explicit scheduling and delay cues. We introduce CloudEdgeVLA, a cloud-edge policy that treats temporal misalignment as a representation-learning problem. A cloud VLA encodes delayed observations into slowly varying task features, while a lightweight edge head combines the latest available cloud feature with current local vision. During training, current and randomly delayed frames are paired with the same current action target in fresh and stale paths. This objective encourages the cloud representation to preserve task-level information while the edge path supplies state-sensitive corrections. Across four LIBERO suites, CloudEdgeVLA retains 63.8--78.0% success with a 40-step uniform-delay window, whereas VLASH reaches at most 6.4% and the evaluated single-path baselines at most 3.0%. By removing blocking synchronization from the control loop, the design offers a practical route to scalable VLA deployment in which cloud models can grow while edge computation remains lightweight and responsive.

</details>


### [300] [Grasp Execution Without a Planner: Configuration-Space Grasp Distance Fields with Certified Safety & Guaranteed Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00600)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs)，一种基于手臂-手构型空间上的平滑softmin距离场，通过静止反馈律沿负梯度方向执行抓取，无需规划器、存储轨迹或抓取姿态选择。使用CBF-CLF二次规划进行安全过滤，并在预抓取构型处通过迟滞切换模式来处理手-物接触切换。在仿真中成功完成50个物体中46个的抓取与举起，保留了94%的抓取质量裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多指抓取执行架构需要预先规划无碰撞轨迹并跟踪目标抓取姿态，但执行过程中的物体姿态不确定性或扰动可能使规划轨迹失效，导致代价高昂的重新规划。作者希望消除对规划器和轨迹存储的依赖，实现一个无需显式抓取选择的反馈控制方法。

Method: 1) 构建抓取候选集上的平滑softmin距离场（GDF）；2) 通过跟随距离场负梯度实现静止反馈控制律；3) 使用CBF-CLF二次规划过滤控制命令，约束自碰撞、工作空间、物体和障碍物间隙，并将受阻进展作为显式松弛量报告；4) 在预抓取构型处采用带迟滞的模式切换，将物体碰撞规避切换为接触接纳；5) 使用力矩质量势垒保持实现抓取的力闭合裕度。

Result: 在固定基座机械臂和Unitree G1人形机器人上的运动学仿真中，控制器成功地在杂乱和动态场景中抓取并举起50个物体中的46个（涵盖原始、家居和对抗性物体类别）。举起过程中抓取质量裕度中位数保留94%，QP求解时间每20ms控制步仅0.09ms。逐步softmax权重证实控制器执行最近候选，无需单独的选择步骤。

Conclusion: GDF方法证明了无需规划器和抓取选择即可完成安全、鲁棒的抓取执行，理论保证softmin距离估计的误差上界以及安全集合的前向不变性，为多指抓取控制提供了一种新的反馈范式。

Abstract: Standard multifingered grasp execution architectures plan a collision-free trajectory to a selected grasp pose and track it with a feedback law. Execution-time object pose uncertainty or perturbations may invalidate the planned trajectory, forcing a costly replanning step. We present Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs), a smooth softmin distance field over grasp configurations in the arm-hand configuration space. Our controller executes a grasp by following this field's negative gradient with a stationary feedback law, eliminating the need for a planner, stored trajectory, or grasp selection. For safety, we filter this command through a CBF-CLF quadratic program (QP) constraining self-collision, workspace, object, and obstacle clearance, reporting impeded progress as explicit slack. We prove that the softmin tracks the true set distance within \(\log N/ρ\) for \(N\) candidates and smoothing parameter \(ρ\), and that the filtered loop renders the safe set forward invariant. Since no smooth field captures hand-object contact switching, we switch modes with hysteresis at a pregrasp configuration, trading object collision avoidance for contact admission. A wrench-quality barrier then keeps the realized grasp's force-closure margin within a prescribed tolerance of its value at hold onset. We evaluate in kinematic simulation on a fixed-base arm and a Unitree G1 humanoid, both fitted with the same underactuated hand. Our controller navigates cluttered and dynamic scenes to safely reach for, grasp, and lift 46 of 50 objects spanning primitive, household, and adversarial classes. Across those lifts, the executed grasps retain a median 94\% of their synthesized quality margin, at 0.09 ms QP solve time per 20 ms control step. Per-step softmax weights confirm that our controller executes the nearest candidate, obviating a separate selection step. Project page: www.clintonenwerem.com/gdf.

</details>


### [301] [From Failures to Supervision: DynamicEnvPlan for Robust Long-Horizon Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00613)
*Hao Yuan,Yuxin Wang,Lei Ji,Zhiwei Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicEnvPlan，一个面向动态物理环境的闭环高层规划框架，通过规划-扰动-守卫校正的数据合成流程生成恢复导向轨迹，并采用分阶段监督微调，使智能体能够在环境偏离和执行不确定性下学习恢复策略，在104个任务-场景组合上将成功率从33.3%提升至76.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界交互本质上是动态的，环境在执行过程中可能演化，要求智能体在非平稳条件下调整计划。现有具身任务基准虽能暴露此类失败，但往往将失败仅视为评估结果，而非可学习的恢复信号。作者希望将执行失败转化为可训练的信号，使规划器具备从扰动中恢复的能力。

Method: DynamicEnvPlan是一个闭环框架，包含：（1）扩展具身任务执行，引入人形智能体、高层原始技能、结构化语义记忆和可控扰动；（2）数据合成设计包含规划模块、扰动模块和守卫校正模块，将动态执行状态转化为恢复导向轨迹；（3）采用分阶段监督微调，使规划器同时从名义执行轨迹和受扰恢复轨迹中学习。

Result: 在104个任务-场景组合上，涵盖i.i.d.、组合泛化和分布外设置，DynamicEnvPlan将基础规划器的成功率从33.3%提升至76.2%，并在所有七项评估指标上均取得改善，包括安全性和可负担性（affordance）合规。

Conclusion: 将环境扰动下的执行失败转化为可学习的恢复信号，并通过对恢复轨迹的分阶段微调，能够显著提升具身智能体在动态物理环境中的长期规划能力，验证了闭环学习框架的有效性。

Abstract: Physical-world interaction is inherently dynamic, as environments can evolve during execution, requiring agents to adapt their plans under non-stationary conditions. We study this challenge through long-horizon embodied planning under environment deviations and execution uncertainty. Existing embodied-task benchmarks can expose such failures, but these failures are usually treated as evaluation outcomes instead of learnable signals for training agents to recover. In this work, we introduce DynamicEnvPlan, a closed-loop framework for high-level planning in dynamic environments. It extends embodied task execution with humanoid agents, high-level primitive skills, structured semantic memory, and controllable perturbations. Our data synthesis design consists of planning, perturbation, and guarded correction modules that turn dynamic execution states into recovery-oriented traces. The resulting traces are used for staged supervised fine-tuning, enabling the planner to learn from both nominal execution and perturbed recovery trajectories. Using 104 task-scene combinations spanning i.i.d., compositional generalization, and out-of-distribution settings for fine-tuning and evaluation, DynamicEnvPlan boosts success rate from 33.3% for the base planner to 76.2%, while improving across all seven evaluation metrics critical to physical-world interaction, including safety and affordance compliance.

</details>


### [302] [Learning-Based Motion Planning for Dynamic Environments: From Foundational Algorithms to Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00625)
*Zongyuan Shen,Shalabh Gupta,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Gao Wang,Rui Cheng,Yaming Ou,Zhongqiang Ren,Yikui Zhai,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2015至2025年间动态环境下的运动规划研究，重点探讨了基于学习的方法如何扩展、补充或与经典规划方法交互，提出了一个"学习角色"分类法，将现有方法分为直接策略学习、学习增强经典规划、混合规划和训练增强方法四类，并分析了关键因素及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的运动规划是机器人学的基础问题，涉及自动驾驶、服务机器人、仓储物流等多个应用领域。近年来基于学习的方法快速发展，但缺乏一个系统的框架来理解学习方法与经典规划方法之间的关系和交互方式。

Method: 提出"学习角色"分类法，将学习方法按在规划流程中的参与方式分为四类：直接策略学习、学习增强经典规划、混合规划和训练增强方法。对每类方法总结问题设定、代表性算法、核心思想、集成机制、优缺点，并分析观测表示、预测不确定性、交互建模、规划器集成、安全约束和训练策略等关键维度。

Result: 系统梳理了2015-2025年间代表性工作，建立了学习与经典规划方法关系的分类框架，总结了各方法的优势与局限，并识别出影响学习型动态运动规划的关键因素。

Conclusion: 指出未来研究方向包括解决仿真到现实的差距、安全可认证规划、密集人群导航、感知-规划耦合以及具身人工智能等开放挑战。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic environments is a fundamental problem in robotics, aiming to generate safe and efficient paths, trajectories, or control actions in the presence of moving obstacles, uncertain predictions, and multi-agent interactions. It has broad applications in autonomous driving, service robotics, warehouse logistics, human-robot collaboration, crowd navigation, and multi-robot systems. This survey reviews representative works published primarily between 2015 and 2025, with a particular focus on how recent learning-based advances extend, complement, or interact with classical planning foundations. We first revisit classical planning methods as algorithmic foundations and reference frameworks for learning-based extensions. We then propose a role-of-learning taxonomy that categorizes existing methods according to how learning participates in the planning pipeline, including direct policy learning, learning-augmented classical planning, hybrid planning, and training enhancement methods. For each category, we summarize the main problem settings, representative algorithms, key ideas, integration mechanisms, strengths, and limitations. We further analyze how observation representations, prediction uncertainty, interaction modeling, planner integration, safety constraints, and training strategies shape learning-based motion planning in dynamic environments. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including sim-to-real gap, safe and certifiable planning, dense crowd navigation, perception-planning coupling, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [303] [FlowPilot: Real-Time World-Action Modeling for Agile UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00635)
*Runqing Wang,Ding Yu,Pengyuan Min,Xinhong Zhang,Wei Xiao,Yu Hu,Jie Chen,Fu Zhang,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowPilot，一种紧凑的世界-动作模型，用于无人机的实时机载导航。通过流匹配联合去噪未来深度观测和可执行轨迹，采用双流混合Transformer耦合视频和动作专家，在部署时仅输出轨迹，以度7伯恩斯坦多项式参数化动作保证C²连续可跟踪性，并在仿真与真实四旋翼上验证了高效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无人机导航方案存在两类局限：map-then-optimize管道需要局部重建，计算开销大；端到端策略缺乏显式场景预测能力，难以应对动态和杂乱环境。因此需要一种既能进行未来场景预测又能生成可执行轨迹的紧凑模型。

Method: FlowPilot采用流匹配（flow matching）框架，联合去噪未来的深度观测和可执行轨迹，形成世界-动作联合建模。模型使用双流混合Transformer（dual-stream mixture-of-transformers），通过共享注意力机制耦合视频专家和动作专家。部署时以动作中心方式运行，仅输出轨迹。轨迹由度7伯恩斯坦多项式参数化，当前状态约束初始控制点，网络预测五个自由控制点，提供C²连续的参考路径（含速度、加速度和加加速度的闭式解）。训练采用三级深度金字塔：高吞吐仿真、真实感仿真和真实机载数据。

Result: 在闭环仿真中，FlowPilot在杂乱程度增加和命令速度高达8m/s的情况下，优于学习和优化类基线方法。在物理四旋翼上，完整的感知到动作管道在Jetson Orin NX上运行时间低于18ms，在室内杂乱环境和森林环境中仅使用机载传感和计算即可达到5.5m/s的速度。

Conclusion: FlowPilot通过联合建模未来场景预测与轨迹生成，在保证实时性和可跟踪性的同时，显著提升了无人机在杂乱环境中的导航性能，为机载自主导航提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We present FlowPilot, a compact world-action model for real-time onboard UAV navigation from depth. Unlike map-then-optimize pipelines that require local reconstruction or end-to-end policies that lack explicit scene prediction, FlowPilot jointly denoises future depth observations and executable trajectories with flow matching. A dual-stream mixture-of-transformers couples video and action experts through shared attention, allowing future-scene prediction and trajectory generation to inform each other. At deployment, the model runs action-centrically and outputs only a trajectory. To ensure trackability, actions are parameterized as degree-7 Bernstein polynomials: the current state constrains the initial control points, and the network predicts five free control points, yielding C^2-continuous references with closed-form velocity, acceleration and jerk. FlowPilot is trained on a three-level depth pyramid spanning high-throughput simulation, photorealistic simulation, and real onboard data. In closed-loop simulation, it outperforms learning- and optimization-based baselines under increasing clutter and commanded speeds up to 8m/s. On a physical quadrotor, the full perception-to-action pipeline runs in under 18ms on a Jetson Orin NX and reaches 5.5m/s in cluttered indoor and forest environments using only onboard sensing and computation.

</details>


### [304] [Assistant Placement Aria: A Benchmark for Egocentric Placement Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00652)
*Amir Belder,Gonçalo Dias Pais,Refael Vivanti,Omri Carmi,Daniel DeTone,Oren Shrout,Ido Gattegno,Ayellet Tal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Assistant Placement Aria，这是首个面向虚拟放置（VP）任务的基准数据集，涵盖全局、局部和以人为中心的约束，包含合成与真实室内场景，并标注了2D面板放置、坐姿建议和电视放置三个子任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助场景中，选择符合人类意图的目标放置位置是一个关键挑战。传统放置任务只关注单一预定义位置，而虚拟放置（VP）需要识别所有合理的放置位置，涉及全局和局部的场景几何、语义及合理性推理，但目前缺乏相关基准数据来推动该领域研究。

Method: 构建了Assistant Placement Aria基准数据集，包含合成和真实室内场景，以2D图像、3D点云和场景文本描述三种模态呈现，并设计了三个VP子任务：2D面板放置、坐姿建议和电视放置。还评估了多种基础模型在目标检测和分割上的表现。

Result: 成功构建了首个面向虚拟放置任务的基准数据集，并在该基准上评估了多个目标检测与分割基础模型的性能。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了虚拟放置领域数据匮乏的空白，有望推动这一具有挑战性的方向获得更多研究关注。

Abstract: Human assistance in robotics spans around several tasks such as navigation, object manipulation, and placement, where a key challenge is selecting target destinations that align with human intentions or preferences. We focus on this challenge in the context of Virtual Placement (VP), the task of identifying all plausible target locations given scene context and human-centric constraints. This differs from traditional placement tasks that typically focus on a single, predefined target location. The VP problem is complex, as it requires both global and local reasoning about the scene's geometry, semantics, and plausibility. To address this gap, we introduce {\bf Assistant Placement Aria}, the first benchmark to explore diverse aspects of VP, including global, local, and human-centric constraints. It contains both synthetic and real indoor scenes annotated for three tasks: (i)~2D Panel Placement, (ii)~Sitting Suggestion, and (iii)~TV Placement. Each scene includes 2D images, a 3D point cloud, and a textual description of the objects within the scene. By contributing this benchmark, we aim to encourage further research in this underexplored and challenging field that is critically dependent on relevant data. We also evaluate several foundation models for object detection and segmentation on our benchmark.

</details>


### [305] [Staged Multi-Agent Training (SMAT) for Hip Exoskeletons: Metabolic and Biomechanical Validation of a Simulation-Trained Co-Adaptive Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00715)
*Yifei Yuan,Jakob Wolf,Ghaith Androwis,Xianlian Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对基于物理仿真的四阶段多智能体训练（SMAT）策略进行生理学验证，在髋部外骨骼上对8名健康成年人测试，主动辅助相比被动模式降低净代谢率19.7%（p<0.001），且策略在走路速度和地形上表现出泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的控制器可以在纯物理仿真中训练外骨骼辅助策略，但很少有考虑人机协同适应的控制器通过全身代谢测量在真实用户上得到验证。协同适应是挑战性的，因为设备改变关节动力学后，穿戴者会重组神经肌肉协调，产生非平稳学习问题。

Method: 采用SMAT（分阶段多智能体训练）四阶段课程，逐步训练骨骼肌肉人体智能体和双侧髋部外骨骼智能体。策略部署在髋部外骨骼上，使用间接测热法在无外骨骼、被动和主动三种条件下测量8名健康成年人的代谢成本。

Result: 主动辅助相比被动设备降低净代谢率19.7%（p<0.001）；生物力学分析确认所有受试者髋部机械功率主要为正向（正功率比为0.98）；策略能泛化到不同走路速度和地形。

Conclusion: 单一仿真训练的SMAT策略无需针对受试者重新训练，即可在真实用户上带来显著的代谢收益，且在训练条件之外仍保持鲁棒性。
