Abstract: Learning-based controllers can deliver exoskeleton assistance after training entirely in physics-based simulation, yet few controllers that address human-device co-adaptation have been validated on real users by whole-body metabolic measurement, the standard benchmark for assistive walking. Co-adaptation is challenging: as the device alters joint dynamics, the wearer reorganizes neuromuscular coordination, producing a non-stationary learning problem. Staged Multi-Agent Training (SMAT), a four-stage curriculum that progressively trains a musculoskeletal human actor and a bilateral hip exoskeleton actor, was introduced and shown to reduce simulated hip-muscle activation and provide positive assistance on hardware. This article provides the first physiological validation of SMAT. The policy was deployed on a hip exoskeleton and tested with eight healthy adults, with metabolic cost measured by indirect calorimetry across no-exoskeleton, passive, and active conditions. Active assistance lowered net metabolic rate by 19.7% relative to the passive device (p < 0.001). Biomechanical analysis confirmed predominantly positive hip mechanical power across all subjects (positive-power ratio 0.98), and the policy generalized across walking speeds and terrains. Together, these results show that a single simulation-trained SMAT policy, deployed without subject-specific retraining, delivers a significant metabolic benefit on real users while remaining robust beyond the conditions it was trained on.

</details>


### [306] [OmniAI: A Surface-Adaptive Aerial Projection Interface for Human--Drone Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00721)
*Nikita Kuzmin,Yuhua Jin,Georgii Demianchuk,Mariya Lezina,Fawad Mehboob,Ivan Valuev,Nikolai Lutsenko,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍OmniAI，一种具身空中智能体，通过伺服驱动的MEMS激光投影仪在机载屏幕和环境表面之间切换投影，实现表面自适应的空间交互界面。系统利用RGB-D感知和RANSAC平面拟合实时检测投影表面，并结合语音与空中手势控制，构建面向人机交互的移动空间AR界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在人类环境中缺乏具备空间锚定的交互界面，现有无人机难以在物理环境中进行情境化、直观的沟通与信息呈现。

Method: 采用伺服驱动的MEMS激光投影仪，在机载屏幕与邻近环境表面之间动态切换投影；通过RGB-D传感和RANSAC平面拟合在线检测投影表面（无需预建地图）；使用Web增强的LLM管道生成文本和图像响应；同时支持语音和空中手势两种等效控制方式。

Result: OmniAI成功将语音、空中手势、自适应投影与空中移动性相结合，展示了移动空间AR界面在人机交互中的可行性，支持上下文感知的人机交互。

Conclusion: OmniAI验证了通过自适应投影与多模态交互，无人机可以在环境中实现具有空间锚定的情境化通信，为未来空间计算与无人机交互提供了新方向。

Abstract: Drones in human environments often lack spatially grounded in- terfaces for situated communication. We present OmniAI, an em- bodied aerial agent that supports surface-adaptive interaction by switching projection between an onboard screen and nearby en- vironmental surfaces. A servo-actuated MEMS laser projector renders text-and-image responses from a web-augmented LLM pipeline. Projection surfaces are detected online using RGB-D sensing and RANSAC plane fitting, without pre-mapped geometry. OmniAI provides functionally equivalent voice and gesture con- trol for both drone motion and projected content. By combining speech, mid-air gestures, adaptive projection, and aerial mobility, OmniAI demonstrates a mobile spatial AR interface for context- aware human-drone interaction.

</details>


### [307] [SelfWAM: A Self-Grounded Unified World Action Model for Fast Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00725)
*Bikang Pan,Fan Liu,Haotao Lu,Jingya Wang,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SelfWAM是一个统一的自接地世界动作模型，基于模态特化的混合Transformer架构，联合预测动作、动作条件化的未来RGB帧和机器人自遮挡掩码，通过干净动作条件化和未来自遮挡监督，使未来预测更直接反映执行动作对机器人可见运动及周围场景的影响，在RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作任务中提升了策略性能并保持快速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在预测未来时仅依赖任务提示和观测上下文，容易捕捉到通用任务进展而非动作特有的后果，这限制了模型对动作因果效应的学习能力。

Method: 提出SelfWAM，采用模态特化的Mixture-of-Transformers架构，联合预测动作、动作条件化未来RGB帧和机器人自遮挡掩码。在联合训练中，未来视觉查询可关注演示动作的干净副本，将视频分支转变为动作特异的后果模型；同时使用针对机器人自遮挡掩码预测的提示特定目标函数，去除外观细节，聚焦动作相关的视觉变化。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，SelfWAM产生的未来预测对动作更敏感，保持快速策略推理，并提升了策略性能。

Conclusion: 干净动作条件化和未来自遮挡监督的结合使未来预测更准确地反映执行动作带来的机器人可见运动和环境变化，从而改善了机器人策略学习效果。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by jointly modeling actions and future observations. However, conditioning future prediction only on the task prompt and observation context risks capturing generic task progression rather than the action-specific consequences of the executed action. We introduce SelfWAM, a unified self-grounded WAM built on a modality-specialized Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture that jointly predicts actions, action-conditioned future RGB frames, and robot self-masks, thereby grounding future prediction in the robot's visible body and its action-induced motion. During joint training, SelfWAM allows future visual queries to attend to a clean copy of the demonstrated action, turning the video branch into an action-specific consequence model while leaving the fast action-only inference path unchanged. To focus video learning on action-relevant visual changes, we use prompt-specific objectives for future robot self-mask prediction, which removes appearance details and provides a target whose temporal evolution is tightly coupled with the conditioning action. Together, clean-action conditioning and future self-mask supervision make future predictions more directly reflect how the executed action changes the robot's visible motion and the surrounding scene. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-world manipulation tasks show that SelfWAM produces more action-sensitive futures and preserves fast policy inference, while improving policy performance.

</details>


### [308] [Push-Wiper: Toward General-Purpose Robotic Cleaning across Varied Stains and Surfaces with Segmented Pushing Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00730)
*Renhao Lu,Mingxin Wang,Chenyang Cao,Yang Yang,Guoping Pan,Kangkang Dong,Yi Cheng,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Push-Wiper框架，将高粘度污渍的机器人表面清洁重新定义为聚合问题：通过海绵分段推挤轨迹逐步聚拢污渍，再通过后处理分离聚集物并实现海绵自清洁。采用Diffusion Policy生成自适应推挤动作序列，结合任意表面姿态插值器和混合力位控制器进行执行，清洁评分比基线方法高最多130%，且在无需额外训练的情况下可零样本迁移至固体残渣、液体溢出、未见粘性污渍及不同曲率曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高粘度污渍因其复杂流变特性是机器人表面清洁中的重大挑战：传统擦拭会扩散污渍，而刷洗虽能提供更强摩擦力却有损伤表面的风险。

Method: 1) 将粘性污渍清洁重构为聚合问题，采用海绵通过分段推挤轨迹逐步聚集污渍；2) 后处理阶段分离聚集物并实现海绵自清洁；3) 使用Diffusion Policy生成自适应推挤动作序列；4) 通过任意表面姿态插值器（ASPI）和混合力位控制器执行动作序列。

Result: 清洁评分（污渍去除面积百分比）比基线方法最多高130%；在无需额外训练的情况下可零样本迁移至固体残渣、液体溢出、未见粘性污渍和不同几何形状的曲面。

Conclusion: 实验证明了Push-Wiper在清洁有效性上的优势及其强大的泛化能力，能够处理多种污渍类型和复杂表面几何形状。

Abstract: Viscous stains, characterized by high viscosity and complex rheological properties, remain a major challenge for robotic surface cleaning. Conventional wiping often spreads the stain, while scrubbing provides stronger friction but risks damaging the surface. In this paper, we propose Push-Wiper, a framework that reformulates viscous stain cleaning as an aggregation problem. Push-Wiper employs a sponge to progressively gather stains through segmented pushing trajectories, followed by a post-processing phase that detaches the aggregated material and enables sponge self-cleaning. We adopt a stepwise strategy for stain gathering and leverage Diffusion Policy to generate adaptive pushing action sequences. These sequences are executed through our Arbitrary Surface Pose Interpolator (ASPI) and a hybrid force-position controller, allowing the method to generalize to stains with diverse spatial distributions. Push-Wiper achieves a cleaning score (CS), defined as the percentage of stain area removed, up to 130% higher than baseline methods. Without additional training, Push-Wiper also transfers in a zero-shot manner to solid residues, liquid spills, unseen viscous stains, and curved surfaces with varying geometries. Our experiments demonstrate the cleaning effectiveness of Push-Wiper and its strong generalization ability. The project website is available at https://push-wiper.github.io/.

</details>


### [309] [When Prompts Control Robots: Prompt Injection Attacks in Multi-Agent Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00747)
*Neha Nagaraja,Amisha Bagari,Hayretdin Bahsi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了基于LLM的多智能体机器人系统中的提示注入攻击，涵盖直接注入和间接注入两种方式，实验表明攻击可降低任务完成率并诱导对抗行为，且攻击可通过共享提示结构在智能体间传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型被越来越多地集成到自主机器人系统中用于任务规划和控制，但这种集成使其暴露于提示注入攻击之下，可能导致不安全决策和物理伤害。多智能体设置通过跨智能体污染和更广泛的攻击面进一步增加了风险，但目前缺乏对这一问题的系统性研究。

Method: 作者在基于LLM的多智能体机器人系统中评估提示注入攻击，考虑两种攻击途径：直接注入（攻击任务指令）和间接注入（通过感知模块）。实验涵盖不同的攻击目标复杂度和注入策略，并在单智能体与多智能体两种设置下进行对比，同时分析架构变化对LLM查询及攻击成功率的影响。

Result: 实验表明：提示注入能够诱导对抗行为并降低任务完成率；攻击可以通过共享提示结构从一个智能体传播到另一个智能体；攻击影响取决于提示组成方式和目标智能体；架构变化会影响攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性地调查多智能体LLM机器人系统中提示注入攻击的研究，揭示了多智能体环境中攻击传播的机制，为构建更安全的LLM驱动机器人系统提供了安全评估基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly integrated into autonomous robotic systems for task planning and control, but this integration exposes them to prompt injection attacks that can lead to unsafe decisions and physical harm. Multi-agent settings increase the risks through cross-agent contamination and broader attack surfaces. In this paper, we evaluate prompt injection attacks against an LLM-based multi-agent robotic system, considering both direct injections into task instructions and indirect injections through perception modules. In our experiments across varying attack-goal complexities and injection strategies in both single-agent and multi-agent settings, we show that prompt injection can induce adversarial actions while reducing task completion. We find that attacks can propagate from one agent to others through shared prompt structures, with impacts varying depending on prompt composition and the targeted agent. We further analyze how architectural changes affect LLM queries and, consequently, the attack success. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study that systematically investigates prompt injection attacks in a multi-agent LLM-based robotic system.

</details>


### [310] [ORCESTRA: VLM-driven Visual Robot programming in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00775)
*Ivan Snegirev,Elizaveta Semenyakina,Mikhail Konenkov,Artem Lykov,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ORCESTRA是一个混合现实系统，通过无代码路径点教学和语言引导控制来编程机器人数字孪生，支持异构机器人形态，并将MR验证作为物理部署前的安全层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人编程需要专业知识且缺乏直观性，语言引导的机器人控制虽然自然但由于缺乏物理环境感知和验证机制而存在安全隐患。作者希望弥合这一差距，提供一条在物理部署前进行安全验证的编程路径。

Method: 系统在透视式混合现实工作区中运行，用户可以将机器人数字孪生放置在真实表面上，通过无代码方式教学轨迹和保存机器人相对位姿的回合，或通过语音/文本输入指令，由视觉-语言模型转换为结构化数字孪生计划。两种交互模式共享后端，实现度量空间锚定、具身感知验证、预览、确认和数字孪生执行。

Result: 系统支持包括固定基座机械臂、移动基座和仿人机器人在内的异构机器人形态，证明了MR验证作为语言引导机器人编程的安全层在物理部署前是有效的。

Conclusion: ORCESTRA展示了混合现实环境作为语言引导机器人编程安全验证层的可行性，结合无代码教学与语言控制的统一框架，为异构机器人平台的安全部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: ORCESTRA is a mixed-reality system for programming robot digital twins through no-code waypoint teaching and language-guided control. In a passthrough mixed-reality workspace, users place robot twins on real surfaces, teach trajectories, save robot-relative episodes, or issue spoken/typed commands that a vision-language model converts into structured digital-twin plans. Both interaction modes share a backend for metric grounding, embodiment-aware validation, preview, confirmation, and digital-twin execution. The system supports heterogeneous robot embodiments, including fixed-base manipulators, a mobile base, and a humanoid robot, demonstrating MR validation as a safety layer for language-guided robot programming before physical deployment.

</details>


### [311] [SIPTraj: Map-Free End-to-End Trajectory Prediction via Physics-Guided Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00779)
*Feifei Liu,Zejun Wei,Haozhe Wang,Yazhi Ye,Yuying Zhang,Jintao Cheng,Chi Man Vong,Xieyuanli Chen,Xiaoyu Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SIPTraj，一种无需高精地图的轨迹预测框架，通过层次化智能体-场景编码器（HASE）渐进式地将智能体嵌入场景上下文，并利用物理引导迭代解码器（PGID）将运动学约束注入内部表示，在nuScenes和Argoverse 2 Sensor数据集上超越现有无地图预测器及强地图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无地图轨迹预测方法存在两大挑战：一是缺乏高精地图时，传感器BEV特征无法提供明确的车道拓扑或可行驶区域先验，智能体与场景上下文的关联难以建立；二是物理可行性（运动学约束）难以通过纯数据驱动学习捕获，现有方法仅将物理约束作为输出层惩罚，未真正融入模型内部。两者共同制约了预测的安全性与可靠性。

Method: SIPTraj包含两个核心模块：（1）Hierarchical Agent-Scene Encoder（HASE），通过渐进式层次化机制，以智能体为导向逐步提取场景证据并以场景接地空间细化智能体间关系；（2）Physics-Guided Iterative Decoder（PGID），将瞬时运动学状态作为解码条件，将物理监督信号传播至模型内部表示而非仅作用于输出轨迹，从而显式保证预测轨迹的物理可行性。

Result: 在nuScenes和Argoverse 2 Sensor两个大规模自动驾驶数据集上的广泛实验表明，SIPTraj在所有评价指标上超越现有无地图预测器，并且在不使用任何高精地图的情况下还优于强大的基于地图的基线方法。

Conclusion: SIPTraj通过层次化场景接地和物理引导解码，有效解决了无地图轨迹预测中场景关联和物理可行性两大难题，验证了在无高精地图条件下实现高性能轨迹预测的可行性，并为未来研究提供了开源代码。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction of surrounding agents is a prerequisite for safe planning and decision making in autonomous driving. Without high-definition (HD) maps, sensor-derived bird's-eye-view (BEV) features provide no explicit lane topology or drivable-area priors, making it inherently difficult to ground each agent in its surrounding scene context. Moreover, physical feasibility remains difficult to capture through data-driven learning alone, as kinematic constraints on agent motion cannot be explicitly encoded without structured supervision. Existing map-free predictors extract scene context in an agent-agnostic manner through a single fusion step and treat physical constraints only as output-level penalties, leaving both challenges unaddressed. We propose SIPTraj, a map-free trajectory prediction framework that jointly addresses scene grounding and physical feasibility. SIPTraj introduces a Hierarchical Agent-Scene Encoder (HASE) progressively grounding each agent in agent-guided scene evidence and refining inter-agent relations within the scene-grounded space. To tackle physical infeasibility in predicted trajectories, we develop a Physics-Guided Iterative Decoder (PGID). It conditions decoding on instantaneous kinematic states, propagating physical supervision into internal representations rather than output trajectories alone. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and Argoverse 2 Sensor show that SIPTraj surpasses prior map-free predictors and strong map-based baselines without any HD map at inference. Our code will be released as open-source.

</details>


### [312] [StochSIPP: Safe Interval Path Planning in Stochastic Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00792)
*Ajith Kemisetti,Shahaf S. Shperberg,Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StochSIPP是一个在时间依赖且不确定的道路网络中，考虑局部观测的精确应急规划器。它通过SIPP生成安全宏动作，结合有界AND/OR搜索选择动作，在不确定性条件下保证无碰撞并最小化预期到达时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间依赖且不确定的路径阻塞环境下安全导航，需要在运动前预测观测结果。现有保守的固定路径规划器在门控场景下无法给出可行计划，需要一种能在执行过程中根据局部观测自适应决策的规划方法。

Method: StochSIPP使用SIPP生成经过认证的安全宏动作（终止于下一次观测或目标），在缓存的\"动作-观测\"图上进行有界AND/OR搜索以选择每个可达观测结果对应的动作。通过乐观和稳健的SIPP松弛提供可采纳的上下界来约束搜索。

Result: 在受控道路网络实例中，StochSIPP在保持安全固定路径基线成功率的同时减少了到达时间，并能求解保守固定路径规划器无法求解的门控场景。可扩展性研究表明，随着同时观测的不确定状态数量的增加，计算复杂度快速增长。

Conclusion: 当满足确定性安全区间真实安全、感知精确且执行符合预期时序等条件时，StochSIPP生成的策略可证明无碰撞；在概率设置正确且动作与结果生成完整的前提下，能在给定道路网络和时域内最小化预期到达时间。

Abstract: Safe navigation under uncertain time-dependent blockage requires anticipating observations before committing to motion. We present StochSIPP, an exact contingent planner for temporal roadmaps with uncertain edge and vertex statuses revealed locally during execution. StochSIPP uses SIPP to generate certified-safe macro-actions that terminate at the next observation or the goal, and bounded AND/OR search over a cached action--observation graph to select actions for every reachable observation outcome. Optimistic and robust SIPP relaxations provide admissible lower and upper bounds for bounded AND/OR search. When every interval declared deterministically safe is truly safe, sensing is exact, and execution follows the planned timing, the resulting policy is provably collision-free. With correct independent probabilities and complete action and outcome generation, it minimizes expected arrival time within the roadmap and horizon. Experiments on controlled roadmap instances show that StochSIPP preserves the observed success of safe fixed-path baselines while reducing arrival time, and solves gated scenarios in which conservative fixed-path planners return no plan. A scalability study further reveals rapid growth as the number of simultaneously observed uncertain statuses increases.

</details>


### [313] [DynamicWAM: Dual-Path Motion Conditioning for World-Action Models in Dynamic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00793)
*Yunfan Lou,Hewen Gao,Xiyu Zhu,Zhuoran Qiao,Xuan Han,Yifan Yang,Yifan Ye,Boxian Yao,Zhibo Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynamicWAM是一种面向动态物体操纵的紧凑型世界-动作模型，通过双路径运动条件化（历史光流条件化+运动学描述符注入）提升动态场景中的运动感知与响应控制能力，在DOMINO基准和12项真实世界任务中均超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）仅基于当前帧进行条件化，且采用大型骨干网络同步执行，导致在动态场景中缺乏运动感知能力，响应控制不及时，难以有效处理动态物体操纵任务。

Method: 1) 历史流条件化：利用冻结的预训练视频VAE编码时间对齐的光流帧与当前观测，保留空间运动结构；2) 运动学描述符注入：将位移、持续时间、速度、加速度等运动学信息注入动作专家，提供运动幅度和时序；3) 通过联合世界-动作注意力融合两条互补路径；4) 使用蒸馏紧凑骨干网络和基于实时分块（RTC）的异步执行实现响应控制。

Result: 在DOMINO上实现38.2%的成功率和53.2的操纵分数，超过所有评估基线；在涵盖直线、圆形和复合目标运动的12项真实世界任务中，平均成功率达46.7%，比最强基线高出22.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 双路径运动条件化策略能够有效提升动态物体操纵中的运动感知与响应性能，紧凑骨干与异步执行机制在不牺牲效果的前提下实现了更快的响应控制，验证了该方法的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Dynamic manipulation requires robots to infer target motion and respond promptly, yet existing World-Action Models (WAMs) typically condition only on the current frame and execute large backbones synchronously, limiting motion awareness and responsive control in dynamic scenes. We propose DynamicWAM, a compact WAM for dynamic object manipulation with dual-path motion conditioning. DynamicWAM introduces history-flow conditioning, encoding temporally aligned optical-flow frames alongside the current observation through a frozen pretrained video VAE to preserve spatial motion structure, while injecting kinematic descriptors of displacement, duration, velocity, and acceleration into the action expert to provide motion magnitude and timing. The two complementary paths are fused through joint world-action attention. A distilled compact backbone and real-time chunking (RTC)-based asynchronous execution further enable responsive control. On DOMINO, DynamicWAM achieves a 38.2% success rate and a 53.2 manipulation score, outperforming all evaluated baselines. Across 12 real-world tasks spanning linear, circular, and compound target motion, it achieves a 46.7% average success rate, exceeding the strongest baseline by 22.9 percentage points.

</details>


### [314] [LooperMuscle: Fast and Stable Learning of Humanoid Whole-Body Tracking via Structured Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00820)
*Boyi Liu,Qijin Li,Tianqi Yu,Qinrui Yan,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LooperMuscle，一种组合专家策略学习框架，旨在缩小FastSAC类方法在人形全身跟踪任务中相对PPO的性能差距，同时保持高训练效率。通过语义结构化的专家混合Actor、专家感知分布Critic以及贡献路由回放与延迟课程调度三者形成闭环训练，在约45分钟内恢复了FastSAC（15分钟）与PPO（6小时）之间的大部分性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FastSAC类方法虽能显著缩短人形运动训练时间，但在全身跟踪任务中相比PPO存在明显的性能退化。作者希望同时兼顾训练速度与跟踪质量，弥补这一速度-性能差距。

Method: 提出LooperMuscle框架，包含三个关键组件：1）语义结构化的混合专家Actor；2）专家感知的分布Critic；3）贡献路由回放与延迟课程调度。这三个组件形成一个闭环训练循环：专家贡献指导数据路由，路由数据塑造价值学习，价值梯度反向细化专家分工。

Result: 在人形运动跟踪任务中，LooperMuscle显著优于vanilla FastSAC的跟踪精度；相比FastSAC约15分钟训练但性能不足、PPO约6小时训练但性能更强，LooperMuscle仅需约45分钟仿真训练即可恢复通往PPO的大部分性能差距。

Conclusion: LooperMuscle在保持FastSAC高效训练特性的同时，有效恢复了接近PPO的跟踪质量，为快速策略迭代提供了实用高效的解决方案。

Abstract: FastSAC-style methods significantly reduce humanoid motion training time but often suffer from notable performance degradation compared with PPO in whole-body tracking tasks. We target this speed-performance gap by introducing LooperMuscle, a composed expert policy learning framework that restores tracking quality while preserving high training efficiency. LooperMuscle combines a semantically structured mixture-of-experts actor, an expert-aware distributional critic, and contribution-routed replay with deferred curriculum scheduling. These three components form a closed training loop in which expert contributions guide data routing, routed data shape value learning, and value gradients in turn refine expert specialization. Empirically, our approach substantially outperforms vanilla FastSAC in motion tracking accuracy while requiring far less wall-clock time than PPO: where FastSAC trains in about 15 minutes but underperforms, and PPO achieves stronger results but requires about 6 hours, LooperMuscle recovers a substantial fraction of the remaining gap to PPO in roughly 45 minutes of simulation training, delivering practical efficiency for rapid policy iteration. The code will be released to benefit the research community at https://loopermuscle.github.io/.

</details>


### [315] [RIT*: Riemannian Informed Trees for Cost-Adaptive Optimal Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00822)
*Muhayy Ud Din,Ahmed Nadar,Jan Rosell,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Riemannian Informed Trees (RIT*)，一种将批量知情搜索中的欧几里得基础操作替换为黎曼对应操作的运动规划框架，并引入碰撞自适应度量细化（CARM）在线学习障碍物邻近成本场，在2-D到14-D环境中显著降低高维各向异性空间中的路径成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于欧几里得度量的知情搜索规划器（如BIT*）在高维配置空间和各向异性度量环境中表现不佳，成本函数随空间变化时无法准确引导搜索，导致生成路径质量下降。需要一种能够适应黎曼几何度量的规划框架，以在度量随空间变化的场景中获得更低成本的路径。

Method: RIT*框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 构建更紧凑、成本一致的知情集（informed set）；(2) 在各向异性距离度量下执行最近邻搜索；(3) 通过级联方案高效评估边成本。此外，提出碰撞自适应度量细化（CARM），从碰撞反馈中在线学习障碍物邻近成本场，减少对先验度量设计的依赖。

Result: 在2-D到14-D的多类环境中验证，RIT*在低维和空间恒定度量场景下与现有方法具有竞争力；在空间变化度量的高维配置空间中显著降低路径成本。性能提升随各向异性和维度增加：3-D各向异性基准中位初始成本较BIT*提升13.0%，6-DOF操作中位最终成本提升9.0%，14-DOF双臂规划中提升24.8-63.5%，而欧几里得知情基线方法在该场景下性能退化。

Conclusion: RIT*证明了将黎曼几何引入知情搜索规划框架的有效性，尤其在度量随空间变化的高维配置空间中，能够显著替代欧几里得基础方法，生成更低成本的路径，且CARM的在线学习机制增强了框架在实际应用中的实用性。

Abstract: We present Riemannian Informed Trees (RIT*), a planning framework that replaces Euclidean primitives in batch-informed search with their Riemannian counterparts. RIT* constructs a tighter, cost-consistent informed set, performs a nearest-neighbour search under an anisotropic distance metric, and evaluates edge costs efficiently via a cascading scheme. We further introduce a Collision-Adaptive Metric Refinement (CARM), which learns an obstacle-proximity cost field online from collision feedback, reducing the reliance on prior metric design in practical settings. Experiments across environments from 2-D to 14-D show that RIT* is competitive in low-dimensional and spatially constant-metric settings and produces substantially lower-cost solutions when the metric varies spatially in high-dimensional configuration spaces. Performance gains scale with anisotropy and dimension, reaching up to 13.0% improvement in median initial cost over BIT* in the 3-D anisotropic benchmark, up to 9.0% in median final cost over BIT* in 6-DOF manipulation, and 24.8-63.5% in a 14-DOF bimanual planning problem, where Euclidean-informed baselines degrade. Videos and code can be found here: https://muhayyuddin.github.io/ritstar/

</details>


### [316] [GeminiPainter's sequence-formed pipeline comprised of perception, cognition, planning, and action stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00829)
*Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Aleksey Fedoseev,Iana Zhura,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自主机器人肖像生成系统，结合实时人脸检测、AI素描生成（Gemini Vision API）和六自由度协作机械臂绘制，实现从感知到行动的全自动肖像创作流程，用户评分在质量、执行和体验维度均达到较高水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个完整的感知-认知-行动机器人肖像绘制系统，融合计算机视觉、神经艺术抽象、运动优化与机器人控制，实现自动化、高质量且具有艺术吸引力的机器人肖像创作。

Method: 系统采用三阶段流水线：(1) 从视频帧中实时检测并提取人脸区域；(2) 利用Gemini Vision API将人脸图像转换为极简单线素描；(3) 通过基于图的路径规划优化笔画顺序，并在6自由度协作机械臂上执行平滑轨迹。

Result: 用户5分制评分：素描质量4.33、感知执行4.53、用户体验4.65，表明系统生成的机器人肖像具有高辨识度、吸引力和参与感。

Conclusion: 该系统的感知-认知-行动流水线有效实现了高质量的自主机器人肖像生成，用户评估验证了其在艺术质量和用户体验方面的优异表现。

Abstract: We present an autonomous robotic portrait-generation system combining real-time face detection, AI-based sketch generation, and robotic drawing. The system captures video frames, extracts facial regions, converts them into minimalist single-line sketches using the Gemini Vision API, optimizes stroke order through graph-based path planning, and executes smooth trajectories on a 6-DoF collaborative manipulator. This perception-cognition-action pipeline integrates computer vision, neural artistic abstraction, motion optimization, and robot control. User ratings on a 5-point scale were high for sketch quality 4.33, perceived execution 4.53, and user experience 4.65, indicating recognizable, appealing, and engaging robotic portraits.

</details>


### [317] [Minute-Scale Training for Microrobot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00854)
*Yinghan Sun,Aoji Zhu,Xiang Ji,Yamei Li,Jiachi Zhao,Yun Wang,Li Zhang,Huijun Gao,Lidong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种学习框架，可在数分钟内训练出有效的微机器人导航策略，通过全向量化模拟器（超过10,000个虚拟血管环境，每秒约190,000个状态转移）和任务塑形正则化（TSR）奖励框架，实现了快速收敛、性能提升及跨场景零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于深度强化学习（DRL）的微机器人导航方法在训练效率和有效性方面关注不足，模型训练需要数小时甚至数天，严重阻碍了快速实际部署和参数优化。

Method: 提出一个学习框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）全向量化模拟器，并行化动力学、LiDAR启发式感知和可行性检查，支持超过10,000个虚拟血管环境，每秒产生约190,000个状态转移；（2）任务塑形正则化（TSR）奖励框架，用于加速收敛并提升最终性能。

Result: 训练时间缩短至10分钟以内；动作变化减少至少33.7%；障碍物间隙增加至少2.1%；支持跨不同微机器人类型和导航场景的零样本部署。

Conclusion: 该框架能够大幅缩短设计循环，加速自主微机器人的实际部署进程，为微机器人导航的快速训练和实际应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Microrobots hold significant potential for various applications, where targeted navigation is a basic requirement. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently emerged as a powerful paradigm for fully autonomous microrobot navigation. Yet, current DRL-based approaches pay limited attention to learning efficiency and effectiveness, requiring hours to days for model training. Consequently, this impedes both rapid practical deployment and parameter optimization. To address these challenges, we present a learning framework that enables effective microrobot navigation policies to be trained within minutes. In the proposed framework, we develop a fully vectorized simulator with more than 10,000 artificial vascular environments, parallelizing dynamics, LiDAR-inspired perception, and feasibility checks across thousands of environments to achieve roughly 190,000 transitions per second. To achieve effectiveness in the fast training, we propose a task-shaping-regularization (TSR) reward framework. The TSR framework accelerates convergence, improves final performance, reduces action variation by at least 33.7%, and increases obstacle clearance by at least 2.1% across all evaluated scenarios. Results show that the proposed learning framework reduces training time to under 10 minutes, while supporting zero-shot deployment across distinct microrobot types and navigation scenarios. Collectively, this framework can substantially shorten the design loop and accelerate the deployment of autonomous microrobots.

</details>


### [318] [Bicycle Acrobatics with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00880)
*Shamel Fahmi,Arianna Ilvonen,Samuel Zapolsky,Yu-Ming Chen,Ravi Boggavarapu,Paul Drews,Ashwin Khadke,Dean Molinaro,Kaiyu Zheng,Alfred Rizzi,Gabriel Nelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用强化学习（RL）使自行车机器人在仿真和硬件上学习并组合多种动态特技动作，包括跳跃、翻转、抬前轮等，并通过编排器实现长时程特技的协调执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自行车机器人速度快且能效高，但其欠驱动和非完整动力学特性使得高度敏捷的杂技动作难以实现。作者希望突破这一限制，赋予轮式机器人此前仅见于腿足平台的高水平敏捷性。

Method: 采用多种强化学习公式（航点跟随、姿态到达、扭转跟踪、引导跟踪和动作模仿），使机器人学习多样的特技技能；并引入编排器（orchestrator），利用状态依赖的触发条件在策略之间切换，实现鲁棒的长时间特技序列。

Result: 在定制自行车机器人UMV上验证：可反复穿越高达1米的多张桌子，连续执行超过15次自主跳跃并跟随航点，处理未见过的多桌配置，连续20多次执行抬跳、跳跃、翻转等动作组合，以及连续10多次自主可转向的抬前轮、侧向跳跃和单轮跳下组合。

Conclusion: 证明强化学习可以使自行车机器人达到此前主要与腿足平台相关的敏捷水平，同时保留轮式运动的速度与能效，为自行车特技建立了基础。

Abstract: Bicycle robots are fast and energy efficient, but their simple mechanical design and their underactuated and non-holonomic dynamics make highly agile maneuvers difficult to achieve. Here, we use Reinforcement Learning (RL) to enable a bicycle robot to learn and compose a diverse repertoire of dynamic acrobatic stunts. Using different RL formulations such as waypoint following, pose reaching, twist tracking, guided tracking, and motion imitation, the robot acquires autonomous single and multi-table forward and lateral jumps, steerable jumps, front flips, kip-ups, kip-downs, driving, wheelies, bunny hops, and three-point turns. To coordinate these behaviors, we introduce an orchestrator that transitions between policies using state-dependent triggers, enabling robust long-horizon acrobatic stunts. We validate the approach on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV), a custom bicycle robot, in simulation and hardware. The robot repeatedly traverses tables up to 1 m high, performs more than 15 consecutive autonomous jumps while following waypoints, handles previously unseen multi-table configurations, executes continuous repertoires of kipups, jumps, flips, kip-downs, over more than 20 consecutive trials, and performs more than 10 consecutive autonomous and steerable repertoires of wheelies, lateral jumps, and single-wheel jump downs. These results demonstrate that RL can endow bicycle robots with levels of agility previously associated primarily with legged platforms while preserving the speed and efficiency of wheeled locomotion, establishing a foundation for bicycle acrobatics.

</details>


### [319] [Stipple: Real-Time Incremental Gaussian Splatting with Visual-Inertial Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00931)
*Kilian Northoff,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Basalt视觉-惯性跟踪系统与Brush（基于Rust的高效GPU无关3DGS实现）的实时同步跟踪与重建方法，通过增量训练策略替代3DGS的繁重预处理和训练步骤，实现在机器人或XR等实时应用场景中的3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽然能高效渲染逼真场景，但其繁重的预处理和训练步骤不适合需要实时重建的机器人或XR应用。这类应用需要即时反馈和新环境的交互能力，而VIO/VI-SLAM系统天然具备实时性，因此与3DGS的集成是一个有价值的探索方向。

Method: 该方法利用基于Basalt的高效视觉-惯性跟踪系统进行实时定位，并结合Brush（一个基于Rust的高效GPU供应商无关的3D Gaussian Splatting实现），提出了一种新颖的增量式训练方法。该方法直接利用视觉-惯性跟踪系统生成的信息，替代3DGS繁重的预处理和训练步骤，并提出了多种实际改进措施来提高训练流水线效率，使其能与跟踪线程并行实时运行。

Result: 实验表明，3DGS的许多繁重预处理和训练步骤可以被更高效的增量训练策略所替代，该方法能够利用SLAM系统生成的直接信息实现实时训练，同时多种实际改进措施进一步提升了训练效率。

Conclusion: 本工作强调了SLAM与3DGS互补特性的价值，证明了将两者结合可以为实时3D重建带来有前景的结果，展示了视觉惯性跟踪系统与3DGS高效集成解决实时应用需求的可行性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides efficient rendering of photo-realistic scenes, but its heavy preprocessing and training steps make it a poor fit for applications that require real-time reconstruction in robotics or XR. This capability is important since it allows immediate feedback and interaction with new environments. Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) and simultaneous localization and mapping (VI-SLAM) systems, on the other hand, specifically target these real-time applications, which makes them a good choice for integration with 3DGS. We propose a new method that tracks and reconstructs simultaneously in real-time by leveraging an efficient visual-inertial tracking system based on Basalt together with a novel incremental method built on top of Brush, an efficient Rust-based GPU-vendor-agnostic implementation of 3D Gaussian Splatting. We show that many of the heavy preprocessing and training steps of 3DGS can be replaced with a more efficient incremental training strategy that has direct access to the information generated by the visual-inertial tracking system. Furthermore, we propose and combine multiple practical improvements to increase the efficiency of the training pipeline and adapt it to run in real-time, parallel to the tracking thread. This work highlights the value of exploiting the complementary nature of SLAM and 3DGS, and how that can lead to promising results for real-time 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [320] [VertiAKD: Adaptive Off-Road Kinodynamics on Vertically Challenging Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00945)
*Tong Xu,Chenhui Pan,Francesco Cancelliere,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VertiAKD，一个统一的跨车辆越野运动学知识迁移与自适应框架，能够在复杂地形上同时实现异构车辆间的知识迁移，仅需一分钟新数据即可显著降低长时预测误差并实现鲁棒的闭环轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨车辆自适应方法通常假设平坦地形，而地形感知的运动学模型又需要平台特定的数据采集和重新训练，难以在异构车辆编队和不断变化的越野地形条件下实现高效泛化。

Method: VertiAKD学习一个共享的移动性表征，联合编码车辆配置、轨迹转移以及局部高程和语义地形特征。针对新车辆在未知地形上的有限数据，通过函数编码器识别最相关的移动性描述符并迁移其知识来初始化地形感知运动学模型，随后利用在线流式观测周期性地精炼模型，无需基于梯度的重新训练。

Result: 在Verti-Bench仿真器和五种物理配置的Verti-4-Wheeler平台上评估，仅用一分钟新轨迹数据和地形特征，VertiAKD相比直接移动性描述符迁移在多种未见车辆配置上将长时预测误差降低最多34.52%，相比竞争基线降低94.43%，并在仿真和物理实验中展示了鲁棒的闭环轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: VertiAKD有效地实现了地形感知的跨车辆知识迁移，为准确的越野运动学建模和可靠的越野导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Off-road mobility requires autonomous mobile robots to generalize across heterogeneous vehicle fleets and continuously changing terrain conditions. Existing cross-vehicle adaptation approaches generally assume flat terrain, while terrain-aware kinodynamic models often require platform-specific data collection and retraining. To this end, we propose VertiAKD, a unified framework for transferring and adapting off-road kinodynamic knowledge across diverse vehicles on geometrically and semantically complex terrain simultaneously. VertiAKD learns a shared mobility representation that jointly encodes vehicle configurations, trajectory transitions, and local elevation and semantic terrain features. Given limited data from a novel vehicle operating on unseen terrain, VertiAKD identifies the most relevant mobility descriptors and transfers their knowledge to initialize a terrain-aware kinodynamic model via function encoders, which is then periodically refined online from streaming observations without gradient-based retraining. We evaluate VertiAKD in the Verti-Bench simulator, built on the Chrono multi-physics engine, and on five physical configurations of the Verti-4-Wheeler platform. With only one minute of new trajectory data and associated terrain features, VertiAKD reduces long-horizon prediction error by up to 34.52% over direct mobility descriptor transfer across diverse unseen vehicle configurations and 94.43% over competing baselines. We further demonstrate robust closed-loop trajectory tracking in both simulation and physical experiments, highlighting the effectiveness of terrain-aware cross-vehicle knowledge transfer for accurate modeling and reliable off-road navigation.

</details>


### [321] [GraRe: Grasp Candidate Re-Ranking for Frozen 6-DoF Grasp Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00946)
*Jibao Yuan,Yuhui Zhao,Yinzhen Lv,Chao Xu,Shun Li,Chenxi Deng,Shaofei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GraRe方法，通过重新排序已冻结的6-DoF抓取检测器生成的候选抓取，解决检测器置信度与抓取质量对齐不佳的问题，在GraspNet-1Billion上最高提升13.60个AP点，并通过真实机器人实验验证了其在杂乱场景中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6-DoF抓取检测器通常依据检测器置信度对抓取候选进行排序，但作者在GraspNet-1Billion上的分析发现，检测器置信度与真实抓取质量往往对齐不佳，导致高质量候选被排序过低而影响实际执行效果。因此，作者提出将候选重排序作为独立的优化任务，在不改变检测器及其候选集合的前提下提升排序质量。

Method: GraRe方法从三个维度估计抓取质量：候选属性（candidate attributes）、壳分层局部几何（shell-stratified local geometry）和对象上下文（object context）。候选属性用于条件化局部几何和对象上下文表示，并通过Transformer融合三种特征类型。最终将预测质量与检测器置信度结合，生成最终的抓取候选排序。

Result: 在GraspNet-1Billion数据集上使用三个冻结的检测器进行实验，均获得一致性的性能提升，平均AP最高提升13.60个点。真实机器人实验进一步验证了在杂乱场景中的鲁棒抓取能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，改进候选排序为增强冻结的6-DoF抓取检测器提供了一种实用且有效的途径，无需修改检测器本身即可显著提升抓取性能。

Abstract: Existing 6-DoF grasp detectors typically rank grasp candidates by detector confidence. However, our analysis on GraspNet-1Billion shows that detector confidence is often poorly aligned with grasp quality, causing successful grasp candidates to be ranked too low during execution. Motivated by this observation, we formulate grasp candidate re-ranking as a separate task for frozen detectors, aiming to improve candidate ordering without changing the detector or its grasp candidates. We propose GraRe, which estimates grasp quality from candidate attributes, shell-stratified local geometry, and object context. Candidate attributes condition the local geometric and object-context representations, and a Transformer fuses all three feature types. The predicted quality is combined with detector confidence to produce the final ranking. Experiments on GraspNet-1Billion with three frozen detectors show consistent improvements, with gains of up to 13.60 points in Average AP. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate robust grasping in cluttered scenes. These results show that improving candidate ranking provides a practical way to enhance frozen 6-DoF grasp detectors.

</details>


### [322] [FreqNav: Stage-Wise Frequency Routing for Object-Oriented Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00970)
*Yin Tang,Jiawei Ma,Jiahao Li,Hao Zhang,Zhemin Sun,Jianqiao Sun,Deyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FreqNav，一种面向目标导向的空中视觉语言导航（VLN）的轻量级频率路由自适应感知框架。它根据导航阶段动态地在频率分量之间重新分配视觉token——早期探索侧重低频空间布局，后期着陆侧重高频目标细节——通过频率token路由器和相位依赖接地模块实现，并在固定计算预算下实现约3倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLN方法在所有导航阶段使用相同的视觉token建模不断变化的感知需求，导致无关物体和背景杂波的干扰。早期探索需要低频空间布局信息，后期需要高频目标细节，但现有方法无法动态调整感知优先级。

Method: 将长时空中导航形式化为从空间结构到局部细节的频率偏好转移，提出FreqNav框架。包含三个核心组件：1）Frequency Token Router根据当前导航阶段从双视角观测中选择阶段相关的视觉表示；2）Phase-dependent Grounding Module通过显式监督锚定视觉证据；3）Diffusion Transformer预测平滑轨迹用于连续控制。

Result: 实验表明FreqNav在超越强基线方法的同时实现了约3倍更快的推理速度，真实世界部署进一步验证了其在长时空中自主导航中的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: 通过动态路由不同频率的视觉token以适应导航阶段变化，FreqNav在不增加计算预算的前提下显著提升了目标导向空中导航的性能和推理效率，展示了实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Object-oriented aerial vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires searching for a described target and landing on it precisely, under long-horizon and closed-loop control. Guided by a target-descriptive instruction during navigation, perceptual priorities dynamically evolve: early-stage exploration prioritizes low-frequency spatial layout, and then shifts to high-frequency target details. Existing VLN methods model the varying perceptual requirements across navigation stages with identical visual tokens, leading to interference from irrelevant objects and background clutter. To this end, we therefore formulate long-horizon aerial navigation as a frequencypreference shift from spatial structure to local detail and propose FreqNav, a lightweight frequency-routing adaptive perception framework. Under a fixed computational budget, FreqNav dynamically reallocates visual tokens across frequency components according to the current navigation stage. A Frequency Token Router selects stage-relevant visual representations from dual-view observations, while a Phase-dependent Grounding Module anchors visual evidence through explicit supervision. A Diffusion Transformer then predicts smooth trajectories for continuous control. Experiments show that FreqNav outperforms strong baselines while achieving approximately 3x faster inference. Real-world deployment further demonstrates its effectiveness, efficiency, and practical potential for long-horizon aerial autonomy.

</details>


### [323] [RL Bootstrapping of OpenVLA-OFT for a Novel Robot Embodiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01013)
*Damir Nurtdinov,Alexei Kornaev,Alexander Maloletov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在零示范设定下，将预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略（OpenVLA-OFT）对齐到一种全新的绳驱并联机器人（CDPR）上。作者提出两阶段强化学习方案：先用PPO训练定向运动原语，再用GRPO从PPO检查点继续训练并扩展指令空间。结果显示，在共享方向指令上平均成功率从34.25%提升至53.50%，并初步实现了物体条件指令控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA策略适配通常需要特定实施例（embodiment）的示范数据，但对于形态与大规模机器人数据集中机械臂差异很大的自定义机器人，这一假设过于严格。本文旨在探索一种完全无需实施例特定示范数据的对齐方法。

Method: 不使用监督微调，而是在仿真环境中使用基于强化学习的对齐，奖励由仿真器状态计算稠密几何奖励。训练分两阶段：首先用PPO训练定向运动原语，然后从PPO检查点继续用GRPO训练，扩展指令空间以包含物体条件命令（move to &lt;object&gt;）。

Result: 在四条共享方向指令上，平均保持成功率从PPO后的34.25%提升到PPO→GRPO后的53.50%，其中move left和move backward提升尤为显著。GRPO阶段引入的物体条件指令在8个目标物体上达到39/400=9.75%的严格成功率，定性rollout常显示正确的目标趋近行为。

Conclusion: 该方法完全不使用实施例特定数据集，较之依赖示范数据的OpenVLA和OpenVLA-OFT基线有明显优势。尽管结果尚未建立稳健的操作能力，但为仅依赖强化学习为全新实施例引导出第一个可用的语言条件控制器提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policy to a new robot usually assumes embodiment-specific demonstrations. This assumption is especially restrictive for custom robots whose morphology differs strongly from the manipulators seen in large robot datasets. We study a harder setting: zero-demo embodiment alignment of OpenVLA-OFT on a cable-driven parallel robot (CDPR) with a simple gripper and a previously unseen control interface. Instead of supervised fine-tuning, we use reinforcement learning in simulation with dense geometric rewards computed from simulator state. The training is performed in two stages: a PPO stage for directional motion primitives, followed by GRPO continuation from the PPO checkpoint with an expanded instruction space that includes object-conditioned commands. On the four shared directional instructions, the average held-out success rate improves from 34.25\% after PPO to 53.50\% after PPO$\rightarrow$GRPO, with especially large gains on \texttt{move left} and \texttt{move backward}. In the GRPO stage we additionally introduce \texttt{move to <object>} over eight target objects and obtain 39/400 = 9.75\% strict success, while qualitative rollouts frequently show correct target-directed approach behavior before late-stage instability. Compared with prior OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT results, which rely on demonstration datasets and mostly standard rigid-arm embodiments, our method uses no embodiment-specific dataset at all. The results do not yet establish robust manipulation, but they provide stronger evidence that RL-only bootstrapping can create the first usable language-conditioned controller for a genuinely novel embodiment.

</details>


### [324] [KING: Embodiment-Aware Kinematic Graph Neural Network for Unified Motion Representation of Legged and Wheeled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01015)
*Taku Okawara,Aoki Takanose,Kenji Koide,Shuji Oishi,Masashi Yokozuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出KING，一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的动力学模型，通过将不同机器人形态（轮式和腿式）统一表示为共同图结构，实现了跨形态的里程计估计，并支持通过少量数据（1分钟）快速适应新机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的动力学模型通常在单一形态上训练，难以泛化到新形态。这是因为不同机器人的本体感觉测量（如关节数量、地面接触元素）在含义和结构上存在差异。需要一种能够显式建模机器人形态的统一方法。

Method: 提出KING框架，采用图神经网络构建统一的动力学模型。将轮式和腿式机器人的运动学模型表达为统一图表示，输入仅为机器人形态描述（如URDF文件）和本体感觉数据（编码器和IMU），通过跨形态数据集训练实现统一建模。

Result: 在覆盖多种形态的数据集上训练后，KING在真实环境中实现了高精度的里程计估计，并可通过仅1分钟数据的少样本学习快速适应新机器人形态，无需从头重训。

Conclusion: KING证明了通过统一图表示可以有效解决跨形态动力学模型的泛化问题，为特征缺乏环境中的里程计估计提供了一种通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Kinematic models provide reliable motion constraints for odometry estimation in featureless environments, where exteroceptive sensing degrades and IMU integration drifts. Learning-based kinematic models can achieve more accurate odometry estimation than model-based methods by capturing nonlinear effects; however, most existing learning-based models are trained on a single embodiment and generalize poorly to new embodiments. This generalization is difficult because the meanings and structures of proprioceptive measurements vary across embodiments, including the number of joints and ground-contact elements (e.g., wheels, feet). To address this challenge, we propose KING, a Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based kinematic model that explicitly incorporates robot embodiments by representing them as a common graph. We show that wheel and leg kinematic models can be expressed by a unified representation, enabling a single model for both wheeled and legged robots. Trained on datasets spanning diverse embodiments, KING provides a unified representation of wheeled and legged kinematics and achieves high-accuracy odometry estimation in real environments. KING estimates accurate odometry using only an embodiment description (e.g., a URDF file) and on-board proprioception (encoders and an IMU) and can be adapted to new robot embodiments through few-shot learning with only one minute of data, avoiding retraining from scratch on a new dataset for each robot. The project page is available at: https://smrg-aist.github.io/king_project_page/

</details>


### [325] [Sampling-Based Visibility Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01027)
*Stav Ashur,Avishai Sintov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新的基于采样的任务与运动规划（TAMP）算法VisPRM和VisRRT，专门用于解决具有视场角（field-of-view）约束的可见性设备的规划问题。VisPRM利用环境分层分解和“可见性完整性”概念高效采样具有清晰视线的配置，VisRRT则通过专门的反向运动学求解器在关键时刻“瞥向”目标以快速发现关键配置。实验证明两种算法在成功率和运行时间上均优于RRT、PRM和VIR的改进版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见性设备（如相机、闪光灯、定向天线等）因其独特的视场角特性，使广泛使用的启发式和距离度量失效，传统规划方法无法有效处理这类设备的任务规划问题。

Method: 提出两种采样基于的TAMP算法：VisPRM通过环境分层分解并利用可见性完整性概念高效采样具有清晰视线的配置；VisRRT采用专门设计的反向运动学求解器，在规划过程中适时向目标方向“瞥视”，快速发现关键配置。

Result: 通过在模拟和物理实验中与RRT、PRM和VIR的改进版本比较，VisPRM和VisRRT在成功率和运行时间方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 面向可见性设备的专用采样规划算法能够有效克服传统方法在视场角约束下的不足，为相机、传感器等视觉设备的自主操作提供了更高效的规划方案。

Abstract: Robot Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) algorithms enable autonomous operation by incorporating the specific functions and constraints of end-effector tools, such as grippers or soldering irons, directly into the planning process. In this paper, we explore sampling-based TAMP algorithms specifically designed for a critical subset of devices whose unique properties make traditional planning methods ineffective. Visibility-based instruments, such as exteroceptive sensors, cameras, flashlights and directional antennas, are essential across a vast array of human activities. The unique properties of these devices, and particularly, their field-of-view, render many widely used heuristics and distance metrics less effective. We introduce two new sampling-based algorithms, VisPRM and VisRRT, designed to tackle visibility-based tasks. VisPRM employs a hierarchical decomposition of the environment, leveraging the concept of visibility integrity, to efficiently sample configurations with a clear line-of-sight to the target. A specialized Inverse Kinematics solver enables VisRRT to "glance" in the direction of the target at opportune moments, facilitating the rapid discovery of key configurations. We show that VisPRM and VisRRT achieve a higher success rate and faster runtimes compared to adaptations of RRT, PRM and VIR, through both simulated and physical experiments.

</details>


### [326] [VLAGuard: A Framework for Evaluating and Mitigating Physical Attention Hijacking in Vision-Language-Action Robots within Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01028)
*Dongfu Yin,Jinquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLAGuard框架，用于评估和缓解VLA机器人系统中的策略关键性动作到视觉注意力劫持漏洞，通过VASA压力测试模块和APFT防御机制，显著提升系统在对抗性补丁攻击下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA机器人作为无线传感器网络中的移动边缘节点，面临物理对抗性威胁的严峻挑战，尤其是策略关键性的动作到视觉注意力劫持漏洞尚未得到充分研究和防护。

Method: 提出VLAGuard框架，包含两个核心组件：1）VASA压力测试模块，利用可打印补丁严重干扰机器人的动作条件跨注意力；2）APFT防御机制，通过稳定时空注意力并强制几何一致性来实现防护，且零推理开销。

Result: 在LIBERO模拟实验中，APFT将OpenVLA故障率从100.0%降至25.9%；在2000次真实世界试验中，平均成功率从23.0%提升至67.4%。

Conclusion: 保护注意力通路对提升VLA驱动的传感器网络边缘节点的鲁棒性至关重要，VLAGuard框架有效缓解了物理对抗攻击带来的安全威胁。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) robots as mobile edge nodes within wireless sensor networks (WSNs) requires robust protection against physical adversarial threats. We present VLAGuard, a framework to assess and mitigate a critical vulnerability: policy-critical action-to-vision attention hijacking. We first introduce a stress-test module, Visuomotor Attention-guided Semantic Attack (VASA), using printable patches to severely distract the robot's action-conditioned cross-attention. To counter this, we propose Attention-Protective Fine-Tuning (APFT), a defense that stabilizes spatiotemporal attention and enforces geometric consistency with zero inference overhead. Evaluations across simulated and physical WSN-assisted smart environments demonstrate significant robustness gains. APFT reduces the OpenVLA failure rate from 100.0% to 25.9% in LIBERO simulations. Furthermore, across 2,000 real-world trials, APFT improves the average success rate from 23.0% to 67.4% under severe patch attacks. This highlights that protecting attention pathways is important for improving the robustness of VLA-driven edge nodes in sensor networks.

</details>


### [327] [Diffusion-Based Body Schema Learning Enabling Abnormal-State Adaptation in Musculoskeletal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01029)
*Kento Kawaharazuka,Shuhei Ikemoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的骨骼肌肉机器人身体图式学习框架，通过梯度引导的去噪过程，在无需重新训练的情况下处理肌肉断裂和执行器卡死等异常状态下的传感器与执行器信号估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于自编码器或变分自编码器的方法通过低维潜空间学习平均行为，但仅靠潜空间探索难以处理训练数据中未包含的分布外或异常状态（如肌肉断裂、执行器卡死）。

Method: 提出扩散模型框架，无需低维潜空间，直接在高维空间中通过去噪过程迭代估计物理一致的传感器和执行器值；将身体图式适应形式化为梯度引导的去噪过程，支持部分观测和约束条件下的适应性估计。

Result: 通过骨骼肌肉机器人模型的仿真实验验证了所提框架在肌肉断裂和执行器卡死等异常条件下的有效性，能够自适应估计适当的肌肉长度和肌肉张力。

Conclusion: 扩散模型相比潜空间生成模型在处理骨骼肌肉机器人异常状态下的身体图式学习方面具有优越性，无需重新训练即可适应分布外情况。

Abstract: Musculoskeletal robots require an internal body schema that remains consistent under a wide range of physical state changes, including abnormalities such as muscle rupture and actuator jamming. Conventional approaches based on autoencoders or variational autoencoders learn average behaviors by projecting sensor and actuator signals into a low-dimensional latent space; however, exploration within the latent space alone has limited capability to handle out-of-distribution or abnormal states that are not included in the training data. To address this limitation, this study proposes a diffusion-based framework for body schema learning in musculoskeletal robots. Unlike generative models that operate through low-dimensional latent spaces, diffusion models can directly and iteratively estimate physically consistent sensor and actuator values in the high-dimensional space through a denoising process, even under partial observations and constraints, without requiring retraining. By formulating body schema adaptation as a gradient-guided denoising process, the proposed method enables adaptive estimation of appropriate muscle lengths and muscle tensions even under abnormal conditions such as muscle rupture and actuator jamming. The validity of the proposed framework is verified through simulation experiments using a musculoskeletal robot model.

</details>


### [328] [WAM-Diff2: Hierarchical AR-to-Diffusion Distillation for Highly Efficient Autonomous Driving VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01035)
*Zhihao Zhu,Hanlin Shang,Mingwang Xu,Feipeng Cai,Zhuolin He,Yaoyi Li,Jianhua Han,Hang Xu,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WAM-Diff2，一种多任务离散扩散VLA框架，通过三阶段分层蒸馏策略，将预训练的自回归通用模型转化为并行扩散模型，在保持性能的同时实现最高15.1倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在端到端自动驾驶中受限于高计算延迟和自回归解码带来的暴露偏差；而扩散策略虽然支持低延迟并行执行，但从零训练往往只能得到狭隘的单任务架构，缺乏整体视觉-语言推理能力。将自回归通用模型转化为并行扩散模型可兼顾两者优势，但面临注意力模式不匹配（因果vs双向）和优化目标分歧等架构挑战。

Method: 提出三阶段分层蒸馏策略：(1)渐进式逐块适应（progressive block-wise adaptation），(2)逐块蒸馏（block-wise distillation），(3)跨尺度模型蒸馏（model-wise cross-scale distillation）。通过结构化架构转换保留基础模型的语义基础，同时加速推理。

Result: 在驾驶理解、感知和规划基准上的广泛评估表明，WAM-Diff2有效缓解了暴露偏差，并实现了与自回归基线相当的性能。自回归到扩散的转换带来2.8倍解码加速，结合FlashInfer和CUDA Graphs等系统级优化后，最终实现15.1倍加速。

Conclusion: WAM-Diff2成功弥合了自回归通用模型与扩散执行模型之间的鸿沟，证明通过分层蒸馏策略可以将预训练自回归模型高效转化为并行扩散VLA框架，在保持多任务认知能力的同时获得显著推理效率提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a prominent paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving; however, their efficient deployment is severely constrained by high computational latency and exposure bias arising from sequential autoregressive decoding. Conversely, while specialized diffusion policies enable low-latency, parallel execution, training them from scratch typically yields narrow, single-task architectures that lack holistic visual-linguistic reasoning. Successfully transforming pre-trained autoregressive generalists into parallel diffusion models could combine multi-task cognitive intelligence with execution efficiency, yet this transition presents a formidable architectural challenge due to mismatched attention patterns (causal versus bidirectional) and divergent optimization objectives. To bridge this divide, we introduce WAM-Diff2, a multi-task discrete diffusion VLA framework powered by a three-stage hierarchical distillation strategy. By structuring the architectural shift through progressive block-wise adaptation, block-wise distillation, and model-wise cross-scale distillation, WAM-Diff2 preserves the underlying semantic foundations of the base model while accelerating inference. Extensive evaluations across driving understanding, perception, and planning benchmarks demonstrate that WAM-Diff2 effectively mitigates exposure bias and achieves performance parity with autoregressive baselines. Crucially, the autoregressive-to-diffusion transition yields a 2.8x decoding speedup, which scales to an ultimate 15.1x acceleration when combined with system-level optimizations including FlashInfer and CUDA Graphs.

</details>


### [329] [OC-VLA++: Monocular Geometry-Guided Cross-View Consistency for Viewpoint-Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01066)
*Tianyi Zhang,Ziyang Gong,Zhenjie Yang,Zhe Qian,Haonan Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OC-VLA++ 通过引入几何引导的配对视角监督和显式跨视角动作等变性目标，扩展了 OC-VLA 以解决有限相机覆盖下的视角泛化问题，实验表明在未见视角泛化上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OC-VLA 将机器人动作锚定在相机坐标系中以对齐动作监督与视觉观察，但仅依赖相机空间锚定仍会过拟合训练中见过的少数视角，导致视角泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出两个关键组件：1) 几何引导的配对视角监督，利用同一操作场景在几何相关视角下的配对观测；2) 显式跨视角动作等变性目标，训练模型使不同视角的相机空间预测对应同一机器人坐标系中的动作，显式监督动作预测如何随视角变换。

Result: 在有限相机覆盖下，未见视角的泛化能力获得显著提升，且性能随相机位移增加时下降更为平缓。

Conclusion: 跨视角动作等变性是观察中心动作锚定的有效补充，对真实世界部署的鲁棒性具有重要意义。

Abstract: We propose OC-VLA++, an extension of OC-VLA for viewpoint generalization under limited camera coverage. While OC-VLA grounds robot actions in the camera coordinate system to align action supervision with visual observations, camera-space grounding alone can still overfit to the few viewpoints observed during training. OC-VLA++ addresses this limitation by introducing geometry-guided paired-view supervision and an explicit cross-view action-equivariance objective. Given paired observations of the same manipulation scene from geometrically related viewpoints, the model is trained such that their camera-space predictions correspond to the same robot-frame action. This objective explicitly supervises how action predictions should transform across viewpoints, rather than relying solely on image-level augmentation. Experiments demonstrate substantial improvements in unseen-view generalization under limited camera coverage, with performance degrading more gracefully under increasing camera displacement. These results establish cross-view action equivariance as an effective complement to observation-centric action grounding for robust real-world deployment.

</details>


### [330] [VespaSeg: A Resource-Aware Ground-then-Segment Pipeline for Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01077)
*Savindu Dilshan Wickramasinghe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VespaSeg是一个模块化指代表达分割流水线，使用紧凑的视觉语言模型（Florence-2-base等）进行文本查询定位，配合MobileSAM生成像素级掩码。在RefCOCO验证协议下取得73.64 mIoU，相比Florence-2-large在速度上快1.70倍且显存少1.17 GB，展示了紧凑模块化方案的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指代表达分割需要语言条件定位和像素级掩码，但单片式（monolithic）模型部署成本高昂，需要探索更紧凑、可模块化的替代方案以降低部署负担。

Method: 提出VespaSeg模块化流水线：先用紧凑视觉语言模型（Florence-2-base、Florence-2-large、Moondream2）将文本查询定位为边界框，再用MobileSAM将预测框转换为掩码。对定位和分割阶段进行针对性适配，并研究降低输出token预算等优化策略。

Result: 在包含3,811个记录（每个取首个表达式）的RefCOCO验证协议下，适配后的Florence-2-base流水线取得73.64 mIoU和84.60 precision@0.5。在RTX 6000 Ada上处理22.8查询/秒，平均显存2.20 GB。500查询对比中Florence-2-base（73.73 mIoU）优于Florence-2-large（72.82），且快1.70倍、少用1.17 GB显存。真实框适配将MobileSAM mIoU从82.22提升至86.61，输出token从64降至32不影响精度。

Conclusion: 紧凑模块化的定位与分割方案在精度和效率上具有竞争力，但需在完整标准RefCOCO表达式分割和实际部署硬件上进行更全面的评估。

Abstract: Referring expression segmentation requires language conditioned localization and pixel-accurate masks, but monolithic models can be costly to deploy. We present VespaSeg, a modular pipeline that grounds a text query with a compact vision-language model and converts the predicted box to a mask with MobileSAM. We study Florence-2-base, Florence-2-large, and Moondream2 grounders together with targeted adaptation of the grounding and segmentation stages. Under a repository-specific RefCOCO validation protocol containing the first expression for each of 3,811 referenced-object records, the adapted Florence-2-base pipeline obtains 73.64 mean intersection over union (mIoU) and 84.60 precision at IoU 0.5. On an NVIDIA RTX 6000 Ada GPU it processes 22.8 cached-image queries per second with 2.20 GB mean allocated GPU memory. A matched 500-query comparison gives 73.73 mIoU for Florence-2-base and 72.82 for Florence-2-large, while the base model is 1.70 times faster and uses 1.17 GB less allocated memory. Ablations show that ground-truth-box adaptation raises MobileSAM mIoU from 82.22 to 86.61 and that reducing the Florence-2 output-token budget from 64 to 32 preserves accuracy. These results support compact, modular grounding and segmentation, while also exposing the need for evaluation on the complete standard RefCOCO expression splits and deployment hardware.

</details>


### [331] [Sparse Meets Dense: Correspondence Guided Robotic Manipulation with Rigid-Deformable Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01083)
*Ziyu Zhu,Yue Chen,Xirui Liang,Hojin Bae,Yuran Wang,Zhen Yuan,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向刚体-可变形物体交互操作的混合对应关系表示方法，结合稀疏关键点与稠密对应关系，实现高效且泛化的操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体-可变形物体交互（如挂衣服、给人类穿衣）在日常生活和家庭机器人中十分常见，但由于多触点交互和变形体复杂动力学，传统的单物体操作或刚体交互表示（如6D姿态、结构点）无法提供足够的任务特定信息。

Method: 提出混合对应关系表示：1）引入结构感知、任务感知和交互感知的稀疏关键点，基于刚体和变形体的全局结构生成并依据局部交互接触进行筛选；2）在变形体上构建稠密对应关系用于精确跟踪关键点；3）稀疏关键点编码任务特定信息用于精细操作，稠密对应确保高效跟踪和对新变形、形状及场景的泛化。

Result: 大量实验验证了该方法在刚体-可变形交互任务中的有效性和广泛适用性，并支持通过最少演示实现一对新任务的一次性迁移。

Conclusion: 混合表示结合了稀疏关键点与稠密对应的优势，能够在刚体-可变形交互中实现高效的精细操作与良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Manipulation involving rigid-deformable interactions, such as hanging clothes or dressing humans, is common in daily life, making it essential for household robots. Compared to single-object manipulation or interactions between rigid bodies, these tasks are particularly challenging due to the rich multi-point contacts and the complex dynamics of the deformable bodies during interaction. Therefore, object-centric representations such as 6D poses or structural points without task-specific information become insufficient for these interactions. In this work, we propose a hybrid correspondence-based representation tailored for rigid-deformable interactions. First, to capture intricate interaction information, we introduce structure-, task-, and interaction-aware sparse keypoints. The keypoints are generated based on the global structures of both rigid and deformable objects, and filtered by their local interaction contacts. However, tracking these sparse keypoints through the interaction remains difficult due to the high-dimensional dynamics of deformable objects. Therefore, we further construct dense correspondences on the deformable objects for accurate keypoint tracking throughout the manipulation. This hybrid design combines the advantages of both representations: sparse keypoints encode rich, task-specific information for fine-grained manipulation, while dense correspondences ensure efficient tracking and generalization to novel deformations, shapes, and scenarios. Together, they enable one-shot transfer to new tasks with minimal demonstrations. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and broad applicability of our method.

</details>


### [332] [CAAT: Contact-Aware Attention Scaling and Tactile Masking for Data-Efficient Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01102)
*Jiaming Jiang,Yuzhe Huang,Hao Liang,Pei Lin,Shengcheng Luo,Fanrong Dong,Jiaping Wu,Chenxi Xiao,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CAAT，一个轻量级的接触感知框架，通过注意力缩放和动态触觉掩码显式地引入接触先验：在接触前强调视觉信息，在接触时强调触觉信息，并抑制静态背景token。CAAT可集成到现有Transformer策略中而无需修改动作解码器，在仿真中将ACT成功率提升18个百分点（相对直接融合）和10个百分点（相对门控融合），在真实世界实验中平均成功率60%，比最强基线高出21.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的视觉-触觉策略通常依赖token拼接或可学习门控，缺乏显式的接触感知先验，难以从演示中高效学习跨模态表示。

Method: CAAT通过注意力缩放和动态触觉掩码显式融入接触先验：利用当前触觉观测与无接触参考的比较来抑制静态背景token；在接触前强调视觉信息，接触时强调触觉信息。框架轻量，可集成到常见Transformer策略中且无需改动动作解码器。

Result: 仿真中，集成CAAT的ACT相比直接视觉-触觉融合平均提升18.0个百分点，相比门控融合提升10.0个百分点；真实世界实验中，CAAT在ACT、Diffusion Policy和π0上平均成功率达60.0%，比最强基线平均高出21.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式接触先验和动态触觉掩码能有效提升视觉-触觉策略学习，并适用于多种策略架构，验证了CAAT的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: In contact-rich manipulation, visual observations primarily guide motion in free space, whereas tactile observations become particularly informative during contact. However, standard Transformer-based visuo-tactile policies typically rely on either token concatenation or learnable gating. These approaches lack explicit contact-aware priors, making it difficult to efficiently learn effective cross-modal representations from demonstrations. To address this limitation, we propose CAAT, a lightweight contact-aware framework that explicitly incorporates contact priors through attention scaling and dynamic tactile masking. Specifically, CAAT emphasizes visual information before contact and tactile information during contact. It also suppresses static background tokens by comparing the current tactile observation with a non-contact reference. CAAT can be integrated into commonly used Transformer-based policies without modifying their action decoders. In simulation, integrating CAAT with ACT improves the average success rate by 18.0 percentage points over direct visuo-tactile fusion and by 10.0 percentage points over gated fusion. In real-world experiments using a visuo-tactile UMI platform, CAAT achieves an average success rate of 60.0% across ACT, Diffusion Policy, and $π_0$, outperforming the strongest baseline by an average of 21.1 percentage points. These results demonstrate that explicit contact priors and dynamic tactile masking are effective in improving visuo-tactile policy learning and task performance of diverse policy architectures. https://mrjiangjm.github.io/caat/

</details>


### [333] [FeDepth: Federated Learning for Depth Estimation under Robot Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01129)
*Ganghyeon Lee,Inha Lee,Junhee Lee,Jeongeon Lee,Sung Whan Yoon,Kyungdon Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FeDepth，一种基于描述符的软聚类联邦学习框架，用于解决机器人深度估计中因域偏移导致的性能下降问题，通过允许客户端参与多个聚类来捕捉机器人环境中连续且模糊的域过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人感知方法依赖集中式学习，难以扩展到异构机器人平台；联邦学习虽能实现分布式训练，但在客户端异质性导致的域偏移下性能严重退化。真实机器人部署中数据分布重叠，打破了聚簇联邦学习假设的客户端域可清晰分离的前提。

Method: 提出两个真实且未被探索的非独立同分布（non-IID）场景，反映平台、环境和深度分布方面的异质性；并设计FeDepth框架，通过描述符建模客户端关系，采用软聚类方式让客户端参与多个簇，以捕捉域之间的连续和模糊过渡。

Result: 大量实验表明，FeDepth在多种深度估计架构上始终优于标准联邦学习和聚簇联邦学习基线，显著提升了对域偏移的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FeDepth为联邦机器人感知提供了一种实用且有效的解决方案，填补了聚簇联邦学习在机器人深度估计中面对重叠域分布场景的空白。

Abstract: Although recent robot perception research emphasizes training on data from diverse environments to improve generalization, most existing methods still rely on centralized learning, which is inefficient and difficult to scale across heterogeneous robot platforms. Federated learning (FL) offers an alternative by enabling distributed training without raw data transfer, but it suffers from severe performance degradation under domain shifts caused by heterogeneity across clients. In real robotic deployments, data distributions often overlap across platforms, environments, and sensing conditions, making it difficult to partition clients into clearly separated domains. However, this characteristic breaks the assumption of clearly separable client domains commonly used in clustered FL. To address this gap in robot perception, particularly in depth estimation, we introduce two realistic and unexplored non-IID scenarios that reflect heterogeneity in terms of platform, environment, and depth distribution. We then propose FeDepth, a descriptor-based clustered FL framework that models client relationships through soft clustering. Unlike hard clustering methods that assume clearly separated clusters, FeDepth allows clients to participate in multiple clusters, capturing continuous and ambiguous domain transitions commonly observed in robotic environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FeDepth consistently improves robustness over standard FL and clustered FL baselines across multiple depth estimation architectures, providing a practical and effective solution for federated robot perception. Our project page is available at https://vision3d-lab.github.io/fedepth/.

</details>


### [334] [Complete Motion Planning using Workspace-Fibered Decomposition for nR-Planar Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01172)
*Aayush Rath,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向nR平面冗余机械臂的工作空间-纤维分解框架，通过增量构建低维非冗余子链的障碍约束可达工作空间，并递归地通过冗余方向纤维提升，避免了完整构型空间障碍几何的显式构建，实现了高效的降维空间运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在高维构型空间中进行冗余机械臂的运动规划计算复杂度极高，尤其在杂乱环境中需要处理完整的构型空间障碍几何。现有方法缺乏对可达性结构的精确刻画和早期不可行性检测手段。

Method: 提出工作空间-纤维分解框架：首先建立最小非冗余子链的障碍约束可达工作空间，提供可行性的精确判定；然后通过增量纤维提升过程，利用雅可比行列式连续性约束传播可达工作空间结构，在由递归构建的工作空间-纤维流形上直接进行降维空间规划。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在各提升阶段保持了无碰撞连通性结构，同时相较于直接构型空间推理，显著降低了碰撞检测的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作空间-纤维分解方法为冗余平面机械臂在杂乱环境中的运动规划提供了一种高效且结构保持的替代方案，能够在避免完整高维构型空间构建的同时实现精确的可行性判定和规划。

Abstract: We propose a workspace-fibered decomposition framework for motion planning in nR planar redundant manipulators operating in cluttered environments. Rather than planning directly in the full n-dimensional configuration space, the method incrementally constructs obstacle-constrained reachable workspaces of lower-dimensional non-redundant sub-chains and recursively lifts them through redundant orientation fibers. This yields a sequence of reduced planning manifolds that preserve branch-consistent reachability structure while avoiding explicit construction of the full configuration-space obstacle geometry. We first establish that, for planar position-only manipulators, the obstacle-constrained reachable workspace induced by the minimal non-redundant sub-chain provides an exact characterization of feasibility with respect to the connected component of the start configuration, enabling early infeasibility detection prior to introducing redundant degrees of freedom (DOF). We then introduce an incremental fiber-lifting procedure that propagates reachable workspace structure through successive redundant links while enforcing local inverse-kinematic branch consistency using Jacobian determinant continuity constraints. The resulting representation admits efficient reduced-space planning directly on recursively-constructed workspace-fiber manifolds. Experimental results on redundant nR planar manipulators demonstrate that the proposed construction preserves collision-free connectivity structure across successive lifting stages while substantially reducing collision checking complexity relative to direct configuration space reasoning.

</details>


### [335] [PRISM: Privileged Probabilistic Latent Supervision for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01201)
*Volodymyr Havrylov,Faris Janjoš,Andreas Look,Jürgen Mathes,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现端到端自动驾驶中VLM监督带来的性能提升并非源于VLM的推理能力，而是源于训练过程中建立的潜在连接。基于此，提出了一种概率深度监督框架，直接从GT数据正则化中间潜在表征，在nuScenes数据集上实现规划L2误差降低8%、碰撞率降低3%，且几乎无额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统仅依赖输出级监督，导致深层模型的隐藏层梯度较弱。虽已有工作引入VLM监督潜在特征并取得经验性提升，但其背后的理论机制尚不明确，需要更深入的理解和更高效的监督方法。

Method: 首先通过实验揭示VLM监督性能提升的真正来源是潜在连接而非VLM推理能力。基于此洞察，提出概率深度监督框架：将模型潜在特征视为可重参数化的分布，通过优化证据下界（ELBO）从GT数据直接正则化中间潜在表征，特别是针对轨迹相关的潜在层使用未来GT路径进行监督。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，使用相同的训练数据和E2E架构，相比有竞争力的向量化基线方法，规划L2误差降低8%，碰撞率降低3%，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 从GT数据直接对中间潜在表征进行概率深度监督，是一种比VLM监督更有效且开销更低的替代方案，为端到端自动驾驶的训练范式提供了新思路。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving (E2E AD) systems integrate perception, prediction, and planning into a single differentiable architecture. While these models show great promise, their standard training often relies on output-only supervision, which can lead to weak gradients for the hidden layers of increasingly complex models. Recent works have integrated vision-language model (VLM) supervision for latent features to address this, yielding substantial empirical gains, yet leaving the underlying theoretical mechanisms poorly understood. Our investigation into this methodology reveals that the resulting performance gains stem not from VLM reasoning capabilities, as previously assumed, but rather from the latent connections forged between the E2E AD model and ground-truth (GT) data during training. Building on this insight, we propose a probabilistic deep supervision framework that regularizes intermediate latent representations directly from GT data. By treating model latents as reparameterizable distributions, we optimize the architecture via the Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO). Our evaluations conducted on the nuScenes dataset demonstrate that supervising trajectory-related latents with future GT paths consistently improves planning performance. Using identical training data and E2E architectures, our method achieves an 8% reduction in planning L2 error and a 3% decrease in collision rates compared to competitive vectorized baselines, all while incurring negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [336] [EndoWAM: A Grounded World-Action Model for Generalizable Endoscopic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01221)
*Jinsong Lin,Zikang Pan,Wanhao Liu,Chi Kit Ng,Liangjing Shao,Zihang Yu,Ziyu Wang,Yin Wang,Jiaxi Wang,Jeremy Yuen-Chun Teoh,Zhiyong Xiong,Huxin Gao,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了EndoWAM，据作者所知是首个用于通用机器人内窥镜导航的世界动作模型（WAM）。EndoWAM通过未来接地技术，利用视频世界模型的中间去噪特征预测未来观测中的目标任务区域，并与离散动作专家通过共享预测表征耦合，实现单次去噪过程中的实时控制。同时引入了覆盖三种解剖学不同手术的内窥镜运动数据集EndoMotion，实验表明EndoWAM在零样本泛化到未见视角、环境和目标方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主内窥镜导航可减轻临床医生操作负担，但由于组织变形、瞬时遮挡和快速变化的视角，鲁棒控制仍然具有挑战性。现有基于学习的策略仅从当前观测预测动作，未显式建模未来动态，在安全关键场景中鲁棒性和可靠性受限。虽然世界动作模型（WAM）通过耦合预测性视觉动态与动作生成提供了有前景的替代方案，但将其扩展到机器人内窥镜检查面临训练数据有限、视角多样性受限、解剖结构可变形和推理延迟高等挑战。

Method: EndoWAM引入未来接地（future grounding）技术，从视频世界模型的中间去噪特征中预测未来观测中的任务相关目标区域。具体而言，EndoWAM将轻量级扩散Transformer用于未来目标区域预测，并通过共享预测表征与离散动作专家耦合。这种设计将目标感知监督注入预测动态建模中，提高对视觉退化和视角变化的鲁棒性，同时通过单次去噪实现实时控制。此外，作者构建了EndoMotion数据集，覆盖输尿管镜检查、食管镜检查和内镜逆行胰胆管造影（ERCP）三种解剖学不同的手术。

Result: EndoWAM在所有基线和替代接地策略上持续表现优越，并在未见视角、环境和目标上展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。结果表明EndoWAM是一个预测性、目标接地框架，能够在视觉受限的内窥镜环境中实现准确、通用和长时程导航。

Conclusion: EndoWAM作为一种预测性、目标接地的世界动作模型框架，为视觉受限内窥镜环境中的准确、通用和长时程导航提供了有效解决方案，推动了自主内窥镜导航的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous endoscopic navigation can reduce clinicians' operational burden, yet robust control remains challenging due to tissue deformation, transient occlusions, and rapidly changing viewpoints. Existing learning-based policies typically predict actions from current observations without explicitly modeling future dynamics, limiting their robustness and reliability in safety-critical settings. World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising alternative by coupling predictive visual dynamics with action generation, but extending them to robotic endoscopy remains challenging due to limited training data, restricted viewpoint diversity, deformable anatomy, and high inference latency. We present EndoWAM, which is, to our knowledge, the first WAM for generalizable robotic endoscopic navigation. EndoWAM introduces future grounding, which predicts task-relevant target regions in future observations from intermediate denoising features of a video world model. Specifically, EndoWAM couples a lightweight diffusion transformer for future target-region prediction with a discrete action expert through a shared predictive representation. This design injects target-aware supervision into predictive dynamics modeling, improving robustness to visual degradation and viewpoint changes while enabling real-time control in a single denoising pass. We further introduce EndoMotion, a robotic endoscopic motion dataset spanning three anatomically distinct procedures: ureteroscopy, esophagoscopy, and endoscopic retrograde cholangiopancreatography (ERCP). EndoWAM consistently outperforms all baselines and alternative grounding strategies, while demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization to unseen viewpoints, environments, and targets. These results establish EndoWAM as a predictive, target-grounded framework for accurate, generalizable, and long-horizon navigation in visually constrained endoscopic environments.

</details>


### [337] [Hermite Curves as Trajectory Priors for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01265)
*Qi Lv,Jianming Xing,Zhao Yang,Mingyuan Yao,Yinan Shi,Yawei Jueluo,Mike Zheng Shou,Xiang Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将埃尔米特轨迹先验引入VLA模型，用分段三次埃尔米特曲线参数化动作块以显式保证平滑性和连续性，并通过三种变体验证了该先验作为学习归纳偏置比运行时约束更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型中的动作块结构薄弱，通常将每个块展平为逐时间步控制，依赖隐式数据学习，导致实际执行中出现抖动运动和边界不连续问题。

Method: 将轨迹块参数化为由端点位置和速度定义的分段三次埃尔米特曲线，在离散自回归和连续生成范式中实例化为三种变体：Hermite Tokens（自回归预测量化边界变量）、Hermite Scaffold（分解基础骨架和残差）、Hermite Regularization（仅作为辅助训练目标施加先验）。

Result: Hermite Regularization在三种变体中表现最优，在LIBERO上从95.9%提升至98.7%，在LIBERO-plus上从85.7%提升至90.9%，在四个真实机器人任务上从63.4%提升至90.0%，且无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 轨迹分析表明，显式结构化轨迹先验最适合作为学习归纳偏置而非运行时约束发挥作用。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for robotic manipulation, the action chunk remains a weakly structured interface. Existing work typically flatten each chunk into per-timestep controls, relying on implicit data learning that manifests as jagged motion and boundary discontinuities during physical execution. To address these limitations, we introduce Hermite trajectory priors, parameterizing the chunk trajectory as a piecewise cubic Hermite curve defined by endpoint positions and velocities to explicitly enforce smoothness and continuity. We instantiate this fixed operator across discrete autoregressive and continuous generative paradigms via three variants: (1) Hermite Tokens, which predict quantized boundary variables autoregressively; (2) Hermite Scaffold, which decomposes clean actions into a base scaffold and residuals; and (3) Hermite Regularization, which applies the prior strictly as an auxiliary training objective. Across simulation benchmarks and real-robot platforms, Hermite Regularization achieves superior performance among these three variants, improving π0.5 baseline success rates from 95.9% to 98.7% on LIBERO, 85.7% to 90.9% on LIBERO-plus, and 63.4% to 90.0% across four real-robot tasks without additional inference overhead. Trajectory analyses reveal that explicitly structuring trajectory priors serves most effectively as a learning inductive bias rather than a runtime constraint.

</details>


### [338] [DreamTrajectory: Trajectory-Guided Action Generation with World Model Alignment for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01381)
*Zheng Yang,Wenjie Zhang,Xiangyu Chen,Wenxuan Song,Xianpeng Wang,Yihang Kang,Wen Chen,Lujia Wang,Renjing Xu,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DreamTrajectory，一种轨迹引导的移动操作框架，通过联合预测末端执行器轨迹与全身动作块，并利用轻量级轨迹世界模型进行测试时搜索-预测-评分，显著提升了语言条件下的移动操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Vision-Language-Action (VLA)策略在移动操作中存在两个核心局限：(i)直接将观测映射到全身动作块，缺乏显式的任务空间运动规划，导致基座与手臂协调预测不精确；(ii)开环执行预测的动作块，不检查动作能否实现预期运动，导致控制误差和未建模接触的累积，造成计划与实际运动之间的差距。

Method: DreamTrajectory包含两个关键组件：(1)单一动作专家同时预测意图级末端执行器轨迹和全身动作块，使轨迹显式地引导基座-手臂动作生成；(2)轻量级轨迹世界模型预测候选动作块会产生的轨迹，并通过测试时搜索-预测-评分流程选择与计划轨迹最一致的候选动作。

Result: 在MS-HAB基准上，轨迹引导将平均成功率从32.3%提升到47.5%，测试时优化进一步将成功率提升至54.8%，在接触丰富的铰接物体任务上增益最大。在三个真实世界移动操作任务中，平均成功率分别达到63.3%、81.7%和90.0%。

Conclusion: DreamTrajectory证明了显式的任务空间轨迹引导与测试时轨迹级验证相结合，能够有效提升移动操作中基座-手臂协调的精度和闭环鲁棒性，尤其在接触丰富的铰接物体任务中表现突出。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires a robot to coordinate base and arm motion under continuously changing viewpoints and contact conditions, within an action space far larger than that of fixed-base manipulation. Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are limited in two respects. (i)They map observations directly to whole-body action chunks, searching this large action space without an explicit task-space motion plan, which makes coordinated base--arm prediction imprecise. (ii)They execute the predicted chunk open-loop, without checking whether the actions can realize the motion the policy intended, so control errors and unmodeled contacts accumulate into a gap between planned and realized motion. We present DreamTrajectory, a trajectory-guided framework for language-conditioned mobile manipulation that introduces one component for each limitation. Addressing(i), DreamTrajectory jointly predicts an intention-level end-effector trajectory and a whole-body action chunk in a single action expert, so that the trajectory explicitly guides base--arm action generation instead of remaining implicit. Addressing(ii), a lightweight trajectory world model predicts the trajectory that a candidate action chunk would induce, and a test-time search--predict--score procedure selects the candidate best aligned with the planned trajectory. On MS-HAB, trajectory guidance raises average success from 32.3% to 47.5% and test-time refinement further to 54.8%, with the largest gains on contact-rich articulated-object tasks. On three real-world mobile manipulation tasks, the corresponding average success rates are 63.3%, 81.7%, and 90.0%.

</details>


### [339] [SG-WAM: Self-Guided World Modeling in Geometry-Aware Policy Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01397)
*Ruiteng Zhao,Zhengshen Zhang,Yue Su,Wenshuo Wang,Jiahui Li,Zhiyuan Yang,Francis E. H. Tay,Marcelo H. Ang,Haiyue Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-WAM是一个自引导框架，直接在策略表征空间中学习几何感知的动作条件动力学模型，通过可学习动力学令牌和自引导世界预测器实现未来状态预测，在LIBERO上达到98.5%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）要么依赖感知上较重的观测空间目标，要么使用不与动作生成和几何结构联合对齐的辅助隐空间，难以同时满足动作相关性和几何感知的要求。

Method: SG-WAM在策略派生的表征空间中学习几何感知的动作条件动力学。引入可学习动力学令牌和自引导世界预测器预测未来潜在状态；通过策略骨干的指数移动平均副本生成预测目标；几何监督结构化策略图像令牌表征；将潜在未来预测、几何基础和流匹配动作生成端到端联合优化。

Result: 基于0.9B模型无大规模具身预训练，在LIBERO上达到98.5%平均成功率，在LIBERO-Plus上达到73%，在分布内和分布外真实世界评估中均优于强基线。

Conclusion: 在策略表征空间中联合建模几何感知动力学和动作生成是可行的，且无需大规模预训练即可实现高性能，证明了自引导训练范式的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple action generation with prediction of future states. Their effectiveness depends on whether future dynamics are modeled in a space that is both aligned with action generation and sufficiently geometry-aware to capture where and how actions change the scene. Existing WAMs typically satisfy only part of this requirement, relying on either perceptually heavy observation-space targets or auxiliary latent spaces that are not jointly structured for action relevance and geometry. We propose SG-WAM, a self-guided framework that learns geometry-aware action-conditioned dynamics directly in the policy-derived representation space. SG-WAM introduces learnable dynamics tokens and a Self-Guided World Predictor that forecasts their future latent states conditioned on intervening robot actions. Prediction targets are generated by an exponential moving average copy of the same policy backbone, providing stable supervision within the representation family used by the action expert. Geometric supervision further structures the policy image-token representations, providing spatially grounded context for the dynamics tokens and yielding a future-alignment space that is both action-relevant and geometry-aware. Latent future prediction, geometric grounding, and flow-matching action generation are jointly optimized end-to-end in a unified framework. Built on a 0.9B model without large-scale embodied pretraining, SG-WAM achieves 98.5% average success on LIBERO and 73% on LIBERO-Plus, while outperforming strong baselines in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [340] [Demystifying When and Why VLAs Fail in Contact-Rich Tasks and How to Fix Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01402)
*Carlota Parés-Morlans,Nils Kuhn,Isabel Liu,Alberta Longhini,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言-动作模型在接触密集型操作任务中失败的根本原因，识别出两类失败模式（精度失败源于流匹配策略训练不匹配，力失败源于力信号的独特结构），并提出针对性的FACT方法，在五个任务上实现66%的平均成功率，优于最佳基线方法的41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作主要集中于通过力增强架构和训练时正则化来解决接触失败问题，但接触失败的根源尚未被充分探索，本文旨在深入理解VLA模型在接触密集型操作中失败的深层原因。

Method: 识别出两类失败模式：精度失败（源于流匹配策略训练不匹配）和力失败（源于力信号的独特结构），针对每类失败模式设计了对应的目标机制，并将两者组合为FACT方法。

Result: FACT在五个接触密集型任务上达到66%的平均成功率，而最佳先前基线的平均成功率为41%，评估涉及近2,500次真实世界部署测试。

Conclusion: 通过识别并针对性地解决两类不同的失败根源，可以有效提升VLA模型在接触密集型操作任务中的表现，相比仅从架构层面修补具有更显著的改进效果。
