Abstract: We address the problem of understanding when and why Vision-Language-Action models struggle with contact-rich manipulation tasks that require precise physical interaction. Prior work has primarily focused on addressing contact failures through force-augmented architectures and training-time regularizers, yet the root causes of these failures remain underexplored. We identify two distinct failure modes underlying this gap. Precision failures are rooted in a flow-matching policy training mismatch, and force failures arise from the distinctive structure of force signals. We address each failure mode with a targeted mechanism and combine them into FACT, which achieves 66% average success rate across five contact-rich tasks against 41% for the best prior baseline, in an evaluation spanning almost 2,500 real-world rollouts.

</details>


### [341] [GenTrack: Physical Alignment for Robot-Native Motion Generation and Zero-Shot Humanoid Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01410)
*Zeyu Ling,Xinyao Yu,Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng,Zhanke Wang,Qing Shuai,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenTrack提出了一个在线生成器-追踪器协同训练框架，通过执行接地、组相对生成器对齐与追踪器训练的交替进行，在无需额外数据采集的情况下缩小重定向参考运动与实际机器人可执行运动之间的差距，显著提升零样本人形机器人追踪的覆盖范围和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用人形追踪器虽然能执行多样化的运动参考，但其零样本覆盖依赖于成本高昂的大型具身语料库扩展；文本到运动生成器虽可提供可扩展的监督信号，但基于人类运动或重定向数据的训练会在运动学合理性与机器人可执行性之间存在固有差距；现有的单向流水线要么固定生成语料库，要么固定奖励追踪器，无法闭环优化。

Method: GenTrack采用在线生成器-追踪器框架，交替进行两个核心步骤：(1)执行接地的、组相对的生成器对齐；(2)在新生成的参考运动上训练追踪器。同时引入锚定（anchoring）和排练（rehearsal）机制以约束训练过程中的漂移问题，实现联合在线后训练。

Result: 在Unitree G1平台上，使用ProtoMotions和SONIC作为骨干网络，在包括公开AMASS和LAFAN基准以及包含1,024个野外提示-运动对的私有分布外测试集上评估。在线协同训练策略持续生成更具机器人可执行性且语义对齐良好的运动，追踪器的零样本覆盖范围显著扩大，追踪精度提升尤其在分布外参考上表现突出。

Conclusion: 联合在线后训练有效缩小了重定向参考运动与机器人原生运动之间的可执行性差距，在不进行额外数据收集的情况下推进了零样本人形机器人控制，突破了静态参考池的局限性。

Abstract: General-purpose humanoid trackers can execute diverse references, but their zero-shot coverage depends on large embodied corpora that are costly to extend. Text-to-motion generators offer scalable supervision, yet models trained on human motion or retargeted data inherit a gap between kinematic plausibility and robot executability. Existing one-way pipelines fix either the generated corpus or the reward tracker. We introduce GenTrack, an online generator--tracker framework that alternates execution-grounded, group-relative generator alignment with tracker training on newly generated references; anchoring and rehearsal constrain drift. On Unitree G1, we evaluate GenTrack with ProtoMotions and SONIC backbones across three zero-shot tracking splits including public AMASS and LAFAN benchmarks, and a private out-of-distribution test set of 1,024 prompt-motion pairs in the wild. The online co-training strategy consistently produces generators that output more robot-executable motions with strong semantic alignment, and trackers with markedly broader zero-shot coverage and improved tracking accuracy, especially on out-of-distribution references. These results demonstrate that joint online post-training effectively narrows the executability gap between retargeted references and robot-native motion, advancing zero-shot humanoid control without additional data collection and beyond the limitations of a static reference pool.

</details>


### [342] [When Replanning Becomes the Bottleneck: Budgeted Replanning for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01428)
*Shuaijun Liu,Feiyang You,Xingwei Chen,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BRACE，一个将重规划建模为预算控制循环的控制器，配合E-RECAP渐进式token剪枝方法，在多个具身智能平台上大幅减少重规划调用的token消耗（62-92%）并将SLO违规率从85.5-100%降至4.7-50%，在困难场景下达到80%成功率且仅4.6%的SLO违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体频繁重规划时，LLM调用积累的文本上下文随时间增长，导致重规划延迟出现重尾分布并可能错过实时截止时间，而仅靠平均延迟或成功率难以检测此类失败模式。

Method: BRACE将重规划形式化为带预算的控制循环，决策是否重规划、选择重规划模式、分配显式token预算和延迟SLO；E-RECAP是成本感知的渐进式token剪枝方法，在transformer层间预测token效用并剪枝，同时保留关键的头部和尾部token。

Result: 在Meta Habitat、RoboFactory和AirSim上，BRACE+E-RECAP将重规划token数减少62-92%，SLO违规率从85.5-100%降至4.7-50%；在更困难的RoboFactory场景中达到80%成功率且仅4.6%的SLO违规。

Conclusion: 尾部感知的每次调用预算分配在跨具身智能平台上有效，既能保持任务成功率又能满足实时性约束，证明该方法是解决LLM重规划延迟问题的可行方案。

Abstract: Embodied agents replan frequently to recover from execution drift, partial observability, and coordination hazards, but each LLM-based replanning call can consume an accumulated textual context that grows over time and across agents. Once this context becomes large, replanning latency develops heavy tails and can miss real-time deadlines even when task success remains high, a failure mode that is hard to detect from average latency or success alone. We present BRACE, a controller that formulates replanning as a budgeted control loop by deciding whether to replan, selecting a replanning mode, and allocating an explicit token budget and latency service-level objective (SLO) while accounting for optional efficiency modules. As a reusable component, we introduce E-RECAP, a cost-aware progressive token pruning method that predicts token utility and prunes replanning contexts across transformer layers while preserving critical head and tail tokens. Across Meta Habitat, RoboFactory, and AirSim, BRACE with E-RECAP reduces replanning-call token counts by 62-92% and SLO violation rates from 85.5-100.0% to 4.7-50.0% in settings where task success is already saturated. In a harder RoboFactory setting where open-loop, frozen-plan, and No BRACE all fail, BRACE + E-RECAP reaches 80.0% success with 4.6% SLO violations, demonstrating that tail-aware per-call budgeting is effective across embodied platforms.

</details>


### [343] [DynamicManip: Enabling Dynamic Manipulation from a Single Static Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01452)
*Haoran Liao,Pengyue Wang,Shuoyu Chen,Kehan Cheng,Xuhang Chen,Yuhao Lin,Mu Lin,Zhizhao Liang,Xiaoyi Fan,Chengyi Xing,Dan Niu,Yi-Lin Wei,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicManip框架，通过静态到动态的数据增强流水线和动态感知的自适应策略，解决了动态操作任务中数据需求大和策略执行延迟高两大挑战，在仿真和真实环境中均取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态操作是机器人在复杂动态环境中不可或缺的能力，但学习该类任务面临两个主要挑战：(1)动态场景的组合复杂性导致需要大量数据；(2)动力学的快速变化要求策略实时且准确地执行。

Method: (1)提出静态到动态的数据增强流水线，从单个静态演示合成多样化的动态操作演示；(2)引入动态感知自适应策略，根据任务动力学调整推理频率；(3)构建包含多样动态任务和自动评估系统的动态操作基准。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的广泛实验表明，DynamicManip在数据效率和动态操作性能上均有显著提升，平均成功率提高18.4个百分点，策略查询延迟降低32.9%。

Conclusion: DynamicManip通过高效的数据增强和低延迟的自适应推理策略，有效解决了动态操作学习中的数据效率和实时执行问题，为机器人动态操作提供了可扩展且一致的评估方案。

Abstract: Dynamic manipulation is a critical capability for robots operating in complex and dynamic environments, where robots must interact with objects that are moving or require rapid adjustments. However, learning models for dynamic manipulation tasks face two major challenges: (1) the combinatorial complexity of dynamic scenarios leads to substantial data requirements, and (2) rapid variations in dynamics require real-time and accurate policy execution. In this paper, we propose DynamicManip to address these challenges through an efficient data augmentation pipeline and a low-latency imitation policy. We first propose a static-to-dynamic augmentation pipeline that synthesizes diverse dynamic manipulation demonstrations from a single static demonstration. Second, we introduce a dynamic-aware adaptive policy that adjusts its inference frequency according to task dynamics, enabling responsive and effective dynamic manipulation. Third, we build a dynamic manipulation benchmark, which includes diverse dynamic tasks with an automatic evaluation system for scalable and consistent assessment. Extensive experiments in both simulation and the real world demonstrate that DynamicManip not only provides significant improvements in data efficiency but also achieves better performance in dynamic manipulation tasks, with a mean success rate 18.4 percentage points higher and policy-query latency 32.9% lower.

</details>


### [344] [Rapid Embodiment Adaptation for Quadrupedal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01506)
*Dichen Li,Bo Ai,Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters,Hao Su,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线本体适应性框架，用于四足机器人运动控制。该方法通过训练在随机化本体参数下的通用策略，配合轻量级适应性模块，能在半秒内识别硬件变化（如关节限制和躯干质量变化），并基于推断的硬件状态进行条件控制。实验表明该方法在仿真和真实Unitree Go2机器人上均显著优于非适应性方法，即使在完全锁死的腿和5kg负载等严重情况下仍能保持稳定运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松适应身体变化（如衰老、受伤或负重），但基于学习的机器人策略在硬件属性发生变化时往往会失效。现有方法缺乏对硬件状态变化的显式识别与快速适应能力，因此需要一种能够在线推断本体参数并据此调整控制的框架。

Method: 该方法包含两个核心组件：1）在随机化本体参数下训练的通用策略网络；2）一个轻量级适应性模块，从短交互历史中推断当前的本体参数（包括关节角度限制和躯干质量）。推断出的硬件状态被用作策略的条件输入，实现闭环控制。

Result: 在仿真中，该模块能够准确估计本体变化，闭环控制性能显著优于直接基于交互历史进行条件控制的策略。在真实Unitree Go2机器人上，系统在最严重的测试场景下（完全锁死的腿和5kg额外负载）仍保持稳定运动，而非适应性方法在这些情况下失效。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了显式在线本体识别对于快速适应关节限制和负载质量变化具有实用性，为处理更广泛的不确定、退化或变化的机器人硬件场景迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Humans readily adapt their movements as their bodies change through aging, injury, or load carrying, but learning-based robot policies often break when hardware properties shift. We introduce an online embodiment adaptation framework for quadrupedal locomotion that infers embodiment parameters from short interaction histories and conditions control on the inferred hardware state. Our method pairs a generalist policy trained under embodiment randomization with a lightweight adaptation module that identifies physical changes within half a second. We evaluate two representative forms of embodiment variation: joint-range constraints and trunk-mass changes, corresponding to joint-level kinematic degradation and body-level dynamic variation. In simulation, the module accurately estimates these changes and enables closed-loop control that substantially outperforms policies conditioned directly on interaction history. On a real Unitree Go2 robot, our system maintains stable locomotion under severe instances of the evaluated changes, including a fully locked leg and a 5 kg payload, where non-adaptive methods fail. These results demonstrate the practicality of explicit online embodiment identification for rapid adaptation to joint-limit and payload-mass changes, and provide a step toward handling broader forms of uncertain, degraded, or changing robot hardware.

</details>


### [345] [Learning Smooth SE(3) Trajectories under Left-Invariant Riemannian Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01562)
*Yuwei Wu,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的框架，用于在SE(3)流形上、通用左不变黎曼度量下近似生成高阶光滑轨迹。方法通过神经网络学习多项式系数子集和轨迹时长，其余系数解析确定以满足边界条件，训练由欧拉-拉格朗日最优性条件、度量加权平滑目标和可行性约束引导，实现毫秒级推理，逼近数值优化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SE(3)上刚体运动的最优轨迹生成在一般边界条件和耦合旋转-平移度量下需要昂贵的数值边界值求解器，限制了其在实时机器人规划与控制中的应用。现有闭合解仅适用于特殊度量或静止到静止边界条件，缺乏通用高效的解决方案。

Method: 将体旋轨迹用高阶多项式参数化，采用神经网络学习部分多项式系数和轨迹时长，剩余系数通过解析方法强制满足边界条件。网络训练损失由三部分构成：欧拉-拉格朗日最优性条件、度量加权平滑度和可行性约束。框架以度量为条件，可泛化到不同度量结构和运动条件。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，该方法生成的平滑轨迹与数值优化解高度接近，推理时间达到毫秒级。两个实际应用得到验证：带途经点的多样化运动基元实时生成，以及动态条件下四旋翼飞行的轨迹精化。

Conclusion: 带几何结构的学习式运动生成为SE(3)上传统基于优化的轨迹生成方法提供了高效替代方案，兼顾了几何一致性和实时性能，有望推动几何一致轨迹生成在实时机器人规划控制中的广泛部署。

Abstract: Optimal trajectory generation for rigid-body motions on Lie groups can be formulated as a variational problem that minimizes energy functionals defined by Riemannian metrics. While closed-form solutions exist for special cases such as product metrics and rest-to-rest boundary conditions, solving the general problem with arbitrary boundary states and coupled rotational-translational metrics often requires computationally expensive numerical boundary value solvers. These limitations restrict the use of geometrically consistent trajectory generation in real-time robotic planning and control. This paper presents a learning-based framework for approximating higher-order smooth trajectories on SE(3) under general left-invariant Riemannian metrics. The method parameterizes body-twist trajectories using high-order polynomials and relies on a neural network to learn a subset of the polynomial coefficients and the trajectory duration. The remaining coefficients are analytically determined to enforce the boundary conditions. The training of the network is guided by losses derived from Euler-Lagrange optimality conditions, metric-weighted smoothness objectives, and feasibility constraints. The metric-conditioned framework enables generalization across diverse metric structures and motion conditions. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach generates smooth trajectories that closely approximate solutions from numerical optimization while achieving millisecond-level inference times. We demonstrate two practical applications of the proposed framework: real-time generation of diverse motion primitives with waypoint traversal, and refinement for quadrotor flight under dynamic conditions. These results suggest that learning-based motions with geometric structure can provide an efficient alternative to conventional optimization-based methods for trajectory generation on SE(3).

</details>


### [346] [Uncovering and Mitigating Positional Blind Spots in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01573)
*Dongdong An,Pengjie Zhao,Yihao Huang,Wenbing Tang,Ziming He,Jiayi Zhu,Jifeng Ning,Qin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段黑盒框架，用于发现并缓解视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作中的"位置性盲区"（PBS）——即空间中局部区域的失败率显著升高的问题。第一阶段通过网格划分和单侧似然比检验定位PBS，第二阶段利用从这些区域收集的示教数据进行LoRA微调来提升模型在该区域的能力，同时保持其他区域性能不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型评估方法基于预定义物体配置上的成功率聚合，隐含假设模型在操作空间中能力均匀分布。但作者发现这一假设不成立：即使指令和其他场景因素不变，仅移动任务无关干扰物的位置就会在局部空间区域引发显著失败的升高，即"位置性盲区"（PBS）。这个问题尚未被系统性地发现和解决。

Method: 提出两阶段黑盒框架：（1）发现阶段：将工作空间网格化，对每个网格单元应用单侧对数似然比检验，定位失败风险显著升高的PBS单元；（2）缓解阶段：从PBS区域收集示教数据，通过LoRA对策略进行微调，提升局部区域能力同时尽量保持全局性能。

Result: 在五个SOTA VLA策略、两个基准上评估发现：所有模型均存在普遍且空间集中的PBS，失败率最高达0.58。所提出的搜索策略平均F1分数为0.678，分别优于随机搜索和自适应采样基线0.268和0.178。基于发现的区域进行定向微调，将整体失败率降低了40.00%–85.19%。

Conclusion: PBS是VLA模型中普遍存在且空间集中的现象，现有基于成功率聚合的评估方式会掩盖这一缺陷。所提出的黑盒发现与缓解框架能够有效定位PBS并通过定向微调显著降低失败率，为VLA模型的空间稳健性评估与改进提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve promising performance in robotic manipulation, typically measured by success rates aggregated over predefined object configurations, an evaluation that implicitly assumes spatially uniform competence across the workspace. However, this assumption does not hold: even with the instruction and every other scene factor held fixed, merely relocating a task-irrelevant distractor can sharply raise the failure probability within localized, spatially coherent regions, which we term Positional Blind Spots (PBS). In this paper, we propose a two-stage black-box framework to uncover and mitigate PBS. During the uncovering stage, we grid the workspace and apply a one-sided log-likelihood-ratio test to localize PBS cells with significantly elevated risk. During the mitigation stage, we fine-tune the policy via LoRA on demonstrations collected from these PBS regions, improving competence there while largely preserving performance across the rest of the workspace. We evaluate our framework on five state-of-the-art VLA policies across two benchmarks, and find that PBS are pervasive and spatially concentrated in all of them, with failure rates up to 0.58. Our search strategy achieves an average F1-score of 0.678, outperforming random search and adaptive sampling baselines by 0.268 and 0.178, respectively. Guided by the discovered regions, targeted fine-tuning reduces the overall failure rate by 40.00%--85.19%.

</details>


### [347] [Perception-and-action system for humanoid robot task execution in construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01600)
*Yanxi Liu,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种感知-动作系统，使类人机器人能够从工人演示中学习并执行建筑施工任务，包含Humanoid-PoseNet和Humanoid-ActionNet两个深度网络，实验成功执行八种施工动作，平均运动跟踪误差82.45mm MPJPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人与人形工作环境高度契合，有潜力在土木和建筑工程领域与人类协作或自主执行危险繁重任务，但现有研究对如何赋予类人机器人实际施工能力探索有限，缺乏从人类演示中学习施工任务的有效方法。

Method: 提出一个新颖的感知-动作系统，包含两个深度网络：Humanoid-PoseNet用于提取人体姿态并转化为类人机器人可执行的机械可行姿势；Humanoid-ActionNet基于转化后的姿势学习机器人可执行的动作。

Result: 实验结果表明类人机器人可靠地执行了八种与建筑相关的动作，平均运动跟踪误差为82.45mm MPJPE（平均每关节位置误差）。

Conclusion: 该工作为在建筑领域部署类人机器人协作者提供了早期探索和基础，展示了从工人演示中学习施工任务的可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots, with their human-like shape and multi-tasking capabilities, are well-aligned with human-dominated workplaces, like those in civil and construction engineering, where they could collaborate with human workers or autonomously perform physically demanding and hazardous tasks. Despite this promise, limited research has explored how to endow these robots with the practical capabilities needed to perform construction tasks. To this end, this study proposes a novel perception-and-action system that enables humanoid robots to learn and perform construction tasks from worker demonstrations. This system contains two deep networks: Humanoid-PoseNet, which extracts human postures and translates them into mechanically feasible poses for a humanoid robot; and Humanoid-ActionNet, which learns robot-executable actions based on these translated poses. Experimental results demonstrate that the humanoid robot reliably executed eight construction-related actions, achieving an average motion-tracking error of 82.45 mm MPJPE (Mean Per Joint Position Error). This work provides an early step toward deploying humanoid collaborators in construction.

</details>


### [348] [AffordTrajDP: Dynamic Affordance-Guided Visuomotor Policy Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01603)
*Gaoyuan Wu,Ziyu Shan,Haoyang Du,Yuyao Jiang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AffordTrajDP，一个动态的仿射引导模仿学习框架，通过基于物体SE(3)位姿的时间传播构建仿射轨迹，为机器人操作提供随时间一致的状态感知引导，在ManiSkill3上达到70.0%的平均成功率，并在多种真实机器人实验中验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仿射引导模仿学习方法使用静态仿射（如固定接触点），在精密任务或物体位置扰动下会产生不一致的问题，导致接触后轨迹漂移。

Method: 给定RGB-D观测，检索一个锚定仿射（捕捉末端执行器与目标物体之间的期望接触点），通过仿射传播以物体SE(3)位姿作为传播媒介，向前传播生成仿射轨迹，提供时间一致的、状态感知的操作全流程引导。

Result: 在ManiSkill3上达到70.0%的平均成功率，优于强基线最高达17.8%。在Galaxea A1和UR7e机械臂上覆盖StackCube、PickCup、AdapterInsertion、Ring-on-Peg、Put-in-Bowl和USB Insertion任务的真实实验验证了在物体位置变化和外观变化下的鲁棒性，并在Galaxea A1上评估了已见和未见物体实例，消融实验确认了各组件贡献。

Conclusion: 动态仿射轨迹比静态仿射更适用于精密操作任务和位置扰动场景，利用物体SE(3)位姿作为传播媒介是构建时间一致引导的有效方式，能够显著提升操作成功率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Affordance-guided imitation learning has shown impressive performance in robotic manipulation tasks by compressing visual perception into task-specific geometric constraints (e.g., fixed contact points). However, the commonly used static affordances can become inconsistent in precision-critical tasks or under object location perturbations, leading to post-contact trajectory drift. To address this issue, we propose AffordTrajDP, a dynamic framework that constructs affordance trajectories via object-centric temporal propagation to guide the progressive manipulation process. Specifically, given an RGB-D observation, our core insight is that a retrieved anchor affordance, which captures the desired contact point between the end-effector and the target object, can be propagated forward via affordance propagation, using the object's SE(3) pose as a natural propagation medium, to yield an affordance trajectory that provides temporally consistent, state-aware guidance throughout execution. AffordTrajDP achieves 70.0% average success rate on ManiSkill3, outperforming strong baselines by up to 17.8%. Real-world experiments on Galaxea A1 and UR7e robotic arms, covering StackCube, PickCup, AdapterInsertion, Ring-on-Peg, Put-in-Bowl, and USB Insertion, further validate robustness under object placement variations and appearance changes, with seen and unseen object instances evaluated on Galaxea A1, and ablations confirm the contribution of each proposed component.

</details>


### [349] [A Forward-Inverse Dynamic Game Framework for Enhanced Multi-Agent Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01636)
*Tianle Liu,Youcheng Niu,Jing Zeng,Shuo Li,Jinming Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于KL正则化的动态博弈框架，用于多智能体轨迹规划中的反馈纳什均衡求解。通过状态依赖的自适应权重平衡最优性与行为先验，并基于最大熵逆向强化学习与物理信息正则化推断未知成本参数，在安全关键场景中实现了对有限理性智能体和空间变化交互强度的有效建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态博弈方法通常假设智能体完全理性且目标已知，或依赖固定正则化，难以捕捉安全关键场景中的有限理性行为和空间变化的交互强度，需要更自适应的框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出KL正则化动态博弈模型，采用状态依赖权重自适应平衡最优性与行为先验；设计基于最大熵逆向强化学习的上下文感知逆博弈模块，并引入物理信息正则化确保与正向博弈的结构一致性。

Result: 证明了正则化局部博弈每次迭代的适定性，并表明自适应权重函数在有界名义轨迹更新下保持Lipschitz连续。数值仿真与多机器人协同导航及汇入场景实验验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过KL正则化和自适应权重机制，有效解决了未知目标和状态依赖耦合下的多智能体轨迹规划问题，为安全关键场景中的有限理性博弈建模提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper studies feedback Nash equilibrium (FBNE) seeking for multi-agent trajectory planning in nonlinear dynamical systems with unknown agents' objectives and state-dependent inter-agent coupling. While dynamic game theory provides a principled framework for such problems, existing approaches typically assume fully rational agents with known objectives or rely on fixed regularization, limiting their ability to capture bounded rationality and spatially varying interaction intensity in safety-critical settings. To this end, we propose a KL-regularized dynamic game with a state-dependent weight that adaptively balances optimality and behavioral priors. To infer unknown cost parameters from demonstrated behaviors, we develop a context-aware inverse game module based on maximum-entropy inverse reinforcement learning with physics-informed regularization, ensuring structural consistency with the forward game. We establish per-iteration well-posedness of the regularized local game and show that the adaptive weighting function remains Lipschitz continuous under bounded nominal-trajectory updates. Numerical simulations and multi-robot experiments on cooperative navigation and merging scenarios validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [350] [SyncPlan: Long-Horizon LLM Coordination with Explicit Synchronization and Adaptive Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01652)
*Shen You,Xiaoming Zhu,Weining Weng,Hefei Mei,Weixuan Wang,Zhongshen Li,Zeji LI,Ye-Wen Wang,Zijun Liao,Juchao Zhuo,Yang Wei,Fuhao Qiu,Siqin Li,Zhenjie Lian,Danei Gong,Junkai Ji,Xiangtao Li,Qiuzhen Lin,Liang Wang,Ka-Chun Wong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SyncPlan，一个用于长期多智能体协调的"计划-执行-纠正"框架。它通过中央LLM协调器一次性生成各智能体的动作链，利用显式等待原语和死锁检测保障依赖关系，并通过轻量级计划陈旧性检测器在环境变化时触发重新规划。在Overcooked和Honor of Kings基准上，SyncPlan在取得最先进任务成功率的同时，运行耗时不到现有LLM协调器的0.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体协调方法在效率和适应性之间存在根本性权衡：反复调用LLM或多轮通信虽然能动态适应环境但产生大量延迟，且容易受到异步进度和环境变化的影响；而一次性规划则产生开环计划，在执行中易于过时或失败。亟需一种既能保持低开销又能动态适应环境变化的协调方案。

Method: SyncPlan采用"计划-执行-纠正"三阶段框架：(1) 中央LLM协调器在单次规划调用中为每个智能体生成动作链；(2) 执行阶段通过显式等待原语和死锁检测强制执行智能体间及智能体-环境依赖；(3) 轻量级计划陈旧性检测器持续评估剩余计划，在环境变化使其失效时触发重新规划。此外，通过SFT和面向规划的RL（包含密集任务进度和结果级执行反馈）优化协调器。

Result: 在Overcooked基准和Honor of Kings环境上的实验表明，SyncPlan取得了最先进的任务成功率，同时相比现有LLM协调器，墙钟运行时消耗不到0.05%。

Conclusion: SyncPlan通过显式同步机制和自适应纠正策略，有效解决了LLM多智能体协调中效率与适应性的权衡问题，在复杂动态环境中实现了高效且稳健的长期协调。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent coordination faces a fundamental trade-off between efficiency and adaptivity in dynamic environments. Existing approaches typically rely on repeated LLM invocations or multi-round communication to adapt decisions during execution, introducing substantial latency and making coordination vulnerable to asynchronous progress and environmental changes. Conversely, one-shot planning reduces coordination overhead but produces open-loop plans that can quickly become stale or fail when actions depend on other agents and the environment. We introduce SyncPlan, a plan-execute-correct framework for long-horizon coordination through explicit synchronization and adaptive correction. Given the state and team-level task, a centralized LLM coordinator generates per-agent action chains in a single planning call. During execution, explicit wait primitives and deadlock detection enforce inter-agent and agent-environment dependencies, while a lightweight Plan Staleness Detector continuously assesses the remaining plan and triggers replanning when environmental changes invalidate its assumptions. We further optimize the coordinator through SFT and planning-oriented RL with dense task progress and outcome-level execution feedback. Experiments on the public Overcooked benchmark and the complex Honor of Kings environment show that SyncPlan achieves state-of-the-art task success rates while using less than 0.05% of the wall-clock runtime compared with existing LLM-based coordinators. Code and datasets will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [351] [ProtoAct: Turning Wet-Lab Protocols into Embodied Robotic Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01690)
*Zhe Liu,Jiaming Gu,Zhaohui Du,Zhe Wang,Huanbo Jin,Quan Lu,Qi Wang,Ting Xiao,Minting Pan,Dongzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProtoAct框架，将自由形式的生物湿实验室协议转换为状态感知、可执行于机器人的动作序列，并引入BioP2E数据集（22个细胞培养协议，258个监控条件，910个可执行子任务，962个接地动作调用）。在7个大语言模型上验证了框架的有效性，并通过消融实验证明了检索、后置检查和模式约束的互补贡献，最终支持模拟和真实机器人环境下的VLA模型训练与执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物湿实验室协议是为训练有素的研究人员编写的，常隐含常规操作、状态依赖条件和上下文参数，导致难以转化为机器人可执行的动作。现有方法缺乏将协议文本结构化解析并转化为可执行动作序列的机制，因此需要一种能够弥合生物协议理解与实体机器人执行之间鸿沟的框架。

Method: ProtoAct框架包含三个核心组件：1) ProtoRAG，通过检索人工标注示例实现上下文敏感的解析；2) RefineChecker，用于检测并修正缺失或不一致的步骤；3) ActSchema，将精炼后的流程映射为受约束的JSON函数序列。同时构建了BioP2E数据集用于评估。

Result: 在7个大语言模型上的评估表明ProtoAct可以有效地与不同骨干模型实例化。消融实验证实检索、后置检查和模式约束具有互补贡献。解析出的子任务支持演示收集和VLA模型训练，在模拟和真实机器人环境中均实现了成功执行。

Conclusion: ProtoAct为生物协议理解与实体机器人执行之间提供了实用的接口，证明了通过检索增强、一致性检查和结构化模式约束，可以将模糊的生物实验文档转化为机器人可执行的精确动作序列，为自动化生物实验铺平了道路。

Abstract: Biological wet-lab protocols are written for trained researchers and often leave routine operations, state-dependent conditions, and contextual parameters implicit, making them difficult to translate into robot-executable actions. We present ProtoAct, a structured protocol-grounding framework that converts free-form biological procedures into state-aware, embodiment-ready action sequences. ProtoAct uses ProtoRAG to retrieve manually annotated examples for context-sensitive parsing, employs RefineChecker to detect and revise missing or inconsistent steps, and applies ActSchema to map the refined procedure into constrained JSON function sequences. We further introduce BioP2E, for which we manually annotate 22 cell-culture protocols into 258 monitoring conditions, 910 executable subtasks, and 962 grounded action calls. Evaluation across seven large language models demonstrates that ProtoAct can be effectively instantiated with different backbones. Ablations confirm that retrieval, posterior checking, and schema constraints make complementary contributions. The parsed subtasks further support demonstration collection and VLA model training, enabling successful execution in both simulation and real-robot settings. ProtoAct thus provides a practical interface between biological protocol understanding and embodied robotic execution.

</details>


### [352] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Parallel-Link Leg Mechanisms via Simulator-Side Dynamics Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01697)
*Jinsong Hong,Jangho Kim,Jihwan Lee,Donghyun Kim,Sehoon Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出模拟器侧系统归一化（S3N）方法，解决并联连杆机构在串行树替代模型中的仿真到现实动力学差距。S3N通过坐标变换将执行器惯性和阻尼纳入串行坐标动力学（S3N-Act），并单独辨识执行器和连杆级频率响应来恢复残余连杆惯性（S3N-Full），在2自由度验证中分别将关节位置和力矩均方根误差降低80.9%和82.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并联连杆机构在仿真中常被表示为串行树替代模型，但传统的基于雅可比矩阵的状态和力矩映射虽然保持运动学和虚功关系的一致性，却无法解释坐标诱导的执行器惯性和阻尼的重新分布，以及串行树简化过程中省略的连杆惯性，导致仿真到现实的动力学差距。

Method: 提出S3N方法：S3N-Act通过坐标变换将执行器惯性和阻尼纳入串行坐标动力学；S3N-Full通过分别辨识执行器级和连杆级频率响应来恢复残余连杆惯性。该方法保持树拓扑结构不变，在模拟器侧对有效动力学进行归一化。

Result: 2自由度验证中，S3N-Full相对雅可比映射基线将关节位置和力矩RMSE分别降低80.9%和82.1%；俯仰原地运动中S3N-Act和S3N-Full将地面反作用力范数RMSE分别降低65.1%和62.4%；圆形运动中S3N-Full将相位平均的命令归一化仿真到现实差距从17.3%降至9.9%。

Conclusion: 模拟器侧归一化能够改善运动和力层面的仿真到现实一致性，使得在串行树框架中进行策略训练时能够获得与硬件一致的动力学，更好地表征物理并联连杆机构。

Abstract: This paper addresses the sim-to-real gap in dynamics arising when a parallel-link mechanism is represented by a serial-tree surrogate in simulation. Conventional Jacobian-based state and torque mappings preserve consistency with the kinematic and virtual-work relations but do not account for the coordinate-induced redistribution of actuator inertia and damping and the linkage inertia omitted during serial-tree reduction. To address this gap, Simulator-Side System Normalization (S3N) is proposed to normalize the serial-tree simulator's effective dynamics while preserving its tree topology. S3N-Act incorporates actuator inertia and damping into the serial-coordinate dynamics through coordinate transformation, whereas S3N-Full restores residual linkage inertia by separately identifying actuator- and leg-level frequency responses. In the 2-DoF validation, S3N-Full reduced the joint-position and torque RMSEs by 80.9% and 82.1%, respectively, relative to the Jacobian-mapping baseline. During pitch-in-place motion, S3N-Act and S3N-Full reduced the RMSE of the ground reaction force norm by 65.1% and 62.4%, respectively. During circular locomotion, S3N-Full reduced the phase-averaged, command-normalized sim-to-real gap from 17.3% to 9.9%. These results show that simulator-side normalization improves motion- and force-level sim-to-real consistency. It enables policy training in a serial-tree framework with hardware-consistent dynamics that better represent the physical parallel-link mechanism.

</details>


### [353] [TWINS: A Tactile Wearable Isomorphic Arm Networked System for Contact-Rich Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01733)
*Takahide Kitamura,Masaki Murooka,Natsuki Yamanobe,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TWINS是一个用于涉及身体表面接触的操控任务的机器人系统，由操作者穿戴的双臂设备和同构机器人组成，通过嵌入胸部和手臂的分布式触觉传感器测量身体表面接触，实现示范、学习和执行的一体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人操控系统主要聚焦于末端执行器操控，难以教授和执行涉及手臂和胸部身体表面接触的操控任务，缺乏能够统一采集、学习和执行此类任务的系统。

Method: TWINS由可穿戴双臂设备和同构机器人组成，两者具有相同的关节配置和外部尺寸，并在胸部和手臂中嵌入分布式触觉传感器以同步测量身体表面接触与关节运动。研究者在四个涉及身体表面接触的操控任务上采集示范数据，训练模仿学习策略，并部署到同构机器人上执行。

Result: 实验结果表明，TWINS提供了一个统一的机器人系统，能够完成涉及身体表面接触的操控任务的示范、学习和执行，实现了由身体表面触觉观测引导的操控。

Conclusion: TWINS有效解决了传统末端执行器操控系统的局限性，为涉及身体表面接触的复杂操控任务提供了完整的示范-学习-执行闭环解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning for manipulation have increased the importance of collecting real-world demonstration data. However, existing robotic systems primarily focus on end-effector manipulation, making it difficult to teach and execute manipulation tasks involving body-surface contact with the arms and chest. This paper presents TWINS (Tactile Wearable Isomorphic Arm Networked System), a robotic system for manipulation involving body-surface contact. TWINS consists of a Wearable Dual-Arm Device, which is worn by the operator, and an Isomorphic Robot with the same joint configuration and external dimensions. Distributed tactile sensors embedded in the chest and arms enable the measurement of body-surface contact synchronized with joint motion. Using the Wearable Dual-Arm Device, we collected demonstrations for four manipulation tasks involving body-surface contact. We then trained imitation learning policies using the collected demonstrations and deployed them on the Isomorphic Robot, enabling manipulation guided by body-surface tactile observations. Experimental results demonstrate that TWINS provides a unified robotic system for demonstration, learning, and execution of manipulation involving body-surface contact. https://mmurooka.github.io/twins-project-page/

</details>


### [354] [A Tilt-Rotor UAV with a Gripper for Stable Contact-Based Tasks via Environmental Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01736)
*Joshua Taylor,Nursultan Imanberdiyev,Wei-Yun Yau,Guillaume Sartoretti,Efe Camci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型无人机平台，通过环境锚定机制从自由飞行过渡到稳定约束工作平台。系统结合倾转旋翼机构和欠驱动缆索驱动棱柱夹爪，实验表明锚定后位置漂移RMSE降低超过95%，可承受75N纵向反作用力，稳定性误差不超过3mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人在物理交互任务中保持稳定姿态是重大挑战，限制了其在接触式任务中的应用。需要一种能从不受限飞行过渡到稳定约束工作平台的新型方案。

Method: 系统由两部分组成：1) 带倾转旋翼机构的多旋翼，可解耦俯仰与前进运动，实现非零俯仰角下的稳定悬停；2) 新型欠驱动、缆索驱动的棱柱夹爪，具备顺应性以适应不规则几何形状，通过锚定环境来稳定无人机。

Result: 真实机器人飞行测试结果表明：锚定显著改善交互任务稳定性，相比自由飞行基线位置漂移RMSE降低超过95%（即使有风条件下）；锚定系统可承受高达75N的纵向反作用力并保持稳定姿态；在多种目标几何形状和朝向下，位置漂移RMSE始终不超过3mm。

Conclusion: 研究验证了该方法在复杂物理交互任务（如钻探采样树木健康、在难以到达的位置安装传感器）中的可行性。系统已发布视频供进一步参考。

Abstract: Maintaining a stable pose during physical interaction is a significant challenge for aerial robots, often limiting their use in contact-based tasks. This paper presents a novel uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) platform designed to transition from unconstrained flight to a stable, constrained work platform via environmental anchoring. Our system comprises: 1) a multirotor with a tilt-rotor mechanism that decouples pitch from forward motion, enabling stable hover at non-zero pitch angles, and 2) a novel underactuated, cable-driven, prismatic gripper featuring compliance to adapt to irregular geometries, designed to stabilize the UAV by anchoring it to its environment. We present the design and prototyping of the complete system and validate its performance through a series of real-robot flight tests. Results demonstrate that anchoring significantly improves stability for interaction tasks, reducing positional drift RMSE by over 95% compared to a free-flight baseline, even under windy conditions. The anchored system can withstand longitudinal reaction forces up to 75N while maintaining a stable pose. Furthermore, across a range of target geometries and orientations, the system demonstrated consistent stability with a positional drift RMSE that never exceeded 3mm. These results establish the viability of our approach for complex physical interaction tasks, such as sampling tree health by drilling or sensor installation in hard-to-reach locations. Watch our UAV at: https://youtu.be/HDQ8S4ZW3Ls

</details>


### [355] [Hybrid Impedance-Admittance Control with Multi-Link Aerial Robot for Contact-Rich Surface Sliding Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01800)
*Zicheng Luo,Maolin Lei,Jinjie Li,Yicheng Chen,Zicen Xiong,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多连杆空中机器人的混合阻抗-导纳控制策略，通过关节和旋翼执行器的功能分离，同时实现阻抗控制和导纳控制的优势，从而在未知表面实现鲁棒且柔顺的表面滑动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多连杆空中机器人在接触密集的空中操作任务（如表面滑动）中面临挑战，需要同时具备扰动鲁棒性和对不确定表面几何的柔顺适应性。传统阻抗控制和导纳控制具有相反的力-运动因果性，无法通过同一执行器（如旋翼推力）同时实现。

Method: 利用多连杆空中机器人的关节形态，实现力和运动调节在关节与旋翼执行器之间的功能分离：通过关节角度调节生成导纳行为以增强交互适应性，通过调制旋翼推力实现阻抗行为以调节滑动运动，从而实现两种控制范式的结构协调。

Result: 实验结果表明，该多连杆空中机器人在未知表面上实现了鲁棒且柔顺的滑动性能。

Conclusion: 多连杆空中机器人通过结构上的功能分离，能够同时利用阻抗控制和导纳控制的互补优势，在表面滑动等接触密集操作任务中实现兼具鲁棒性和柔顺性的表现。

Abstract: Multi-link aerial robots can actively deform their articulated structures during flight, giving them strong potential for aerial manipulation. However, they still face substantial challenges in contact-rich aerial manipulation tasks such as surface sliding, which requires both disturbance robustness and compliance to uncertain surface geometry. Force-control strategies such as impedance and admittance control are commonly employed to address these requirements. Although impedance control can provide disturbance-resistant interaction and admittance control can offer compliant adaptation, their opposite force--motion causalities prevent their simultaneous implementation when applied through the same actuation source, such as the rotor thrusts used by conventional aerial robots. To overcome this limitation, we propose a hybrid impedance--admittance control strategy for a multi-link aerial robot. The articulated morphology enables a functional separation of force and motion regulation across joint and rotor actuation sources. In this framework, admittance behavior is generated through joint angle regulation to enhance adaptive interaction, while impedance behavior is achieved by modulating rotor thrust to regulate the sliding motion. This structural coordination allows the robot to leverage the complementary strengths of both control paradigms. As a result, the multi-link aerial robot achieves resilient and adaptive surface sliding. Experimental results demonstrate robust and compliant sliding performance on unknown surfaces.

</details>


### [356] [ReTouch: Empowering Contact-Rich Dexterous Manipulation with Online-Refined Tactile Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01824)
*Shiqi Zhang,Xin Zhang,Yedong Shen,Jiajun Deng,Yuxuan Gao,Sha Zhang,Yuan Zhang,Kaixue Long,Jiajia Wu,Jia Pan,Yao Li,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ReTouch，一种视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA），通过触觉预测在线精炼实现接触丰富的灵巧操作，并引入包含900个真实演示的XHT-Dataset数据集进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉信号融合已被证明对接触丰富的操作有效，但将触觉反馈有效集成到灵巧操作中仍未被充分探索。

Method: ReTouch包含两大创新：（1）Tactile-Patch Encoder将触觉观测编码为保留手指身份和局部接触结构的结构化触觉补丁特征；（2）高频动作模块联合预测未来触觉状态和动作块，并在执行过程中利用传入的触觉反馈进行闭环精炼。此外构建了XHT-Dataset数据集，包含在XHand-UR7e平台上收集的7个接触丰富任务的900个真实演示。

Result: 在标准条件下平均成功率比最强基线高18.4个百分点，在挑战性条件下高23.8个百分点。

Conclusion: ReTouch在真实机器人闭环实验中表现出有效性和鲁棒性，证明了闭环触觉预测精炼对灵巧操作的重要性。

Abstract: Fusing tactile signals has proven effective for contact-rich manipulation, enabling robots to perceive contact states and adapt to rapidly changing physical interactions. Yet effectively integrating tactile feedback into dexterous manipulation remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce ReTouch, a vision-language-action model (VLA) that supports contact-rich dexterous manipulation through tactile predictions continually refined online using execution-time feedback. ReTouch builds on two main innovations for tactile representation and closed-loop action generation. First, its Tactile-Patch Encoder represents tactile observations as structured tactile patch features that preserve finger identity and local contact structure, providing contact cues for fine-grained dexterous control. Second, its high-frequency action module jointly predicts future tactile states and action chunks and refines both using incoming tactile feedback during execution. This closed-loop refinement keeps tactile predictions aligned with evolving physical interactions, enabling responsive action correction and improving robustness to contact changes and execution errors. We further introduce XHT-Dataset, comprising 900 real-world demonstrations across seven contact-rich tasks collected on an XHand--UR7e platform, and evaluate ReTouch through closed-loop real-robot experiments. ReTouch surpasses the strongest baseline by 18.4 and 23.8 percentage points in average success rate under standard and challenging conditions, respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [357] [Multi-View Unified Camera Fields: Geometry-Shaped Action-Facing Representations for RGB-Only Multi-Camera VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01826)
*Jiarui Yang,Yehao Lu,Yuning Su,Yufeng Xie,Yu Zhong,Haiyu Lan,Tianjing Hao,Kaixiang Lu,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Enyu Li,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MVUCF，一个仅训练阶段的框架，通过坐标查询深度目标和预处理感知对应目标，在多视角VLA模型中构建跨视图共享的动作朝向潜在场，提升深度恢复和跨视角匹配能力，部署时无需额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂的接触丰富操作任务往往需要多相机观测来共同捕捉末端执行器、物体和目标，但现有VLA模型在多相机场景下通常简单拼接视角token，导致动作表示在度量深度上较弱且跨相机不一致。

Method: MVUCF通过两个目标直接塑造动作模块消费的隐藏状态：(1)坐标查询深度目标使度量深度可恢复；(2)预处理感知对应目标对齐观测同一物理点的不同相机token。推理时移除深度、相机标定和辅助头，仅使用原始RGB图。

Result: 在匹配GR00T-N1.6设置下，MVUCF在LIBERO上达到98.9%，LIBERO-Plus提升22.4个百分点，六个RoboTwin任务（涵盖触摸、移动放置和接触交互三类动作）成功率平均提升23.3个百分点，真实世界人形机器人实验验证了RGB-only部署的有效性。

Conclusion: MVUCF通过训练阶段的几何信息注入，在不增加任何推理开销的前提下，显著增强了多相机VLA模型的深度恢复和跨视角一致性，从而提升复杂接触操作任务的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation, yet complex contact-rich tasks often benefit from multi-camera observations that jointly capture the end effector, objects, and targets under occlusion. Existing multi-camera VLAs usually concatenate view tokens, leaving action representations weak in metric depth and inconsistent across cameras. We introduce Multi-View Unified Camera Fields (MVUCF), a training-only framework that forms a shared action-facing latent field across views. A coordinate-query depth objective makes metric depth recoverable, while a preprocessing-aware correspondence objective aligns tokens observing the same physical point from different cameras. Both directly shape the hidden states consumed by the action module. After geometry injection, depth, camera calibration, and auxiliary heads are removed, so deployment uses the original RGB-only graph with no extra inference FLOPs. Held-out probes confirm stronger depth recovery and cross-view matching. Under matched GR00T-N1.6 settings, MVUCF reaches 98.9% on LIBERO, improves LIBERO-Plus by 22.4 points, and raises success by 23.3 points across six RoboTwin tasks spanning three action families: touch, move-and-place, and contact interaction. Real-world humanoid experiments further provide evidence of its practical effectiveness under RGB-only deployment.

</details>


### [358] [Teleopit: A Full-Embodiment Humanoid Teleoperation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01834)
*Bingqian Wu,Zicheng Xu,Xianghui Fan,Dayu Li,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Teleopit 是一个基于 VR 的全具身遥操作系统，能够将人体、手部和头部信号映射到人形机器人上，包括可配置的灵巧手和2自由度主动视觉模块，通过历史编码器和失败感知重采样提升运动跟踪性能，并采用优化式手部重定向器无需调参即可适配不同灵巧手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形遥操作系统要么简化手部指令，要么依赖专用可穿戴传感器来获取精细手部运动，缺乏一个统一且高效的全身遥操作解决方案，难以同时协调全身运动、灵巧手控制和视角控制。

Method: Teleopit 将 VR 中的身体、手部和头部信号映射到人形机器人肢体、可配置灵巧手和2自由度主动视觉模块。采用历史编码器和失败感知重采样改进运动跟踪器；基于优化的手部重定向器结合归一化手指方向、指尖闭合和拇指框架对齐，无需调整手部特定目标或求解器超参数即可映射到不同灵巧手。

Result: 组件实验验证了跟踪成功率和重定向行为；真实机器人遥操作展示了协调的移动、操作和视角控制。基于 Teleopit 采集的96次成功演示训练的 ACT 和 GR00T N1.7 策略，在部署到人形机器人上分别达到90.0%和95.0%的任务成功率。

Conclusion: Teleopit 证明了统一的全身遥操作系统能够高效采集高质量演示数据，并成功训练出高性能的机器人操作策略，为灵巧手遥操作提供了无需专用传感器和手动调参的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Humanoid teleoperation for demonstration collection requires coordinated whole-body motion, continuous dexterous hand control, and viewpoint control. Existing systems either simplify hand commands or depend on dedicated wearable sensors for fine-grained hand motion. We introduce Teleopit, a full-embodiment teleoperation system that maps body, hand, and head signals from VR to a humanoid body, configurable dexterous hands, and a 2-DoF active vision module. A history encoder and failure-aware rewind sampling improve the motion tracker on both motion-capture and live VR references. An optimization-based hand retargeter combines normalized finger directions, fingertip closure, and thumb-frame alignment to map human hand motion to different dexterous hands without tuning hand-specific objective or solver hyperparameters. Component experiments evaluate tracking success rate and retargeting behavior, while real-robot teleoperation demonstrates coordinated locomotion, manipulation, and viewpoint control. ACT and GR00T N1.7 policies trained on 96 successful demonstrations collected with Teleopit achieve task success rates of 90.0% and 95.0%, respectively, when deployed on the humanoid. The project page is available at https://botrunner64.github.io/teleopit-page.

</details>


### [359] [Weights or Skills? A Survey of Robot-Learning Techniques: from Action-Predicting Weights to Robots that Write their Own Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01851)
*Gaytri Jena,Kapil Wanaskar,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Vasu Sharma,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这篇综述围绕机器人学习领域的两大分支——"权重派"（将能力烘焙进冻结权重的VLA模型）和"技能派"（将可执行技能编写为代码）——组织分析，重点对代码即策略（code-as-policy）方法按自我改进程度进行深度分类，并讨论了商业技能经济带来的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习领域正在分裂为两种截然不同的技术路线（冻结权重策略模型 vs. 可自我编写的代码技能），但缺乏一个统一的框架来组织和理解这一分裂，也缺乏对"技能"一词多重含义的明确辨析。

Method: 提出"权重vs技能"的分析轴心，将代码即策略方法按自我改进程度从零样本程序合成、闭环自我修复、持久技能记忆到开放进化循环进行排列；对77个代表性系统、六个技术家族进行对照分析，并提供自我改进机制的操作性定义及各技术家族能力边界的说明。

Result: 识别出"技能"一词至少有五种不同含义，其中仅有代码意义上的技能能在无梯度更新情况下自我改进；在自我改进谱系的最高级（结合执行反馈、技能记忆与进化搜索的开放回路）仅有极少数系统（ASPIRE、ENPIRE、RoboClaw）占据；商业技能市场中现有解决方案仅提供静态回放，面临适应、跨形态移植、来源、安全验证、组合和标准化等开放问题。

Conclusion: 这是一个有意聚焦而非穷尽式的综述，通过统一的分类学和对比表格揭示了该领域的关键盲区——自发自我改进的开放循环仍几乎未被占据，同时指出了商业技能经济落地面临的一系列尚未解决的技术挑战。

Abstract: Robot learning is splitting into two bets: policies that bake competence into frozen weights (vision-language-action, or VLA, models), and agents that write and refine their own executable skills as code. This survey organises the field around that axis of weights versus skills. Its central analytical contribution is a deep-dive that arranges code-as-policy methods by their degree of self-improvement, from zero-shot program synthesis, through closed-loop self-repair and persistent skill memory, to the sparsely populated cell in which execution feedback, skill memory, and evolutionary search combine into one open-ended loop; only a few very recent systems (for example ASPIRE, ENPIRE, and RoboClaw) occupy that cell. We map the complementary "skills" pole, from unsupervised reinforcement-learning skill discovery to large-language-model skill libraries, and show that the word "skill" is used in at least five distinct senses, of which only the code sense self-improves without gradient updates. We then connect the taxonomy to the emerging skill economy: commercial robot-skill marketplaces now distribute one-tap skills across robots but ship only static playback, which surfaces open problems of adaptation, cross-embodiment portability, provenance, safety verification, composition, and standardisation. This is a deliberately focused survey. Rather than cataloguing the field exhaustively, it examines 77 representative systems across six technique families through one taxonomy and a set of contrast tables, and it supplies operational definitions of the self-improvement mechanisms together with a statement of what each family cannot do.

</details>


### [360] [RADAR Perception for Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance onboard small-scale Quadrotor UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01855)
*Dnyandeep Mandaokar,Bernhard Rinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于毫米波雷达的无人机快速动态避障感知-控制系统，结合交互多模型跟踪器和控制障碍函数控制器，实现了端到端延迟约14ms的实时避障，在390次实验中位置误差均小于0.15-0.93m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机快速动态避障不仅需要低延迟的控制和驱动，还需要可靠的感知能力和足够的感知范围来准确检测障碍物和估计速度，现有方案在感知-控制一体化和延迟方面存在不足。

Method: 推导了感知范围、相对速度和控制延迟之间的延迟与空间界限，建立充分避障条件；采用交互多模型（IMM）的轻量级跟踪器进行目标跟踪，基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的控制器直接输出规避加速度。

Result: 在300次实验中（三种物体尺寸、不同光照条件），x、y、z方向位置误差分别小于0.15m、0.93m和0.87m；在烟雾条件下90次实验具有类似的误差分布。在树莓派4B上的板载实现端到端感知到指令延迟约为14ms。

Conclusion: 该系统是首个在快速动态避障任务中验证的毫米波雷达感知-控制一体化方案，证明了在恶劣视觉条件下（如烟雾）仍能保持可靠的避障性能，且实时可行。

Abstract: Fast dynamic obstacle avoidance (DOA) on uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) demands not only low-latency control and actuation but also reliable perception with sufficient sensing range for accurate obstacle detection and speed estimation. This letter presents, to the best of our knowledge, the first mmWave RADAR-based perception-and-control system for fast onboard DOA. We derive and analyze latency and spatial bounds that relate sensing range, relative speed, and control delay, yielding sufficient conditions for successful avoidance. Our system adopts a lightweight tracker based on interacting multiple models and a controller based on control-barrier functions that directly outputs evasive accelerations. It achieves position errors of less than 0.15 m, 0.93 m, and 0.87 m in x, y, and z directions for 300 experiments with three different object sizes and varying visibility (light and dark), and a similar spread for 90 experiments in smoke. An onboard implementation on a Raspberry Pi 4B demonstrates real-time feasibility with an end-to-end sensing-to-command latency of approximately 14 ms. Code and the full dataset of 390 throws are available (https://tinyurl.com/radardoagit).

</details>


### [361] [World Action Models in Real Time: An Empirical Study of Smooth Execution via Asynchronous Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01880)
*Motubrain Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对World Action Models固定步长动作块生成的推理延迟问题，系统比较了六种异步部署策略（同步执行、纯异步切换、事后动作混合、去噪时混合、推理时速度引导和前缀条件生成），在10 Hz双臂机器人上进行离线轨迹分析和在线实验评估。研究发现时间对齐是异步部署的关键基础，对齐误差会产生持续的块边界不连续且无法仅靠混合修正；前缀条件生成在学习一致动作延续方面表现最佳，在任务性能、执行速度和轨迹平滑性之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models通过迭代去噪生成固定时域的动作块，造成显著推理延迟，导致机器人执行过程中出现停顿、动作过时和动作不连续等问题，需要探索异步部署策略来重叠推理和执行过程，实现响应式且平滑的控制。

Method: 在10 Hz双臂机器人平台上系统比较六种部署策略：同步执行、纯异步切换、事后动作混合、去噪时混合、推理时速度引导和前缀条件生成。评估结合离线轨迹分析和在线实验，覆盖动态操作、精度关键放置和长时程任务三种场景。

Result: 观测、预测和实际执行命令之间的精确时间对齐是根本要求，对齐误差会产生持续的块边界不连续且无法仅通过混合修正；直接动作加权在正确对齐下是简单平滑的基线但在精度关键任务中牺牲精度；推理时速度引导在我们平台上无法可靠约束已提交动作；前缀条件生成在任务性能、执行速度和轨迹平滑性上取得最佳整体平衡。

Conclusion: 异步部署策略在现实机器人系统中的权衡已被阐明，前缀条件生成是最优方案，它通过在训练阶段学习一致的动作延续来避免推理时的混合或引导需求，为部署高延迟World Action Models到实时机器人系统提供了实用指导。

Abstract: World Action Models generate fixed-horizon action chunks through iterative denoising, creating substantial inference latency that can cause pauses, stale actions, and discontinuities during robotic execution. We present an empirical study of asynchronous deployment strategies that overlap model inference with action execution to enable responsive and smooth control. We compare six strategies, including synchronous execution, pure asynchronous switching, post-hoc action blending, denoising-time blending, inference-time velocity guidance, and prefix-conditioned generation, on a 10 Hz bimanual robot. Evaluation combines offline trajectory analysis with online experiments across dynamic manipulation, precision-critical placement, and long-horizon tasks. Our results identify accurate temporal alignment between observations, predictions, and executed commands as a fundamental requirement. Alignment errors produce persistent chunk-boundary discontinuities that cannot be corrected through blending alone. With proper alignment, direct action weighting provides a simple and smooth baseline but sacrifices accuracy in precision-critical tasks. Inference-time velocity guidance fails to reliably constrain committed actions on our platform. In contrast, prefix-conditioned generation achieves the best overall balance between task performance, execution speed, and trajectory smoothness by learning consistent action continuations during training. These findings clarify the practical trade-offs among asynchronous deployment strategies and provide guidance for deploying high-latency World Action Models in real-time robotic systems.

</details>


### [362] [FRA-NBV: A Fast and Reflectivity-Aware Next-Best-View Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01950)
*G. F. Preziosa,E. Setti,M. Faroni,A. M. Zanchettin,P. Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速反射感知的下一最佳视角（FRA-NBV）策略，用于解决深度传感器在反射表面下产生测量缺失的问题。该方法无需先验物体模型或材质反射假设，通过在线椭球体表示定位反射区域，并选择额外视角改变入射角以提高重建覆盖率，实验验证在工业条件下显著改善了重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主3D重建中，反射表面会导致深度传感器测量缺失或不可靠，使传统NBV策略失效。这一问题在包含反射部件的工业应用以及低成本低分辨率深度传感场景中尤为关键，因此迫切需要一种对传感故障具有鲁棒性的重建策略。

Method: 通过缺失深度测量的空间分布识别反射区域，利用基于椭球体的在线物体估计表示在三维空间中定位这些区域；随后采用恢复策略，选择额外拍摄位姿以改变传感器入射角，从而提高对受影响区域的重建可能性。该方法不依赖先验物体模型或材质反射率假设。

Result: 在具有不同几何和反射复杂度的物体上进行了实验，结果表明该方法在现实的工业条件下显著提高了重建覆盖率。

Conclusion: FRA-NBV策略有效解决了反射引起的深度缺失问题，无需先验模型假设即可适用于广泛的工业场景，为反射表面环境下的自主3D重建提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous 3D reconstruction with depth sensors is strongly affected by reflective surfaces, which cause missing or unreliable measurements and reduce the effectiveness of conventional Next-Best-View (NBV) strategies. This limitation is particularly critical in industrial applications involving reflective components and low-cost, low-resolution depth sensing, where robustness to sensing failures is essential. This paper proposes a Fast Reflectivity-Aware Next-Best-View (FRA-NBV) strategy that explicitly addresses reflection-induced depth loss without relying on prior object models or assumptions on material reflectance, making it suitable for a wide range of industrial configurations. Reflective regions are identified from the spatial distribution of missing depth measurements and localized in three-dimensional space using an online ellipsoid-based representation of the object estimate. A recovery strategy then selects additional poses that modify the sensor's angle of incidence to improve the likelihood of reconstructing the affected regions. Experiments on objects with different geometric and reflective complexity demonstrate that the approach significantly improves reconstruction coverage under realistic industrial conditions.

</details>


### [363] [Roomer: Reflective Object-Grounded Model Editing and Repair for 3D Indoor Layout Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01973)
*Lingwei Dang,Ziyan Qiu,Jiajia Cheng,Shishuo Shang,Zhenhao Zhang,Yufei Zhu,Qingxin Xiao,Pan Liu,Shenghui Huang,Yun Hao,Juntong Li,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Roomer是一个反射式修复框架，将室内布局中的局部违规（如碰撞、越界、开口阻塞和流通受阻）转化为稀疏的、以物体为中心的修复问题，通过RoState编码布局、RoReview绑定违规对象、几何条件VLM规划局部编辑，并辅以确定性求解器验证和候选编辑生成，最终通过全场景验证确保修复有效且不引入新问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内布局生成器虽能生成全局合理的布局，但存在局部违规问题；以往工作专注于全场景合成或场景级优化，缺乏对责任对象的识别和局部修复支持。

Method: 提出反射式修复框架Roomer：1) 用RoState编码布局状态；2) 用RoReview将检测到的违规绑定到相关对象；3) 使用几何条件视觉语言模型规划结构化局部编辑；4) 确定性求解器验证编辑并生成候选编辑集合；5) 全场景验证确保候选编辑能解决目标违规且不引入新违规。训练数据来自Roomer-CC受控破坏数据集。

Result: Roomer在修复残余违规的同时保持有效区域，提升了物理有效性和实用性，并且能够跨外部生成器迁移。

Conclusion: Roomer通过对象级稀疏修复实现了对室内布局局部违规的有效修复，弥补了现有方法在责任对象识别和局部修复方面的不足。

Abstract: Existing indoor layout generators produce globally plausible layouts yet may retain local violations such as collisions, out-of-bounds placements, obstructed openings, and blocked circulation. Most prior work focuses on full-scene synthesis or scene-level optimization, with limited support for identifying responsible objects and locally repairing affected regions. We present Roomer, a reflective repair framework that casts these violations as sparse, object-grounded repair problems. Roomer encodes layouts as ``RoState'' and uses ``RoReview'' to bind measured violations to implicated objects. A geometry-conditioned vision-language model planner proposes a structured local edit, while a deterministic solver validates it and generates a finite set of candidate edits when needed. Each candidate is committed only if full-scene verification confirms that it resolves the target violation without new hard violations or broken protected constraints. We train the planner on Roomer-CC, a controlled-corruption dataset that pairs faulty layouts with object-grounded violation evidence and known-feasible inverse StatePatches. Since existing benchmarks rarely assess whether physically valid layouts are usable, we introduce Roomer-Eval to assess distributional quality, physical validity, and practical usability. Experiments show that Roomer repairs residual violations while preserving valid regions, improves physical validity and usability, and transfers across external generators.

</details>


### [364] [Adaptive Human-Robot Collaborative Painting Combining Preference-Based Optimization and Dynamic Motion Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01981)
*C. Cella,M. Ristic,M. Faroni,A. M. Zanchettin,P. Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以人为中心的协作框架，结合偏好优化（PBO）和动态运动基元（DMPs）来优化机器人辅助涂装任务，通过人类反馈迭代调整控制参数，实时适应操作者手部方向，实验表明该方法能有效减少操作者负担并优化过程结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助任务中，传统方法难以平衡自动化效率和人类操作者的个性化需求与舒适度，需要一种能够根据人类偏好实时调整机器人行为的自适应框架。

Method: 融合基于偏好的优化（利用GLISp算法）和修改后的动态运动基元（DMPs），通过人类反馈迭代优化执行时间、机器人响应性和旋转放大等控制参数，使机器人实时调整工件方向以匹配操作者手部方向。

Result: 在不同参与者的涂装任务验证中，该策略有效减少了操作者的体力消耗，同时优化了任务产出效果。

Conclusion: 该方法成功证明了人机协作中偏好优化与动态运动基元结合的可行性，可在降低操作者负担的同时提升任务整体效果。

Abstract: This work presents a human-centered collaborative framework that integrates Preference-Based Optimization (PBO) and Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) to optimize robot-assisted tasks such as painting. The system allows the operator to perform the process while the robot adapts its behavior in real-time, dynamically adjusting the orientation of the piece in order to match the orientation of the operator's hand. The PBO framework leverages the GLISp algorithm to iteratively refine control parameters such as execution time, robot responsiveness, and rotation amplification through human feedback. Moreover, DMPs have been modified to enhance the reactive behavior of the robot and its adaptability to ergonomic requirements. The method was validated with a heterogeneous group of participants executing \rev{painting tasks}. The results show that our strategy effectively reduces operator effort while optimizing process outcomes.

</details>


### [365] [MANGO-Grasp: Mahalanobis Fields over Geometry-Oriented 3D Gaussians for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02014)
*Heng Zhang,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Mike Zheng Shou,Weisi Lin,Yan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MANGO-Grasp提出了一种各向异性交互框架，用几何导向的3D高斯基元表示物体、用形态-运动学描述符表示机器手，并通过马氏距离场实现跨体现的灵巧抓取，在多个基准和真实实验中取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨体现灵巧抓取方法中，物体表示往往未能充分表征局部表面几何，而机器人描述符也没有同时显式编码形态和运动学信息，导致抓取效果受限。

Method: 将物体表示为按几何复杂度自适应分配的、具有外法线的表面对齐板状3D高斯基元；将机器手表示为编码形态-运动学描述符的表面关键点。以关键点-基元对上的马氏距离场作为训练预测目标和推理时优化引导，该场沿表面法向急剧上升、在切平面内平缓变化，以匹配接触的方向性结构。所有体现共享同一优化公式和超参数设置。

Result: 在CMAP和MultiGripperGrasp基准上，较最强已见手基线最多提升8.24个百分点；零样本迁移到未见SharpaWave手时较最强零样本基线最多提升16.57个百分点；真实实验中达到86%成功率。

Conclusion: MANGO-Grasp通过显式建模物体局部几何和机器手形态-运动学信息，以马氏距离场实现对各向异性接触的精准建模，在仿真和真实场景中均展现出优秀的跨体现泛化能力。

Abstract: Cross-embodiment dexterous grasping aims to synthesize stable grasps across heterogeneous multi-fingered hands with little or no embodiment-specific tuning. Existing interaction-centric methods achieve promising results, but their object representations often underrepresent local surface geometry, while their robot descriptors do not explicitly encode both robot morphology and kinematics. We propose MANGO-Grasp, an anisotropic interaction framework that represents objects as geometry-oriented 3D Gaussian primitives and robot hands as surface keypoints encoded into morpho-kinematic descriptors. The object primitives are adaptively allocated by geometric complexity and shaped as surface-aligned plates with outward normals, encoding local geometry. Mahalanobis fields over keypoint--primitive pairs serve as interaction prediction targets during training and as optimization guidance for grasp realization at inference. These fields rise sharply for displacement along the surface normal but only gently within the tangent plane, matching the directional structure of contact. Grasps are realized with one shared optimization formulation and hyperparameter setting across all embodiments. On the CMAP and MultiGripperGrasp benchmarks, MANGO-Grasp outperforms the strongest seen-hand baseline by up to 8.24 percentage points in simulation. It also transfers zero-shot to the unseen SharpaWave hand, improving over the strongest zero-shot baseline by up to 16.57 percentage points, and achieves 86% success in real-world experiments. The code and additional materials will be made available upon publication at https://connor-zh.github.io/MANGO-Grasp/.

</details>


### [366] [Open-DiffLoco: Open-Source Differentiable Learning for Deployable Blind Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02069)
*Martin Opat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Open-DiffLoco是首个开源的可微仿真四足机器人运动策略训练框架，基于MuJoCo XLA中的SHAC算法，无需参考轨迹和复杂奖励设计，即可在约20-60分钟内训练出可部署到真实硬件（Unitree Go2）的盲态运动策略，速度超过1 m/s，速度跟踪RMSE低于0.2 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习训练可部署的运动策略需要复杂的奖励工程和昂贵的训练时间，而可微仿真是高效替代方案，但缺乏能够端到端迁移到真实硬件的开源工具。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 框架在MuJoCo XLA (MJX)中实现Short-Horizon Actor-Critic (SHAC)算法，训练基于本体感觉的策略，去除特权观测（如线速度）且不依赖参考轨迹，使用大幅简化的奖励函数。此外提出Jacobian-Augmented Value Estimation (JAVE)算法扩展，通过监督critic的雅可比矩阵来改进早期一阶策略梯度训练。

Result: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人上部署成功，跟踪全方位速度指令的RMSE低于0.2 m/s，速度超过1 m/s，对不平地形和外部物理干扰具有鲁棒性。训练在单张NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5080 GPU上使用不到6 GB显存，耗时约20-60分钟。

Conclusion: Open-DiffLoco证明了可微仿真能够高效训练可直接部署到真实硬件的运动策略，无需复杂奖励设计或参考轨迹，为四足机器人运动学习提供了首个开源可微仿真部署框架，并提出了JAVE算法进一步提升了训练性能。

Abstract: Developing deployable locomotion policies through conventional reinforcement learning often requires complex reward engineering and expensive training times. While differentiable simulation offers a highly efficient alternative, open-source tools capable of end-to-end transfer of these policies to physical hardware remain limited. This paper introduces Open-DiffLoco, an open-source framework for training deployable blind quadruped locomotion policies with differentiable simulation. The framework implements the Short-Horizon Actor-Critic (SHAC) algorithm in MuJoCo XLA (MJX) and trains a proprioceptive policy that transfers to real-world hardware. The deployed policy removes privileged actor observations, including base linear velocity, and does not rely on reference trajectories. It also uses a substantially simplified reward function, enabling the robot to discover walking patterns without the complex auxiliary rewards typically used in conventional reinforcement learning pipelines. When deployed on physical hardware (a Unitree Go2 quadruped), the trained policy tracks omnidirectional velocity commands with root-mean-square error below 0.2 m/s, reaches speeds above 1 m/s, and remains robust to uneven terrain and external physical disturbances, such as lateral pushes. Across the reported configurations, training uses under 6 GB of VRAM on a single NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5080 GPU and completes in approximately 20-60 minutes. As an algorithmic extension to SHAC, we propose Jacobian-Augmented Value Estimation (JAVE), which supervises the critic Jacobians to improve early first-order policy-gradient training. To our knowledge, Open-DiffLoco is the first open-source framework for training deployable locomotion policies using differentiable simulation. Deployment videos and source code are available at: https://diffloco.martin-opat.com/

</details>


### [367] [TANGO-VIO: Triangulation-Aware Navigation with Guaranteed Feature-Observability for Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02079)
*Ege C. Altunkaya,Abdülbaki Şanlan,Emre Koyuncu,İbrahim Özkol*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TANGO-VIO，一种三角测量感知的导航框架，通过将特征级堆叠轴承矩阵的对数行列式度量嵌入控制障碍函数，在低视差运动下主动维持特征可观测性，并通过软硬件在环仿真与真实飞行实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉辅助导航和视觉惯性里程计中，三角化三维特征位置的质量是状态估计精度的基本前提。当相机发生纯旋转无平移运动或观测轴承向量视差不足时，三角化会变得病态甚至不可能。尽管视觉惯性里程计已被广泛研究，但在导航过程中主动维护特征可观测性尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出TANGO-VIO框架，将特征级堆叠轴承矩阵的对数行列式度量嵌入控制障碍函数，通过名义方向加权的最小偏差速度修正，对聚合三角化信息度量施加下界，从而在特征几何意义上建立可观测性保证。

Result: 通过软件在环仿真和真实飞行实验评估，结果表明在低视差运动下三角化条件得到改善，飞行响应与仿真行为高度吻合，证实了所提安全滤波器的实际可实现性。

Conclusion: TANGO-VIO有效解决了导航过程中主动维护特征可观测性的问题，在低视差场景下改善了三角化条件，并通过真实实验验证了方法的实用性与可靠性。

Abstract: In vision-aided navigation and visual-inertial odometry, the quality of triangulated three-dimensional feature positions is a fundamental prerequisite for state estimation accuracy. Triangulation becomes ill-conditioned or even impossible when a camera undergoes pure rotation without translation, or when the observed bearing vectors provide insufficient parallax. Even though visual-inertial odometry has been extensively studied, the active maintenance of feature-observability during navigation has not been sufficiently addressed in the literature. To address this gap, this study presents TANGO-VIO, a triangulation-aware navigation framework that embeds a log-determinant metric of the feature-wise stacked-bearing matrix into a control barrier function. In this proposed method, the observability guarantee is established in the feature-geometric sense by enforcing a lower bound on the aggregate triangulation-information metric through a nominal-direction-weighted minimum-deviation velocity correction. The proposed architecture is evaluated through software-inthe- loop simulations and real flight experiments. The results show improved triangulation conditioning under low-parallax motion, while the flight response closely reproduces the corresponding simulation behavior and confirms the practical realizability of the proposed safety filter. Supplementary materials are available on the project webpage.

</details>


### [368] [Toward Geometry-Scalable Whole-Body Touch for Humanoids: A 3D-Printed Conformal EIT Skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02080)
*Haofeng Chen,Carson Kohlbrenner,Jiri Kubik,Lukas Rustler,Alexander Dickhans,Karel Bartunek,Alessandro Roncone,Hyosang Lee,Matej Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于电阻抗断层成像（EIT）的共形触觉皮肤，通过几何自适应增材制造工艺实现，无需形态特异性重新设计即可覆盖大面积人体形态，弧形传感器实现平均6mm定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统taxel触觉阵列在表面积扩大时面临布线复杂、曲率适配困难等问题，难以满足人形机器人在接触密集型环境中的全身触觉感知需求。

Method: 采用导电TPU层形成连续传感域，通过接触诱导的导电贴片耦合产生边界电压变化，利用一步Gauss-Newton EIT求解器进行重建；设计包含低电阻接触增强贴片和多孔导电TPU传感层，兼顾灵敏度与可打印性。

Result: 在平面、U形曲面及iCub人脸形状原型上验证了接触定位能力；弧形传感器在18个接触位置上实现平均6mm的定位误差，无需监督后处理。

Conclusion: 增材制造的断层扫描触觉皮肤可减少人形机器人触觉覆盖的形态特异性重新设计负担，为大面积接触传感提供实用路径，支持面向人类环境的部署。

Abstract: Whole-body tactile sensing is a prerequisite for humanoids that operate in contact-rich human environments, but conventional taxel arrays scale poorly with surface area, wiring complexity, and robot-specific curvature. We present a conformal electrical impedance tomography tactile skin fabricated through a geometry-adaptable additive-manufacturing workflow. A flexible conductive TPU layer forms a continuous sensing domain, while contact-induced coupling with conductive patches produces boundary voltage changes that are reconstructed using a one-step Gauss-Newton EIT solver. We first characterize the electromechanical design space of the layered structure and show that low-resistance contact-enhancement patches and a porous conductive TPU sensing layer improve sensitivity while preserving printability. We then validate contact localization on a planar prototype, a curved U-shaped prototype, and a qualitative iCub-face-shaped geometry. The curved sensor achieves a mean localization error of 6 mm over 18 contact positions without supervised post-processing. These results suggest that additively manufactured tomographic skins can reduce the morphology-specific redesign burden for humanoid tactile coverage and provide a practical route toward large-area contact sensing for human-centered deployment.

</details>


### [369] [Dynamic UAV-based search operations using probabilistic diffusion modeling of Man Overboard incident victims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02093)
*Dimosthenis Angelis,Evangelos Boukas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用UAV技术预测和搜索海上落水人员的策略。通过扩展卡尔曼滤波结合Leeway模型预测落水人员可能的漂移区域，并评估了五种搜索方法，其中改进的概率信息搜索（IPIS）方法即使在落水后20分钟才启动搜索、检测成功率仅30%的情况下，仍能达到80%以上的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2010-2019年间，超过70%的游轮落水人员不幸丧生，迫切需要一种可靠的搜索与救援策略来提高落水人员的生还率。

Method: 使用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）结合Leeway模型预测落水人员的搜索区域；利用UAV在该动态区域进行搜索；提出了五种搜索方法：Zigzag、Boustrophedon、Spiral、Probability Informed Search和Improved Probability Informed Search（IPIS），并在模拟环境中进行评估。

Result: IPIS方法在落水事件发生20分钟后才启动UAV搜索任务、且检测方法成功率仅30%的前提下，仍能实现超过80%的平均成功率。所有代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于EKF和Leeway模型的搜索区域预测方法以及IPIS搜索策略能够显著提高海上落水人员的搜索成功率，为实际搜救任务提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: More than 70% of the people that fell overboard cruise ships in the period 2010-2019 lost their lives. This paper presents a strategy for reliably predicting the area a person may be in after a man overboard incident, and describes in detail the search methods to find them utilizing UAV technology. The search area prediction method employs an Extended Kalman Filter that capitalizes on the information from the Leeway model to track the missing person in the sea by taking into account the uncertainty of the movement of the person and the weather conditions in the area. Then, a UAV uses this information to search for the person. Five different methods for searching in this dynamic area are presented and evaluated - the Zigzag, the Boustrophedon, the Spiral, the Probability Informed Search and the Improved Probability Informed Search (IPIS) methods. The IPIS method provides success rate of over 80% on average for finding a person, even if the UAV initiates the search mission 20 minutes after the man overboard incident and even assuming a detection method with a success rate of 30%. All code for the simulation environment and the evaluation of the methods is available on our GitHub page at https://github.com/diangeli/pdms-man-overboard.

</details>


### [370] [Look Where It Matters: Adaptive Visual Refinement for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02197)
*Jin Cui,Yanbin Hu,Xinyue Long,Linkai Li,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AtVLA通过向视觉编码器插入可学习的寄存器token（register tokens），并耦合不确定性门控的局部细化机制，解决了VLA模型在机器人精细操作中视觉表征不可靠的问题，在LIBERO和真实世界基准上分别将成功率从94.2%提升至98.4%、从46.5%提升至69.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型的视觉表征在空间精确的机器人操作中不可靠。作者发现VLA中的视觉编码器也存在通用Vision Transformer中已记录的注意力伪影，且这些伪影与后训练阶段获得的空间感知能力密切相关：有限的全局token容量导致任务相关信息（如物体位置、深度排序、局部几何）溢出到低信息补丁token中。

Method: AtVLA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 在视觉编码器中插入可学习的寄存器token，仅使用具身数据和原始动作目标进行端到端训练，使这些寄存器成为具身空间信息的专用载体，恢复其余补丁token的干净的注意力分布；(2) 不确定性门控的局部细化——动作专家采样多个动作块并从分歧估计不确定性，仅对不确定的预测，通过动作条件注意力rollout识别任务相关区域，将其裁剪并以高分辨率重新编码，附加到缓存前缀中以生成细化动作。

Result: 在LIBERO、SimplerEnv和挑战性的单视角真实世界基准上，AtVLA将LIBERO平均成功率从94.2%提高到98.4%，真实世界成功率从46.5%提高到69.0%。裁剪仅在约30%的重规划步骤中被触发，在代表性部署设置下总计算量仅为基础模型的1.4-1.6倍。

Conclusion: 干净的注意力分布恢复了可靠的目标定位，但无法恢复低分辨率观测中丢失的几何细节，因此AtVLA将注意力矫正与不确定性门控的局部细化相结合，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了空间精确操作的性能。

Abstract: Visual representations of VLA models remain unreliable for spatially precise robotic manipulation. We uncover that vision encoders in VLAs also exhibit attention artifacts previously documented in generic Vision Transformers, and further show that, in embodied policies, these artifacts are closely associated with spatial perception capabilities acquired during post-training. As the encoder learns task-relevant information such as object location, depth ordering, and local geometry, limited global-token capacity causes part of this information to spill into low-information patch tokens. We introduce AtVLA, a framework that inserts learnable register tokens into the visual encoder. Trained end-to-end using only embodied data and the original action objective, these registers emerge as dedicated carriers of embodied spatial information, while the remaining patch tokens recover clean and spatially faithful attention distributions crucial for precise target localization and fine-grained contact. Clean attention restores reliable localization, but cannot recover geometric details lost in low-resolution observations. AtVLA therefore couples attention rectification with uncertainty-gated local refinement. The action expert samples multiple action chunks and estimates uncertainty from their disagreement; only for uncertain predictions, action-conditioned attention rollout identifies the task-relevant region, which is cropped, re-encoded at high resolution, and appended to the cached prefix for refined action generation. Across LIBERO, SimplerEnv, and a challenging single-view real-world benchmark, AtVLA improves the average LIBERO success rate from 94.2% to 98.4% and real-world success from 46.5% to 69.0%. The cropping is triggered on approximately 30% of replanning steps, resulting in only 1.4-1.6x the total computation of the base model under the representative deployment setting.

</details>


### [371] [Learning Panorama-Aware VLA for Mobile Manipulation with Whole-Body Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02257)
*Donglin Yang,Haoran Chen,Xingyu Chen,Lixing Liu,Manyi Li,Changhe Tu,Ke Xu,Xiaojian Ma,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套全身遥操作系统和全景感知VLA策略（PanoVLA），用于轮式双臂移动机器人的操作。通过VR界面实现全身协调控制并采集5.5小时多模态演示数据，PanoVLA基于Mixture-of-Transformers架构引入全景编码与融合模块，在四类真实移动操作任务中实现了91.3%的阶段完成率和73.4%的端到端成功率，显著优于仅依赖局部相机观测的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作面临两大挑战：数据层面，高效采集高质量全身演示需要协调控制移动底盘和机械臂；模型层面，现有VLA模型主要依赖局部相机观测，视野有限，阻碍全局空间理解。这两个挑战分别对应数据采集效率和空间感知能力的不足。

Method: 开发了一个全身遥操作系统，通过单一VR界面实现对轮式双臂机器人的协调控制，采集了5.5小时真实世界移动操作数据集。在此基础上提出PanoVLA策略，采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构，通过专用的全景编码和融合模块引入全局空间上下文，将全景观测与语言指令和机器人状态有效整合用于动作生成。

Result: 在四个真实世界移动操作任务上的评估显示，PanoVLA实现了平均91.3%的阶段完成率和73.4%的端到端成功率，显著优于基于局部视野的基线方法。

Conclusion: 引入全景空间上下文能够提升移动机器人的空间理解和闭环操作性能，验证了全景感知在移动操作VLA策略中的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation is a key capability for embodied intelligence, enabling robots to accomplish complex multi-stage tasks in open-world environments. However, mobile manipulation poses two key challenges for vision-language-action (VLA) policies: At the data level, the efficient collection of high-quality whole-body demonstrations demands the coordinated control of both the mobile base and the robotic arms; at the model level, existing VLA models predominantly rely on local camera observations, whose limited field of view hinders global spatial understanding. To address these challenges, we develop a whole-body teleoperation system and a panoramic-aware VLA policy. The system enables coordinated control of a wheeled bimanual robot through a single VR interface and supports the acquisition of a real-world mobile manipulation dataset comprising 5.5 hours of multimodal demonstrations. Building upon this dataset, we propose PanoVLA, a panorama-aware vision-language-action policy for mobile bimanual manipulation. Built upon a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture, PanoVLA introduces global spatial context through dedicated panorama encoding and fusion modules, enabling effective integration of panoramic observations with language instructions and robot states for action generation. Evaluation on four real-world mobile manipulation tasks demonstrates that PanoVLA achieves an average stage completion rate of 91.3\% and an end-to-end success rate of 73.4\%, substantially outperforming local-view baselines. These results demonstrate that incorporating panoramic spatial context improves spatial understanding and closed-loop manipulation performance in mobile robots.

</details>


### [372] [TS-MAMP: A Remanufactured Agricultural Robot Powered by Second-Life EV Components and NMS-Free On-Device Weed Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02270)
*Weijie Shi,Zicheng Xu,Zhenbang Cheng,Haoran Xuan,Mingbo Duan,Gan Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-MAMP（伸缩套筒模块化农业移动平台），一种基于3R循环经济原则、利用退役低速电动车（LSEV）部件再制造而成的农业机器人。通过配对退役48V无刷直流轮毂电机和筛选60%-80%健康度的铅酸电池模块，动力及底盘成本降低约60%（低于450美元）。平台采用桁架底盘，支持≥200kg静态负载、1200-2000mm可调轮距及≤5分钟模块更换。搭载NMS-free YOLOv10n检测器，在万西作物-杂草数据集上达到80.87% mAP@0.5，部署于Jetson Nano验证了端侧推理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业4.0机器人虽提升田间效率，但对占全球农业主导地位的小农分散地块而言资本密集度过高。与此同时，大量退役低速电动车动力系统仍具机电价值却被破坏性回收。论文旨在弥合这一矛盾，探索利用退役EV部件再制造成本可负担的农业机器人。

Method: (1) 采用3R循环经济原则再制造退役部件：通过反电动势匹配配对退役48V BLDC轮毂电机，筛选SOH为60%-80%的铅酸电池模块并在100mV模块间压差内主动均衡；(2) 设计桁架底盘，实现≥200kg静态负载、1200-2000mm连续可调轮距及≤5分钟模块更换；(3) 开发基于NMS-free YOLOv10n的杂草检测模型，采用一致双分配训练与负样本学习，通过FP16 TensorRT部署到Jetson Nano。

Result: 动力及底盘BOM成本降低约60%，低于450美元；平台静态负载≥200kg，轮距可调范围1200-2000mm，模块更换≤5分钟；YOLOv10n检测器在万西作物-杂草数据集上实现80.87% mAP@0.5（58.41% mAP@0.5:0.95），并在Jetson Nano上成功部署验证端侧推理可行性。

Conclusion: TS-MAMP证明退役EV部件在适度筛选下可被重新工程化为经济实惠的AI农业机器人，为商业自动化尚未覆盖的小农地块开辟了再制造路径，在可持续性与农业智能化之间建立了可行桥梁。

Abstract: Agriculture 4.0 robotic systems improve field efficiency yet remain too capital-intensive for the fragmented smallholdings that dominate global agriculture. Meanwhile, a growing number of retired low-speed electric-vehicle (LSEV) powertrains retain functional electromechanical value but are destructively recycled. This paper presents TS-MAMP (Telescopic-Sleeve Modular Agricultural Mobile Platform), a remanufactured robot built under 3R (reduce, reuse, recycle) circular-economy principles. Retired 48 V brushless-DC (BLDC) hub motors are paired via back-EMF matching, and lead-acid battery modules screened at 60%-80% state of health are actively balanced within a 100 mV inter-module voltage deviation. Together, these reused components reduce the powertrain-and-chassis BOM cost by approximately 60%, to below USD 450 (perception and weeding modules excluded). The truss chassis provides >=200 kg static load, continuously adjustable track width from 1200 mm to 2000 mm, and <=5-minute module changeover. An NMS-free (non-maximum-suppression-free) YOLOv10n detector with consistent dual-assignment training and negative-sample learning achieves 80.87% mean average precision (mAP)@0.5 (58.41% mAP@0.5:0.95) on the Wanxi Crop-Weed dataset, and is deployed via FP16 TensorRT on a Jetson Nano, confirming on-device inference feasibility. TS-MAMP demonstrates that retired EV components, under modest screening, can be re-engineered into affordable, AI-enabled agricultural robots--opening a remanufacturing pathway for the smallholder fields that commercial automation leaves unserved.

</details>


### [373] [TRACE: Ergodic Trajectory Optimization for Active Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02304)
*Ziyue Zheng,Linli Shi,Bingkun He,Wen Jiang,Ziyun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE系统，将主动重建问题重新定义为遍历覆盖问题，使传感器轨迹的时间平均空间统计与地图推断的目标信息分布相匹配，通过核遍历水平规划器结合梯度流和足迹耗散，在Replica数据集上相比NBV基线PSNR提升1.5dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯溅射地图的主动重建系统采用贪婪的单步下一最佳视角选择策略，将视角选择与路径规划解耦，忽略了场景信息的全局结构，导致轨迹低效、在视角之间浪费感知能力。

Method: 将主动重建建模为遍历覆盖问题：从当前地图的不确定性和可见性在线推导目标信息分布，通过带梯度流和足迹耗散的核遍历水平规划器计算遍历轨迹，闭合并结束映射与轨迹优化之间的循环。

Result: 在Replica数据集上与NBV基线相比，PSNR提升1.5dB。

Conclusion: TRACE通过遍历覆盖框架实现了全局优化的主动重建轨迹，有效提升了重建质量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing active reconstruction systems with Gaussian-splatting maps select observations greedily, optimizing a single next-best-view (NBV) at each step and connecting the chosen views by short-horizon path planning. This greedy decoupling disregards the global structure of scene information, producing inefficient trajectories that waste sensing capacity in transit between selected views. In this work, we study active reconstruction as an ergodic coverage problem: the time-averaged spatial statistics of the sensor trajectory should match a target information distribution induced by the current map. Our approach derives this target distribution online from uncertainty and visibility, and calculates ergodic trajectories via a kernel-ergodic horizon planner with gradient flow and footprint depletion, closing the loop between mapping and trajectory optimization. We thoroughly evaluate TRACE on the Replica dataset against the Next-Best-View (NBV) baselines, improving PSNR by 1.5 dB. Code: https://github.com/spikelab-jhu/trace-active-reconstruction.

</details>


### [374] [Residual-Based Adaptive Kalman Filtering for Legged Robot State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02316)
*Mihaela Popescu,Dennis Mronga,Shivesh Kumar,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于残差和创新的协方差自适应方法，用于四足机器人状态估计中的不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器（InEKF），在Unitree Go2上验证了自适应R矩阵可将步态精度提升25%，且无需足底力测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡尔曼滤波器的噪声参数调节困难且需要专家知识，固定的噪声参数无法适应不同的步态和环境，因此需要一种在线自适应策略来调整过程噪声协方差矩阵Q和测量噪声协方差矩阵R。

Method: 提出一种基于滤波残差和创新的协方差自适应方法，应用于融合IMU和腿运动学的InEKF中，在行走机器人状态估计中在线调整Q和R矩阵。

Result: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人的室内外数据集实验中，自适应R足以提升精度，与固定调参的InEKF相比，小跑步态精度提高了25%。所提出的基于残差的自适应方法在不需足底力测量和额外参数调优的情况下，性能与足底力方法相当。

Conclusion: 基于残差的自适应方法能够替代足底力测量方案，在实际应用中无需额外传感器即可实现更优的状态估计精度，具有实用性和推广价值。

Abstract: State estimation is a key component in model-based control of walking robots and, more broadly, applicable wherever hidden variables must be inferred. The Kalman filter is widely used to estimate floating-base position and velocity by fusing multiple sensing modalities. However, tuning noise parameters is challenging and typically requires expert knowledge. Moreover, fixed noise parameters are unsuitable for varying gaits and environments. We propose an online adaptation strategy for the process noise covariance matrix Q and the measurement noise covariance matrix R. Specifically, we introduce a filter residual and innovation-based covariance adaptation method for legged robot state estimation and evaluate it against a baseline approach relying on IMU and foot force measurements. The proposed adaptation is implemented within an Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (InEKF) fusing IMU and leg kinematics. Experiments on indoor and outdoor datasets with a Unitree Go2 quadruped show that adapting R is sufficient and improves accuracy by 25% for the trotting gait compared to the fixed-tuned InEKF. Finally, the proposed residual-based adaptation achieves comparable performance to the foot force approach, without requiring foot force measurements or additional parameter tuning.

</details>
